Always sent **after** the commit and best effort (`notifySafely`): a failing dispatch is logged (ids only) and never changes the HTTP result. Code: `src/lib/decision-notify.ts`, `src/lib/club-registration-notify.ts`.

| Trigger | Event key (`ref_id`) | Recipient and content |
|---|---|---|
| `POST /club-applications/:id/approve` (new member, re-admission, child application) | `application.approved` (application id) | The applicant's account (parent for a child; fallback: contact e-mail of the created member record). Welcome text with the member number |
| `POST /club-applications/:id/reject` | `application.rejected` | Same recipient; only the `applicantMessage` is quoted, never the internal note |
| Application intake (`POST /club-members/apply`, `POST /form-submissions/:id/submit`) | `application.received` (application id) | The applicant's account (parent for a child): receipt. The board notice `application.new` / `exit.new` is class `optional`, see the [Notification catalog](#notification-catalog) #33/#34 |
| `POST /club-exit-requests` (member) | `exit.received` (request id) | The member **and the member's guardians** (also when the child has an own address; each person and e-mail address once): receipt and earliest possible exit date. The same recipients apply to `exit.accepted`, `exit.exclusion` and `exit.rejected` |
| `POST /club-exit-requests/:id/approve`, board-recorded cancellation | `exit.accepted` | The member: "Der Austritt wird zum DD.MM.YYYY wirksam" (or "ist seit ... wirksam" for today/past) |
| exclusion (`POST /club-exit-requests`, approve) | `exit.exclusion` | The member, incl. the stated reason |
| `POST /club-exit-requests/:id/reject` | `exit.rejected` | The member (and the guardians of a child), incl. the board's note. **The `note` of approve/reject (stored as `decisionNote`) and the `reason` of an exclusion are member-facing by contract**: the member reads exactly these texts in `GET /club-exit-requests/mine`, so the mail discloses nothing new; internal remarks do not belong there (system notes such as "Ersetzt durch Ausschluss" are never mailed) |
| Club registration (`club-registrations`, `admin-club-registrations`) | `club_registration.received` / `.review-notify` / `.needs-info` / `.rejected` / `.approved`, `ref_id` `<registration id>#<transition ms>` (`submittedAt` / `reviewedAt` read from the committed row; no `?? new Date()` fallback, a missing stamp fails closed: no mail, error logged) | Applicant (with account id), resp. each `CLUB_REVIEW_NOTIFY_EMAIL` address (deduplicated per address); without that variable the platform admins get a bell entry (G9); platform context (`club_id` NULL), e-mail only, pre-rendered, wording byte-identical to the former ad-hoc mails (snapshot test) |
| `POST /club-members/:id/invite`, `/invite/resend` | `member_invite.created` (invite id) | The invited address; pre-rendered, sent inline, link wiped after sending |

Recipients of exit messages are resolved explicitly (the audience resolver skips people who left): account e-mail, else the member's contact e-mail, else the guardians of a child. "Austritt wird wirksam" is announced with the decision (including the date); a reminder on the effective day itself is part of B13. The auth mails (verification, password reset in `src/auth/auth.ts`) and the "account linked" notice to the inviter stay outside the dispatcher on purpose. `src/lib/club-registration-mail.ts` was removed after `src/lib/registration-mail-snapshot.test.ts` (snapshot `src/lib/__snapshots__/`, recorded from the old code) went green on the dispatcher output.

Tests: `registration-mail-snapshot.test.ts`, the decision assertions in `club-applications.test.ts`, `club-exit-requests.test.ts`, `club-member-invites.test.ts` and `club-registrations.test.ts`.

### Consents (Wave 10 B3)

One service, `recordConsent` (`src/lib/consent.ts`), writes every consent event: the consent routes, the guardian routes (B4) and the **form builder's consent block**. A `consent` field of a form carries an optional `purposeKey` (key of a club purpose); when the board accepts the application, every ticked field becomes a `granted` event of that purpose (`self`, or `guardian` for a child application, dated with the submission, `recorded_by` = the applicant). Such a field can never be `required` (`422` when saving the draft) and an unknown / inactive / text-less purpose is skipped and listed in the mapping report (`consent_purpose_unknown`, `consent_unavailable`), never an error for the acceptance. Nothing in the backend reads a consent to allow or deny access, membership or a feature (voluntariness, tested in `consents.test.ts`). **Form consent block rules (review M2):** the same minor/adult decision as the consent routes applies at acceptance: an under-age *self* applicant (`consent_requires_guardian`) and a *guardian* application for somebody who is no longer a minor (`consent_not_for_guardian`) record nothing; the event refers to the text version that was published when the applicant submitted, and if a newer one is published by acceptance time nothing is recorded (`consent_text_outdated`, the person is asked again). All of these are listed in `mapping.skipped` and never block the acceptance.

Tests: `src/routes/consents.test.ts` (texts and versions, age rule, guardians, paper proof, club scoping, voluntariness, photo revocation incl. old signed URL → 404), `src/routes/form-consent-block.test.ts`, the new routes in `rights-matrix.test.ts`.

### Transition to adulthood (Wave 10 B6)

`majority_sweep` (`src/lib/majority.ts`, hourly, club calendar day via `club_settings.timezone`) and its notice job `majority_notify` (payload: ids and dates only). The **read-time guard of `guardianScope` stays the authority** (a link grants nothing for a ward of 18, whatever the job did); the job makes the transition visible and tidy:

- **28 days ahead** (`MAJORITY_NOTICE_DAYS`, catches up after downtime): notice #56 to the member and the guardians; for a member **without account** additionally #57 to the `members:write` roles, who send the account invitation (the job never invites on its own: that needs a contact address the person gave).
- **On the birthday** (`29 February` counts on 1 March): `guardian_links.valid_until` of the ward's open links = the last day as a minor, one audit entry `club_member.majority` (club, number of links, birthday; no actor), notice #58 to the now adult member. Up to 30 days of missed days are caught up; older cases rest on the read-time guard alone. Idempotent (job keys carry member and birthday; only open links are touched).
- **Consents given for a minor:** the consent views (`GET /club-members/:id/consents`, `/guardian/wards/:wardId/consents`) carry `confirmation: { pending, due, overdue }` per purpose. `pending` = the latest event is a grant by a guardian or on paper (`givenBy` not `self`), the person was a minor when it was recorded and is not any more; `due` = birthday + 90 days. **Informational only:** nothing is revoked, blocked or hidden when it is overdue (a legal decision for K2). The person confirms by granting again (`POST /club-members/:id/consents/:purposeId` as themself writes a new `self` event even though the state is already "granted", a plain repeat of a `self` grant stays a no-op) or revokes.

Tests: `src/lib/majority.test.ts`.

### Guardian access, absences and field visibility (Wave 10 B4, B5, B12)

- **`guardianScope`** (`src/lib/guardian-scope.ts`): everything a guardian reaches is bound to a ward through `guardian_links`, evaluated at read time: confirmed (`confirmed_at`), inside `valid_from`/`valid_until`, guardian account and ward in the same club, ward younger than 18 (the link ends with the majority whatever `valid_until` says) and still in the club. The same helper (`liveWardIds`) now decides the guardian exception of the photo rules (`lib/member-photos.ts`), so an expired link no longer shows a photo. A guardian is a `guest` account with the club role `erziehungsberechtigt` and **no implicit `mitglied` role**; no new `ClubPermission` string exists for guardians. Existing Wave 7 contract unchanged: external guests still read the club-wide calendars without visibility grants and `gast` wifi/links, but no meetings and no member list.
- **Absences** (`src/lib/absences.ts`): data minimisation, no diagnosis field, 200-character note, never part of a notification (Notification catalog #52-#54). New permission `absences:read` (board: all members; `trainer` / `abteilungsleitung`: members of their own departments, resolved from their department-bound `club_roles`). Retention (B9) will delete reports 3 months after the end.
- **Waiting list of wards:** `ward_event_responses` count against the capacity of an event together with the account RSVPs (`POST /events/:id/rsvp` adds them to its count); when a seat becomes free the earliest waiting account moves up, only if nobody waits with an account the earliest waiting ward does.
- **Field visibility** (`src/lib/field-visibility.ts`, table `member_field_visibility`): default `nur_vorstand`, so nothing changed for existing data; the levels only widen what trainers / members see.

Tests: `src/routes/guardian.test.ts` (links and roles, `/my-wards`, IDOR matrix incl. expired / unconfirmed / adult ward / other club, events and answers with capacity, contacts, visibility incl. the photo, absences incl. department scope, overlay and the notification text), the new routes in `rights-matrix.test.ts`.

### Club privacy notice (Wave 10 B11)

`club_privacy_notices` holds the versioned privacy notice of a club (Art. 13/14 GDPR): immutable once published (SHA-256 of the text, a change is a new version), generated from a German template ("Muster, keine Rechtsberatung" until the legal review K2) filled with the club's founding data (`club_registrations` by `club_id`), `club_settings.privacy_controller` / `privacy_contact` and the **effective retention periods** ([Retention rules](#retention-rules-wave-10-b9)). **The notice is information, not a consent (Koppelungsverbot):** taking note of it is stamped, but nothing depends on it.

- **Applicants:** `GET /clubs/:slug/privacy-notice` (session only) gives the join page the current text. `POST /club-members/apply` and `POST /form-submissions/:id/submit` accept `privacyNoticeAcknowledged: true`; the application then carries `privacy_notice_version_id` + `privacy_notice_acknowledged_at` (also on the legacy request; shown in `GET /club-applications/:id` as `application.privacyNoticeVersionId` / `privacyNoticeAcknowledgedAt`). Without it, or when the club published no notice, the application is filed all the same with both columns `NULL`.
- **The standard application form has no consent any more.** Before Wave 10 B11 it carried a mandatory `privacy_consent`, which coupled the application to a consent. `ensureDefaultForms` replaces an **untouched** legacy standard form by the current one as a new version (the old version stays archived, submissions pinned to it stay valid; an open draft moves up by one; a form the board changed is left alone), and publishing a form with a required `consent` field is refused (`required_consent_not_allowed`). Optional, purpose-bound consent blocks (`purposeKey`) are unchanged. Migration `0022` still seeds the old definition (`LEGACY_DEFAULT_APPLICATION_FIELDS`); no migration was added.
- **Members without an account (Art. 14):** `POST /club-members/:id/privacy-notice-sent` notes that the notice was handed over (`club_members.privacy_notice_sent_at` + version; the first date of a version is kept), `GET` shows `{ sentAt, sentVersion, currentVersion, upToDate }`. Erasure clears both.

Tests: `src/routes/privacy-notice.test.ts`, `src/lib/form-defaults-upgrade.test.ts`, the changed form tests (`forms.test.ts`, `form-submissions.test.ts`, `form-acceptance.test.ts`, `form-schema.test.ts`).

### Processor agreement (Wave 10 B10)

The platform (processor) and each club (controller) conclude the AV-Vertrag in the product. `dpa_versions` are published by platform admins (title, Markdown body, `subprocessors [{ name, purpose, location }]`, `tom_annex`, `required_from`, SHA-256 over exactly that content; **immutable once published**, a change is a new version). The **owner** of a club accepts the current version (`dpa_acceptances`: version, account, name typed by the signer, "authorised" confirmation, time; unique per club and version, so accepting twice is a plain repeat). **No contract text ships with the code**: until an admin publishes one, `GET /dpa/current` answers `version: null` and nothing is required.

**The gate** (`src/middleware/dpa-gate.ts`, run by `clubGuard` right after it resolved the club) is **off unless `DPA_REQUIRED=true`** (release gate K4, text released). When on: a version becomes mandatory for a club on the later of its `required_from` date (default: the publication day) and the day the club was created; the club then has `DPA_GRACE_DAYS` (default 30) with a banner (`status.daysLeft`), afterwards every **writing** request (`POST`/`PATCH`/`PUT`/`DELETE`) of the club answers `403` with `error.code = "DPA_REQUIRED"` and `details: { code: "dpa_required", version, deadline }`. A brand-new club therefore sees the notice at once and has the full grace period; a new version of an old club starts its own grace period. **Never locked:** reading, all `/dpa/*` routes, and the rights of data subjects (`/data-export`, `/erasure/plan|execute`, `/erasure-request`, `/data-subject-requests`, revoking a consent), plus the **self-service writes of the person themself** (allowlist `EXEMPT` in `src/middleware/dpa-gate.ts`): `PATCH /club-members/me`, `PUT /club-members/:id/visibility`, `DELETE /club-members/me/photo`, `POST /club-exit-requests` and `/:id/withdraw`, `DELETE /absences/:id` and `/guardian/wards/:wardId/absences/:id`, `/notification-preferences` writes and `/push-tokens` writes. The error is a lock for the whole club, not for a person: members see the same `DPA_REQUIRED` and should be told to ask the owner.

Tests: `src/routes/dpa.test.ts` (draft/publish/immutability, owner-only acceptance, idempotence, outdated version, grace period, lock, open rights, new version, notification job, admin overview), the new routes in `rights-matrix.test.ts`.

### Data export and erasure (Wave 10 B7, B8)

**Privacy contributor registry** (`src/lib/privacy/registry.ts`, contributors in `contributors.ts`): every table with a personal reference to a club member is owned by exactly one contributor that knows how to export it (`exportSubject`, Art. 15) and how to erase it (`eraseSubject`, Art. 17: delete, anonymise, or keep with a reason). Built in: `member_core` (master data, departments, status history, custom fields, visibility, invitation), `consents` (kept as proof), `absences` (reports and ward RSVPs), `guardianship`, `forms_and_applications` (answers and copies erased via `eraseFormDataOfMember`), `exit_requests` (reasons removed, dates kept), `communication` (delivery addresses and texts scrubbed, preferences deleted), `account_data` (roles, availability, RSVPs, loans, damage reports, key holders), `assembly_data` (Wave 11: meeting invitations, attendance, proxies, voting roll, invitation deliveries, ballot rolls and open votes, candidacies, terms; the reference to the person is removed, counts and results stay). `src/lib/privacy/registry.test.ts` **fails the build** when a table with a foreign key to `club_members`, or a cascading foreign key to the account membership `member`, is neither covered by a contributor nor listed in `EXEMPT_TABLES` with a reason (`set null` references to `member` are actor references such as `decided_by` and are not data about the person). Later waves must add their contributor in the same change.

**Export** (`GET /club-members/me/data-export`, board `POST /club-members/:id/data-export`, guardian `GET /guardian/wards/:wardId/data-export`): `{ data: { generatedAt, club, clubMemberId, sections: { <contributor key>: {...} }, files: [{ kind, filename, mimeType, sizeBytes, uploadedAt }], note } }` or, with `format=html`, a readable HTML download (all values escaped). Never cached (`Cache-Control: private, no-store`), at most 5 MiB (`413` above), rate limited per account (`DATA_EXPORT_PER_HOUR`, `DATA_EXPORT_BOARD_PER_HOUR`), one `member_data.export` audit entry (id, format, size, no content). Files are listed from the media registry but never inlined or linked. Delivering an export closes the member's open `access` request. Audit entries about the person and legally required records of the club are not part of it.

**Erasure** (board, `privacy:manage`): `POST /club-members/:id/erasure/plan` is a dry run (the real code runs in a savepoint that is rolled back, so the plan cannot drift from the execution): `{ clubMemberId, displayName, blockers: [{ code, message }], steps: [{ key, deleted, anonymized, kept, keptReason? }], planDigest }`. `POST .../erasure/execute` with `{ planDigest, confirm: true }` re-checks and re-simulates inside the transaction and answers `409 plan_outdated` if the data changed since the plan; otherwise it anonymises and answers `{ data: { clubMemberId, erased: true, steps } }`. Not reversible. `execute` locks the member's form submissions and then the member row (`FOR UPDATE`) before it checks the blockers, so a legal hold or exit request committed in the meantime is seen. **What stays:** the `club_members` row as an anonymised record (`displayName` "Gelöschtes Mitglied", all names, birth date, address, phone, e-mail, emergency contact, member number, photo and exit reason removed; join date, category, status and department periods stay for statistics; the person is set to `ausgetreten` and never matched for a re-join), `consent_records` (proof; no master data in them), the fact that a form submission existed, `meeting_attendance` rows (**pseudonymised**: `member_id` becomes `NULL`, `present` and `has_voting_right` stay so quorum and votes remain verifiable; clients show "Gelöschtes Mitglied" for `memberId: null`), key history entries without the name. **Blockers** (`409` with `details.code` = first blocker and `details.blockers`): `legal_hold`, `exit_pending` (a pending cancellation/exclusion), `open_loans`, `board_role` (hand the role over first), `sole_owner` (organization owner without another owner). `PUT /club-members/:id/legal-hold { active, reason? }` sets or lifts the hold (`reason` mandatory when set; not shown to the member). Fulfilling an erasure closes the member's `erasure` request (or records one) and writes `member_erasure.execute` (counts only).

**Account deletion:** Better Auth's `deleteUser.beforeDelete` (`src/auth/auth.ts`) runs the same erasure for every club the account belongs to or belonged to (`club_members.last_user_id`). All clubs are checked first, a blocker stops the deletion with nothing changed (`400` with `code` `ERASURE_<BLOCKER>`, e.g. `ERASURE_SOLE_OWNER`, `ERASURE_OPEN_LOANS`; additionally `ERASURE_APPLICATION_PENDING` while an application of the account is still under review: withdraw it first). Decided applications stay with `submitted_by_user_id = NULL`, the account's abandoned drafts and their files are deleted at once. Platform admins delete accounts through `DELETE /admin/users/:id`, which runs the same process (Better Auth's `/admin/remove-user` is disabled). `meeting_attendance.member_id` is now nullable with `ON DELETE SET NULL` (migration `0044`), so removing a membership no longer cascades the attendance away.

**Requests with a deadline** (`data_subject_requests`, one month, Art. 12(3)): `POST /club-members/me/erasure-request` and `POST /guardian/wards/:wardId/erasure-request` file an erasure request (`201`, or `200` with the open one), `GET /club-members/me/data-requests` lists the own ones, the board uses `/data-subject-requests` (see API reference; `daysLeft`, `overdue`).

Tests: `src/lib/privacy/registry.test.ts`, `src/routes/member-privacy.test.ts` (export per role and club, escaped HTML, plan without side effects, digest, blockers, anonymisation, pseudonymised attendance, guardian role cleanup, deadlines, `deleteUser` hook), the new routes in `rights-matrix.test.ts`.

### General assembly data model (Wave 11)

Statute parameters live in `club_settings.assembly_rules` (see `/club-settings/assembly`); `lib/assembly-rules.ts` is the only reader. **Invitees, attendance, proxies, the voting roll, voter rolls and candidacies reference `club_members`** (members without an account take part), never a hard cascade on person deletion: the erasure contributor `assembly_data` removes the reference and keeps counts and results. `meetings.rules_snapshot` freezes the rules at invitation time so a resolution is judged by the rules that applied. Tables: `meeting_agenda_items` (structured agenda, `vote_kind` none/resolution/election, `topic` = key of `majorityByTopic`, `announced_in_invitation_id`), `meeting_invitations` (version, kind `einladung`/`nachtrag`, content snapshot + SHA-256, rules snapshot, period check, exception reason) with `meeting_invitation_deliveries` (per member and channel `email`/`inapp`/`post`, status `queued`/`sent`/`failed`/`bounced`/`manual_posted`, no address stored), `meeting_voting_roll` (frozen list of voting members with the basis), `meeting_proxies` (unique per grantor and meeting), `ballots` / `ballot_options` / `ballot_voters` (unique per ballot and member = no double vote, `cast_by` = proxy holder) / `ballot_votes_open`, `elections` / `election_candidates` / `club_role_terms`, `meeting_minutes` (versions, status `entwurf` → `zur_genehmigung` → `genehmigt` → `archiviert`) with `meeting_minutes_amendments`. **`ballot_votes_secret` (ADR option A)** has no member reference, no voter-roll reference, no timestamp and a random UUID primary key; its only columns are `ballot_id` and `option_id`. Limit: against an operator with database access a secret ballot cannot be proven anonymous. The minutes (`meeting_minutes`, `meeting_minutes_confirmations`, `meeting_minutes_amendments`) and `ballot_votes_secret` are registered in the `assembly_data` privacy contributor as tables without a club member reference: they are kept, accounts appear only as creator/confirmer and are set to NULL on erasure; see [Minutes](#minutes-wave-11-b9).

**Invitation (B3)** — `lib/invitation-period.ts` (`checkInvitationPeriod`, pure) counts calendar days in the club time zone: valid when the days from the sending day to the meeting day are at least `invitationMinDays + invitationDeliveryBufferDays` (assumption of the club's statute, not legal advice; a Nachtrag is checked from its own sending day). `lib/assembly-invitation.ts` loads the recipients, plans the channels, stores the snapshot (canonical JSON + SHA-256), hands the messages to the Wave 9 dispatcher (class `mandatory`) after the transaction committed and derives the proof status from `message_deliveries` when it is read — correctness does not depend on the scheduler. The proof stores no address, only channel, status and a machine-readable `errorCode`. Delivery rows are never edited by hand except `post` → `manual_posted`. Routes: `src/routes/meeting-assembly.ts` (mounted into `meetingRoutes`, same guards and read scoping). Tests: `src/lib/invitation-period.test.ts`, `src/routes/assembly-invitations.test.ts`.

**Voting right, attendance and quorum (B4)** — `lib/voting-right.ts` (`deriveVotingRight`, pure): eligible when the category flag `member_categories.voting_right`, a status from `votingStatuses`, the minimum age `votingMinAge` and entry / exit dates all agree on the reference day (the meeting day); while `votingStatuses` / `votingMinAge` are not stated nobody is derived (`rules_missing`). `lib/assembly-attendance.ts`: `loadEligibility` (frozen roll, else live), `freezeVotingRoll`, `overrideVotingRight`, `grantProxy` (statute rules), `evaluateQuorum` (pure) and `computeQuorum`. The quorum counts present voting members plus proxies and never reads the legacy `meeting_attendance.has_voting_right` flag, which only mirrors the roll. Tests: `src/lib/voting-right.test.ts`, `src/lib/assembly-attendance.test.ts`, `src/routes/assembly-voting-roll.test.ts`.

### Ballots (Wave 11 B5, B6)

`lib/ballots.ts` (lifecycle), `lib/ballot-evaluation.ts` (pure `evaluateBallot` / `evaluateChoices`), `lib/ballot-jobs.ts` (sweep, notifications), routes in `src/routes/ballots.ts`. A `ballots` row is a vote on a resolution, a Wahlgang (Wave 11 B7) or a circular resolution; status `draft` → `open` → `closed` → `counted` → `finalized`, or `cancelled`. Opening freezes the statute rules (`rules_snapshot`), the majority and the quorum on the ballot, so a later change of the statute never changes a running or counted ballot. **No double votes:** the vote is ONE transaction — a conditional `UPDATE ballot_voters SET has_voted = true … WHERE has_voted = false AND` the window (`opens_at <= clock_timestamp() < closes_at`, database clock) followed by the insert of the vote; the transaction holds a `SHARE` lock on the ballot, closing takes it exclusively and therefore waits for every vote accepted before the window ended. The unique voter row per ballot and member and the SQL guard make 50 parallel votes of one member exactly one vote (tested). **Secret ballots (ADR-008 option A):** only possible when the club set `secretBallotDigitalAllowed`; the vote goes to `ballot_votes_secret` (`ballot_id`, `option_id`, random UUID key: no member, no voter-roll link, no timestamp), the voter roll only says `has_voted`, nothing but the lifecycle (create, open, close, count, finalize, with aggregated counters) is audited, never a vote, and the individual votes are handed out shuffled with a CSPRNG. **Documented limit:** against an operator with database access a secret ballot cannot be proven anonymous (the order of commits and the voter roll's `cast_by` for proxies stay visible to such an operator); the UI must say so. Majority kinds (assumptions of the product, not legal advice): `einfach` = more yes than no, a tie is rejected; `absolut` = more than half of all votes cast incl. abstentions; `zwei_drittel` / `drei_viertel` = yes is at least 2/3 resp. 3/4 of yes + no; `einstimmig` = at least one yes and no one against; abstentions only count against with `abstainCountsAs = against`. In a session the quorum recorded at opening decides `nicht_beschlussfaehig`. Free options use the relative majority (or the threshold against the voters) and report a tie at the last seat as `gleichstand` instead of deciding it. In-session voters are the members of the frozen voting roll who are **checked in**; a proxy holder votes for an absent grantor (`cast_by_club_member_id`). Presence-hand votes stay possible as manual counts (`POST /meetings/:id/resolutions`).

**Circular resolutions (B6):** `mode = circular`, off until `circularAllowed`; the voters are `all_voting_members` (voting right today, members without an account are counted as eligible but cannot vote digitally, so unanimity fails honestly) or the `board` (accounts with a role of `BOARD_ROLE_TYPES`). `circularUnanimity` needs every eligible member to say yes, otherwise `circularMinParticipation` of the eligible members must take part and the majority applies. The window needs an end (`closesAt`); there is no check-in and no proxy. When it opens the voters get a mandatory message (job `ballot_notify`), 24 h before the end the members who have not voted get one reminder. The sweep (and any read) closes the window; the sweep counts and finalizes by itself. Finalizing creates a meeting of its own for the record (board circulars as `vorstandssitzung`, so they are read-scoped to the board) with the resolution and the decision record as `meeting_minutes` version 1 (`archiviert`, with the result checksum). Tests: `src/lib/ballot-evaluation.test.ts`, `src/routes/ballots.test.ts`, `src/routes/circular-ballots.test.ts`.

### Minutes (Wave 11 B9)

`lib/minutes.ts`, routes `src/routes/meeting-minutes.ts` (mounted in `/meetings`). `meeting_minutes` holds one row per version with the state machine `entwurf` → `zur_genehmigung` → `genehmigt` → `archiviert`; the text hash is SHA-256 of the UTF-8 text (also for the decision record of a circular resolution, which is version 1 `archiviert` with `approved_at`). Editing is only possible in `entwurf` (every edit drops the confirmations of chair and secretary, `meeting_minutes_confirmations`); `submit` needs both confirmations of the current text; `genehmigt` happens only through `approve` with an accepted resolution of a later meeting of the same type (`approved_in_meeting_id`, `approved_resolution_id`). A failed vote leads to `reopen` (the submitted version is superseded: `archiviert` without `approved_at`, never shown to members). Approved versions are immutable in the app AND in the database (trigger `meeting_minutes_guard`, migration 0051); corrections are `meeting_minutes_amendments` (Berichtigungsvermerk, append-only). `meetings.minutes` (free text) is legacy: migration 0048 moved it to version 1, the API neither reads nor writes it any more. Retention: minutes are never deleted by the sweep (see Retention rules); the creating account reference is set to NULL on account erasure.

### Assumptions and limits of the general assembly (Wave 11 review)

Nothing here is legal advice; each point is an assumption of the product the club has to confirm against its statute (J1 is still open).

- **Counting the invitation period (L1).** Calendar days in the club time zone, never 24 h blocks (daylight saving cannot shift the result). The sending day and the meeting day are not counted as extra days: the days from the sending day to the meeting day must be at least `invitationMinDays + invitationDeliveryBufferDays`. A Nachtrag is checked from its own sending day; moving the date later is judged from the first invitation (`PATCH /meetings/:id`).
- **Quorum only at opening (L2).** The quorum (`beschlussfaehig`) is checked when a ballot is opened and recorded on it. People leaving during the voting do not change a running ballot; there is no continuous quorum check. A new ballot checks again. The minutes freeze the numbers when they are submitted.
- **Secret ballots have a residual correlation limit (L3).** `ballot_votes_secret` carries no person, no timestamp and a random key, and the output is shuffled; but `ballot_voters.has_voted` and `cast_by` (proxy holder) exist next to it. Anybody with database access (operator, backup) can narrow votes down, in small groups, with few voters or when a ballot has very few votes per option, up to certainty (for example the last voter, or unanimous results). It is not a proof of anonymity against an operator; the UI says so and the club decides whether this is acceptable under its statute.
- **Statute changes (M8).** The statute parameters are frozen per invitation (`meeting_invitations.rules_snapshot`, copied to `meetings.rules_snapshot`) and per opened ballot (`ballots.rules_snapshot`), so changing the parameters later does not change a called assembly or a running or counted ballot. Before the first invitation the live parameters apply; a club that changes its statute should send the invitation (or a Nachtrag) afterwards. Moving the date re-checks the period with the parameters frozen at the first invitation.

### Elections (Wave 11 B7)

`lib/elections.ts`, routes `src/routes/elections.ts`. An election is the office to fill (`role_type`, department, `seats`, `term_months`, election lead who must not stand); each Wahlgang is a `ballots` row of kind `election` (options = candidates + abstention, `max_choices` = open seats, optional own majority kind, optional secret), so voting, window, quorum check, counting and the checksum are exactly those of [Ballots](#ballots-wave-11-b5-b6). `evaluateChoices` (pure) qualifies candidates against the majority (relative majority for `einfach`, thresholds against the voters otherwise) and reports a tie at the last seat as `gleichstand`; `POST /elections/:id/decide` turns it into elected candidates and demands the documented lot. Acceptance feeds the role model: `club_roles` gets the role with `term_ends_at`, `club_role_terms` the history entry; the previous holders end only when the whole new group is complete and active, so the club is never left without an officer while a successor still waits for an account. Elected persons without an account are `pending_account`; `grantPendingElectionRoles` runs inside the transactions that link an account (`lib/member-invites.ts`, both invitation-accept paths) and grants the role then. Audited: `election.create|update|candidate|decide|accept|role_grant|role_end` (ids and counts; the lot note stays in `elections.decision`). Open: the election lead has no extra rights (the board acts with `assembly:manage`); the term-expiry watch and reminders are Wave 11 B8. Test: `src/routes/elections.test.ts`.

#### Verifying the Wave 7 migrations (B12)

**Automated count check.** `src/db/migration-counts.test.ts` builds a scratch database migrated to `0013` (state before Wave 7), seeds a realistic dataset via SQL (3 clubs, 300 accounts with owner/admin/member/guest roles, 225 `club_memberships` with and without number/birth date, past/boundary/future `left_at`, empty and 800-character names, duplicate names and member numbers, umlaut and free-text categories, 40 guardian links incl. cross-club, departments/roles/applications, key holders, loans, meeting invitees/attendance, availability, event attendees, calendar visibility, photo values of every kind) and applies `0014`-`0021`. It asserts: row counts of all 27 preserved tables before == after; content fingerprints of every untouched column; exactly one `club_members` row per `member` (no duplicates, unique `member_id`, same club); master data, member numbers, join dates and the `left_at` -> `status` mapping (`<= today` = `ausgetreten`, future stays `aktiv`); categories folded and created once; `guardian_links` repointed to `club_members.id` with none dangling; legacy `visible_to_guests`/`meetings.type`/photo-key/wifi-security mappings; one `location_key_history` entry per key holder; idempotency (re-running the 0014 backfill, the 0015 fold, 0020 and the migrator changes nothing); strictly increasing journal and unbroken snapshot chain. No migration bug was found by it.

**On a copy of production.** Never run this against the live database. Restore a dump into a separate database, then:

```bash
export DATABASE_URL=postgres://user:pass@host:5432/copy_of_prod
npm run db:verify-counts > before.txt     # pre-Wave-7 copy: only row counts (club_members does not exist yet)
npm run db:migrate:runtime                # applies 0014-0021
npm run db:verify-counts > after.txt      # row counts + integrity checks
diff before.txt after.txt                 # only the new tables and the Wave 7 checks may appear; every `rows.<table>` of a pre-existing table must be identical
```

The script runs in a `READ ONLY` transaction and prints only table names, counts and flags (`OK`/`FAIL`), never names, e-mail addresses or member numbers, so the output can be pasted into a ticket. Checks (all must be `OK`, exit code 0): every `member` has its `club_members` row, no duplicate `member_id`, linked rows match the account's club, no `ausgetreten` without `left_at`, every linked member has a category and `last_user_id`, no umlaut/unnormalised category keys and all five default categories per club, no dangling `guardian_links` ward and the legacy `ward_member_id` column gone, no foreign/external/traversal `photo_key`, every key holder covered by a history entry, no `open` wifi with a password, `meetings.type` within the controlled set, no unvalidated constraint. Informational lines (not failures): duplicate member-number groups (legacy data), `aktiv` members with a future `left_at`, cross-club guardian links. The script needs `tsx`, i.e. host mode (a checkout), not the production container.

## Notification catalog

Complete inventory of everything MyVerein sends or schedules by itself, read from the code (Wave 9, branch `feature/communication`). One row per trigger. German long form with gaps and silent states: vault `Technical Reference - Benachrichtigungs-Katalog`. **Keep this table in sync**: every new `notify(...)`, `notifySafely(...)`, `sendEmail(...)` or `enqueue(...)` that reaches a person needs a row in the same commit (find them with `grep -rnE "notify\(|notifySafely|notifyInBackground|sendEmail\(|REMINDER_JOB_TYPE" src --include=*.ts`).

Legend. **M** = `mandatory` (ignores preferences, no unsubscribe link, cannot be switched off), **O(cat)** = `optional` in category `announcements` / `events` / `reminders` / `board` (e-mail and push can be switched off per account in `/settings`, globally or per club, in-app copy is always written, unsubscribe link in every mail). Channels: **in-app** = `message_deliveries` row (account holders only; shown in the club inbox), **mail**, **push**. "Delivery dedupe" = unique index `message_deliveries_system_event_uidx` on (event key, `ref_id`, channel, member or user) resp. the announcement indexes; mails are additionally keyed `notify.email:<deliveryId>`. Every mail honours `club_settings` sender name, reply-to and signature (club context only) and is written in `club_settings.mail_language` (`de` default, `en`). Recipients are always resolved through the audience resolver unless stated: left, dormant (`ruhend`) and deceased members never receive anything, a shared address gets one mail, account-less members are reachable by their contact e-mail only (no in-app, no push), `includeGuardians` adds the guardians of matched wards.

### A. Announcements (manual, club context)

| # | Trigger (exact state or transition) | Key / job | Recipients | Class, channels | Dedupe | Suppressed when, setting | Text | Authored at, seen at |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `POST /club-announcements/:id/send` on a `draft`/`scheduled` announcement without `publishAt` (claims `sending`, then `sent`) | `announcement` | Audience snapshot at send time (all, departments, role types, categories, members; guardians unless switched off) | O(announcements); channels chosen by the author (default in-app; mail, push optional; urgent + push adds mail) | Announcement unique indexes (announcement, member or user, channel); status claim `FOR UPDATE`; resend after crash is a no-op for existing rows | Opt-out of mail/push (checked at write time and again at send time); no address → `skipped_no_address`; withdrawn/cancelled/expired before the mail job runs → `skipped`; limits refuse the send, announcement stays draft (`ANNOUNCEMENT_MAX_PER_CLUB_DAY` 20, `..._PER_AUTHOR_HOUR` 10, `..._URGENT_PER_DAY` 3, `..._MAX_RECIPIENTS` 1000, confirmation from `ANNOUNCEMENT_CONFIRM_THRESHOLD` 50, duplicate window 10 min); department authors: own departments only, never urgent | Free text of the author; urgent: subject `[Dringend] ...` + banner; template `announcement`/`urgent` | Website `/mitteilungen` tab "Verfassen" (`announcements:write`, or `announcements:write_department`); not in the app. Seen: website `/mitteilungen` "Schwarzes Brett" + badge, app tab Mitteilungen + badge, mail. Push: backend ready, app has no registration yet |
| 2 | `POST /club-announcements/:id/send` with `publishAt` → status `scheduled` → job at `publishAt` | `announcement.publish` → same send path as #1 | as #1 (snapshot at publish time) | as #1 | Job key `announcement.publish:<id>:<publishAt ms>` + as #1 | Stale job (rescheduled, cancelled, edited, no longer `scheduled`) is a no-op; author lost the right to address the audience → announcement `cancelled` + audit `send_denied`; limit or audience cap hit at run time → back to `draft`, nothing sent | as #1 | Website "Verfassen" (schedule). Seen as #1 |
| 3 | `POST /club-announcements/:id/resend-failed` (status `sent`, not expired) | `notify.email` | Recipients whose mail delivery is `failed` | as #1 (mail only; failed push is not resent) | Job key `notify.email:<deliveryId>:retry:<ms>` | Opted out since → `skipped_optout`; withdrawn/expired → refused (409) | Same snapshot as the original | Website "Verlauf" tab, button "Fehlgeschlagene erneut senden". Seen: mail |

Seen as #4 |
| 60 | `POST /meetings/:id/invitations` sends the formal invitation (or a Nachtrag) to a general assembly (Wave 11 B3) | `meeting.invitation` (direct `notify`, after the invitation was stored) | Every member who is not left and not just interested: accounts (in-app, push, mail to the account address) and members without account (mail to the contact address); no address → post list | **Mandatory**, in-app + mail + push (ignores notification preferences: a formal invitation must not be switched off) | Delivery `ref_id` = invitation id, one row per person and channel; a shared address is mailed once | Statute parameters incomplete, agenda missing, period violated without exception reason, meeting not a planned `mitgliederversammlung` (all `422`, nothing is sent) | Title "Einladung: <title>" / "Nachtrag zur Einladung: <title>", time, place, optional note, numbered agenda | Authored at send time; seen when the member opens it; the proof is `meeting_invitation_deliveries` |

| 62 | The sweep finds an open circular resolution whose window ends within 24 h (job `ballot_notify` kind `reminder`, once per ballot) (Wave 11 B6) | `ballot.reminder` (ballot id) | Eligible voters who have **not voted yet** | Optional (category `reminders`), in-app + mail + push | as #61 | Ballot no longer open | "Erinnerung: Abstimmung: <title>" | Authored by the sweep |

### C. Reminders (automatic, worker)

Recurring job `reminder-sweep` (every 15 min) → one `notify_reminder` job per due reminder, job key `reminder:<kind>:<ref>:<date>` (exactly once across sweeps and instances); the job calls the dispatcher with `ref_id` `<ref>#<date>`, class O(reminders), channels in-app + mail, plus **push** for the personal, time-critical kinds #8, #9, #10, #12 (`PUSH_REMINDER_KINDS`; the leadership digests and expiry notices stay in-app + mail). "Day" is the calendar day in `club_settings.timezone`; date-based kinds go out from `REMINDER_DIGEST_HOUR` (default 8) club time. All are authored automatically; the club switches them in website `/verein` → Einstellungen → "Erinnerungen und Automatik" (`members:write`).

| # | Trigger | Key / job | Recipients | Suppressed when, setting | Text |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 8 | Event not cancelled, `startsAt` in (now, now + lead] | `reminder.event`, key `...:event:<eventId>:<local start date>` | Attendees `angemeldet` (accounts) and their guardians | `reminder_event_enabled`, `reminder_event_lead_hours` (24); waitlist, no RSVP: nothing; moving the event to another day can remind again (new date in the key) | `Erinnerung: <title>`, "Du hast ... zugesagt", when |
| 9 | Open loan with `dueAt`, from `reminder_loan_lead_days` (1) before the due day until the due day | `reminder.loan_due`, key `...:loan_due:<loanId>:<due date>` | The borrower (needs a `club_members` row) and their guardians | `reminder_loan_enabled`, `reminder_loan_lead_days`; returned or no due date | `Rückgabe fällig: <item>` |
| 10 | Open loan overdue (`isLoanOverdue`) on day 1, 4 and 7 after the due day (after downtime only the latest missed step), then never again | `reminder.loan_overdue`, key `...:<loanId>:<due date + step>` | The borrower and their guardians | `reminder_loan_enabled` | `Rückgabe überfällig: <item>` |
| 11 | Items with a maintenance interval whose due date is within `reminder_maintenance_lead_days` (7) or overdue; one digest per club and day | `reminder.maintenance`, key `...:maintenance:<clubId>:<date>` | Everyone with `inventory:write` (by role) | `reminder_maintenance_enabled`, `..._lead_days`; nothing due → nothing | `Wartung fällig: n Gegenstände`, list (max 15) |
| 12 | Meeting `geplant` with a date, invitees with response `ausstehend`, from `reminder_rsvp_lead_days` (3) days before the meeting day | `reminder.rsvp`, key `...:rsvp:<meetingId>:<meeting date>` | Pending invitees only, and their guardians | `reminder_rsvp_enabled`, `reminder_rsvp_lead_days`; already answered → not included | `Bitte antworten: <title>` |
| 37 | Open member invitation (not accepted, member still without account and not left) that expires within `reminder_invitation_lead_days` (3) calendar days, from the digest hour | `reminder.invite_expiring`, key `...:invite_expiring:<inviteId>:<expiry date>` | Roles with `members:write` (everybody who may re-send it), no guardians | `reminder_invitation_enabled`, `reminder_invitation_lead_days`; accepted, member left or linked meanwhile; a re-sent invitation is a new row and gets its own reminder | `Einladung läuft ab: <member>`, expiry date, where to re-send; never the invited address |
| 38 | The same invitation ran out unaccepted, up to 7 days after the expiry day (older ones stay silent) | `reminder.invite_expired`, key `...:invite_expired:<inviteId>:<expiry date>` | as #37 | as #37 | `Einladung abgelaufen: <member>` |
| 39 | Credential (custom field type `credential`) with `validUntil` within `reminder_credential_lead_days` (30), valid through `validUntil`, from the digest hour | `reminder.credential_expiring`, key `...:credential_expiring:<valueId>:<validUntil>` | The holder; their guardians only where a guardian may read the field (`canGuardianRead`) | `reminder_credential_enabled`, `reminder_credential_lead_days`; **sensitive** and archived fields never remind; member left; renewing the credential (new date) starts a new cycle | `Qualifikation läuft ab: <field>`, person, date |
| 40 | The credential ran out, up to 7 days after `validUntil` | `reminder.credential_expired`, key `...:credential_expired:<valueId>:<validUntil>` | as #39 | as #39 | `Qualifikation abgelaufen: <field>` |

Seen at: inbox (website `/mitteilungen`, app tab Mitteilungen), mail with unsubscribe link (category `reminders`).

Guardians: #8, #9, #10 and #12 reach the guardians of the person concerned (`includeGuardians`), so their texts are neutral ("liegt eine Zusage vor"), not "du"; the maintenance digest (#11) does not. Events, loans and RSVPs are bound to accounts (`member.id`), so a child **without** an account cannot be an attendee, borrower or invitee and has no reminder of its own; the guardians are reached through the child's account where one exists, and through the department audience of a cancellation (#5) and the exit decisions (#17-#20) for account-less children.

### E. Decisions about a person's own request (mandatory, after commit, best effort via `notifySafely`)

All: class **M**, in-app (if account) + mail, no preference applies, no unsubscribe link. #15, #16, #18, #19, #20 additionally go out as **push** (never alone; the receipts #17 and #32 do not): the payload is the generic inbox deep link (`type: message`, no club id, no request id) and a user who switched push off in every category gets no push for decisions either (in-app and mail stay). A failing dispatch is logged (ids only) and never changes the HTTP result. In-app copies sit in the club inbox, which needs club access (`clubGuard`): a rejected applicant and a member whose exit is already effective cannot open it, so **mail is the only reliable channel** there.

| 16 | `POST /club-applications/:id/reject` | `application.rejected` | as #15 | as #15 | `... wurde abgelehnt`; quotes only the applicant message, never the internal note |

| 18 | Board approves a request or books a cancellation (`.../approve`, board path of `POST`) | `exit.accepted` | as #17 | Delivery unique | `Dein Austritt bei <club> wurde angenommen`, "wird zum DD.MM.YYYY wirksam" or "ist seit ... wirksam", note |
| 19 | Board excludes a member (exclusion path) | `exit.exclusion` | as #17 | Delivery unique | `Ausschluss aus <club>`, reason |
| 20 | `POST /club-exit-requests/:id/reject` | `exit.rejected` | as #17 | Delivery unique | `Deine Austrittsanfrage bei <club> wurde abgelehnt`, board note, "Du bleibst Mitglied" |
| 32 | Application intake (same three entry points as #33) | `application.received` (application id) | The applicant's account (the parent for a child application) | Delivery unique | `Dein Aufnahmeantrag bei <club> ist eingegangen`, "der Vorstand meldet sich"; class **M** because the applicant has no other proof of receipt |

### H. Loan and key handovers (author action, switch `club_settings.notify_handover`)

`src/lib/handover-notify.ts`, called after the commit, best effort, inline (no job). Class **O(reminders)**, in-app + mail, recipients: the borrower / key holder **and the guardians of a child**; the texts name the person, so they read the same for everybody. Common suppression: `notify_handover = false`, mail opt-out of the recipient (in-app stays), recipient without a `club_members` row. **Self-service rule:** a loan is always borrowed by the caller (#41 is therefore the written proof and always sent); a return or an extension, and every key action, is **not** sent when the actor is the affected person. The leadership digests (#11 maintenance, #14 exit effective) and the due/overdue reminders (#9, #10) are unchanged.

| # | Trigger | Key (`ref_id`) | Suppressed when, setting | Text |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 41 | `POST /inventory-items/:id/loans` (borrowed) | `loan.issued` (loan id) | as above | `Ausleihe: <item>`, "Rückgabe bis: DD.MM.YYYY" or "kein Rückgabedatum" |
| 42 | `PATCH /inventory-items/:id/loans/:loanId` without `dueAt` by somebody else (`inventory:write`) | `loan.returned` (loan id) | the borrower returns it themself | `Rückgabe bestätigt: <item>` |
| 43 | `PATCH /inventory-items/:id/loans/:loanId` with `{ dueAt }` (extension, `inventory:write`) | `loan.extended` (`<loanId>#<new due ms>`: every new date is a new message, the same date is refused with `422`) | the borrower is the actor | `Rückgabe verlängert: <item>`, new and previous date |
| 44 | `POST /locations/:id/key-holders` (key handed out) | `key.issued` (key history entry id) | the holder is the actor (e.g. the board gives itself a key); a duplicate hand-out is `409` and sends nothing | `Schlüsselausgabe: <location>`, key label; the internal note is never quoted |
| 45 | `DELETE /locations/:id/key-holders/:memberId` (key taken back) | `key.returned` (key history entry id) | the holder is the actor | `Schlüsselrückgabe: <location>`, key label |

**54 triggers.** Delivery infrastructure (not triggers, send what the rows above queued): `notify.email` (one mail per delivery, retry with backoff, throttle `MAIL_SEND_RATE`), `notify.push` and `notify.push_receipts` (Expo, payload without text or personal data), recurring `notify.reap_dead_deliveries` (every 10 min: `queued`/`sending` rows whose job is `dead` become `failed`). Jobs that run but send nothing: `form_cleanup_sweep`, `retention_sweep` (tasks `jobs-retention`, `inbox-retention`, `delivery-retention`, `rate-limit-events`).

### I. Absence reports (author action, Wave 10 B5)

`src/lib/absences.ts`, called after the commit, best effort (`notifySafely`). Class **O(events)**, in-app + push (the push text is the generic key text), recipients: the **trainers and department leads** (`club_roles` `trainer` / `abteilungsleitung` of the member's open departments, accounts only, never the actor and never the person themselves). A member without a department tells nobody (the board reads the reports in `GET /absences/club`). **No health data in any text:** the title and body name the person and the period only, never the kind (`krank` / `abwesend` read the same), the note or the word "sick"; the note stays in the database and in the `absences:read` views (tested).

| # | Trigger | Key (`ref_id`) | Suppressed when, setting | Text |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 52 | `POST /absences` (member) or `POST /guardian/wards/:wardId/absences` (guardian) | `absence.reported` (absence id) | actor is the only trainer; mail/push opt-out per account (in-app stays) | `Neue Abwesenheitsmeldung`: "<name> hat sich für <period> abgemeldet." |
| 53 | `PATCH /absences/:id` or `PATCH /guardian/wards/:wardId/absences/:id` | `absence.changed` (`<id>#<updatedAt ms>`: every change is a new message) | as #52 | `Abwesenheitsmeldung geändert`: new period |
| 54 | `DELETE /absences/:id` or the guardian route (withdrawn; a repeat sends nothing) | `absence.withdrawn` (absence id) | as #52 | `Abwesenheitsmeldung zurückgezogen` |

### States that deliberately send nothing

Event created; event edit that changes only title, description, category or capacity; change of an already cancelled event; delete of an event without RSVP rows; `notify:false` (audited); RSVP, cancel of an RSVP (audit only; the person who moves up from the waitlist is told, see #36); meeting created, answer given, invitee removed, deleting a meeting that was held, cancelled or has no invitees; announcement draft, cancelled, withdrawn (no retraction notice, the entry just disappears from the inbox); member status changes, role and guardian-link changes, profile edits, imports; loans and keys: only the actor's own return/extension and key action, or a club with `notify_handover` off (otherwise see H); direct exits (booked by the board) at the effective day; dormant, left and deceased members as audience; empty audience; mandatory classes never get an unsubscribe link; account-less members get no in-app and no push.

### Gaps (states that arguably should notify but do not)

6. **Deadlines (remainder):** the reserved class `invitation_deadlines` (Wave 11 "invitation with a deadline") still sends nothing; reminders for member invitation links (#37/#38) and expiring credentials (#39/#40) exist since G6.
8. **Account mails (remainder):** verification (#27) and password reset (#28) stay direct Better Auth mails **on purpose**: they must work before an account has a verified address (the dispatcher's mandatory class would otherwise depend on contact data that is not proven yet), so they have no retry and no proof. There is still no notice for a deleted account.
11. **Author UI:** closed in the website and the app (notify switches, cancel with reason and audience preview via `GET /events/:id/cancel-preview`, Treffen switch). Still missing in both clients: per-club preferences UI, mail-language switch and the `notify_age_transition` switch.
12. **Language (remainder):** system texts of club messages exist in German and English (`club_settings.mail_language`), preferences can be set per club. Still German only: the club registration mails (no club exists yet), the unsubscribe confirmation pages and the texts of account-less recipients are the club's language, not their own (no language on the person).

## API reference

| Route | Method | Auth | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/api/auth/*` | GET, POST | varies | Better Auth's own routes: sign-up, sign-in, session, 2FA, organization, admin. See `/api/auth/reference` for the generated OpenAPI docs. |
| `/health` | GET | none | Liveness — never touches the database, can't flap on a Postgres blip |
| `/ready` | GET | none | Readiness — pings Postgres, returns 503 if unreachable; includes `worker` heartbeat when the process runs the job worker |
| `/my-clubs` | GET | session | Clubs the caller already belongs to (`clubId`/`clubName`/`memberId`/`orgRole` per row) — how a client picks a `clubId` for every other club-scoped route below. **Clubs the person has left are not listed** (effective status `ausgetreten`/`ausgeschlossen`/`verstorben`, see [Exit workflow](#exit-workflow)). No "browse public clubs" directory in Wave 1 |
| `/club-members/apply` | POST | session | Aufnahmeantrag — writes ONLY a `pending` row into `club_applications` (no `member`/`club_members` row until a board member approves, see `/club-applications`). Body `{ clubId or clubSlug, category?, birthDate? }` for old clients (`409 form_required` only for a club whose published form is an old version with a mandatory consent, see below; `privacyNoticeAcknowledged?: boolean` stamps the privacy notice, see [Club privacy notice](#club-privacy-notice-wave-10-b11)), or — with the form builder — `{ clubSlug|clubId, submissionId?, answers?, formVersionId?, onBehalfOf?: "self" \| "guardian" }` (`guardian` = a parent applies for a child, see [Child applications](#child-applications-wave-8-b8); needs `answers` or `submissionId`, never the legacy shape → `422`; [Application acceptance](#application-acceptance-and-mapping-wave-8-b6); `FORM_SUBMISSIONS_PER_HOUR` per user for every shape). `201 { data: { application } }` (legacy) / `201 { data: { application, submission: { id, status, formVersionId, onBehalfOf }, warnings } }` (`200` if the submission was filed before; `warnings` only for child applications: `child_is_adult`, `birth_date_missing`). `422` with `details.fields` for invalid answers, `409 FORM_VERSION_OUTDATED` for a stale form version (clients: re-render, keep answers by key). 404 for an unknown slug/club. **`409` with distinguishable codes** (nested format `{ error: { code, message, details } }`): `ALREADY_MEMBER` (`details: { clubId, clubMemberId }`) if the caller is a member, `APPLICATION_PENDING` (`details: { clubId, applicationId, submittedAt }` — the caller's own application; also for a parallel duplicate via the partial unique index) if one is open; a blocked re-join keeps `code: "CONFLICT"` with `details.code = "member_rejoin_blocked"`. Re-applying after a `rejected` application is allowed. **A former member** (effective status `ausgetreten`, account membership row still present) **may re-apply**; an `ausgeschlossen`/`verstorben` person gets `409` with `details.code = "member_rejoin_blocked"` until the board reopens them. Audit `club_member.apply` (`subjectId` = application id) |
| `/club-members` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Member list **with and without account**, searchable and paged (B9). Query (all optional): `q` (name; member number and e-mail — contact + account — only count for callers with `members:read_sensitive`, otherwise ignored, so the search is no oracle), `status` (comma list of the 7 statuses, matched against the **effective** status), `category` (comma list of category keys), `departmentId` (current assignment), `hasAccount=true\|false`, `ageMin`/`ageMax` (whole years from `birth_date`; **403 without `members:read_sensitive`**), `sort=name\|joinedAt\|memberNumber` (default `name`; `memberNumber` needs `members:read_sensitive`, else 403) with `order=asc\|desc`, `page` (default 1), `pageSize` (default **50**, max 100). Invalid values → `422`. Sorting is stable (secondary key `id`); `sort=memberNumber` is **natural** (text prefix, then the numeric suffix without leading zeros: `X-2` < `X-0003` < `X-10`; members without number last). **Scoping:** external `guest` accounts get `403` (no member list for guardians; Wave 10 brings their own routes); callers with neither `members:write` nor `members:read_sensitive` only get members with an effective status `probe`/`aktiv`/`ruhend` plus their own row — former members and interessenten are hidden server-side and `status` filters only narrow that set (`total` matches). Response `{ data: [member shape], page, pageSize, total }` — **changed frame:** earlier versions returned every member in `data`; without query parameters you now get the first 50 by name, so clients must read `total` and page. E-mail, contact e-mail, member number, birth date, address, phone, emergency contact and `suggestedCategory` are included only for the caller's own row or with `members:read_sensitive`. Departments/roles/categories are batch-loaded for the page (no N+1) |
| `/club-members/invites/preview` | POST (GET deprecated) | session (no club membership needed) | **Preferred: `POST` with body `{ token }`** (the token stays out of URLs and access logs). The `GET ?token=` variant answers identically but is **deprecated** (adds a `Deprecation: true` header; it will be removed once the clients have moved) → `200 { data: { clubId, clubName, memberName (masked: "Max M."), invitedEmail (masked: "m***@example.com"), emailMatches, expiresAt } }`. `404` for unknown, expired or used tokens (indistinguishable), `422` for a malformed token. Rate limit 20/min per user (shared by preview and accept). See [Account linking](#account-linking) |
| `/club-members/invites/accept` | POST | session (no club membership needed) | Body `{ token }` → `200 { data: { clubId, clubName, clubMemberId, memberId, merged } }`: links the invited member to the signed-in account; if the account already is a member of the club with an untouched own record, that record is **merged** into the invited one (`merged: true`, `clubMemberId` = the kept invited row). `404` invalid/expired/used token, `403` if the (verified) account e-mail differs from the invited address (case-insensitive; checked before anything about merging is revealed), `409 details.code = "merge_conflict"` (+ `details.conflicts`: kinds only) if the account's own record holds data that cannot be merged automatically, `409` for a blocked re-join or a second invitation of an account that is already linked. Single use, race safe; the inviting board member gets a notification mail. See [Account linking](#account-linking) |
| `/club-members/import-template` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | CSV template (`text/csv`, UTF-8 BOM, `;`) with the header row and one example line; ready for the importer (B10) |
| `/club-members/import` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`, checked **before** the body is validated; 10 requests/min per user, `MEMBER_CSV_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE`) | CSV import with dry run (B10, see [Member CSV import/export](#member-csv)). Body `{ csv, dryRun? (default **true**), confirmToken?, mapping?, defaultCategoryKey? }`. Dry run `200` → `{ data: { dryRun: true, committable, delimiter, columns: [{ header, field }], summary: { rows, ok, duplicates, errors }, rows: [{ line, status: 'ok'\|'duplicate'\|'error', issues: [{ field, code, message }], duplicates: [{ reason: 'name_birthdate'\|'email', clubMemberId, line }] }], confirmToken, confirmTokenExpiresAt } }`; writes nothing. Commit (`dryRun: false` + `confirmToken` of a dry run of exactly this file) `201` → `{ data: { dryRun: false, created, skippedDuplicates, summary, rows } }`. File-level problems and a missing/foreign token are `422` with `details.code` (`csv_too_large`, `csv_too_many_rows`, `csv_empty`, `csv_malformed`, `csv_missing_name_column`, `import_not_confirmed`, `import_has_errors`, `import_nothing_to_do`); a token that was already used is `409 import_already_applied`. The commit response also carries `skippedSinceDryRun` (duplicates that appeared since the dry run) |
| `/club-members/export` (alias `/club-members/export.csv`) | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write` or `members:read_sensitive`; sensitive columns need `members:read_sensitive`; 10/min per user) | CSV download for Excel DE (`text/csv; charset=utf-8`, BOM, `;`, CRLF, `Content-Disposition: attachment`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`). Query: `columns=` comma list in the wanted order (default: all columns for `members:read_sensitive`, otherwise the non-sensitive ones), `status=`, `category=` (keys), `departmentId=`, `includeFormer=true` (without `status`, former members are left out). **Custom fields** are opt-in columns `cf:<key>` in `columns=` (header = the field label; never in the default selection; only live definitions the exporter may read; an unknown, archived or unreadable key all answer the same `422 unknown_column`, so existence does not leak); the audit entry lists the field ids. Unknown column `422` (`details.allowed`), a sensitive column without `members:read_sensitive` `403`. Cells starting with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, TAB or CR are prefixed with `'` (CSV/formula injection). Writes an `audit_log` entry `club_member.export` (actor, columns, filters, row count; no personal data) |
| `/club-members/categories` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Non-archived member categories for **every** member (also needed by the apply/profile screens): `[{ id, key, name, votingRight, minAge, maxAge, autoTransitionToKey, sortOrder, archived, archivedAt, memberCount }]`, ordered by `sortOrder`, `name`. `memberCount` = members that still belong to the club (not left). `?includeArchived=true` needs `members:write` (403). Default categories are created on first access |
| `/club-members/categories` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Body `{ name (required), key?, votingRight? (default true), applicantSelectable? (default false), minAge?, maxAge?, autoTransitionToKey?, sortOrder? }`. `key` (a-z, 0-9, `_`, `-`; derived from the name if omitted, e.g. "Ehren Jugend" → `ehren-jugend`) is the stable identifier. `201` with the category; `409` for an existing key (archived categories keep theirs), `422` for `minAge > maxAge`, an `autoTransitionToKey` without `maxAge`, pointing to itself, to an unknown/archived category or closing a **cycle**. `sortOrder` defaults to last. Audit `member_category.create` |
| `/club-members/categories/:id` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Change `name`, `votingRight`, **`applicantSelectable`** (whether an applicant may choose the category in the application form; only these are offered and accepted there, others are `422 not_an_option`), `minAge`, `maxAge`, `autoTransitionToKey` (null clears), `sortOrder`. **`key` is immutable** (`422` if sent). Same validation as POST on the resulting state. `404` for unknown/foreign ids. Audit `member_category.update` |
| `/club-members/categories/:id` | DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | **Archives** the category (history and references stay valid). If members that still belong to the club use it, `?moveTo=<categoryId>` is mandatory (`409` with `details.memberCount` otherwise; `moveTo` must be another non-archived category of the club, else `422`): they are moved in the same transaction with a history entry "Kategorie archiviert". Members who already left keep the archived category. Other categories whose `autoTransitionToKey` pointed here are cleared. The default category `aktiv` cannot be archived (`409`); an archived one answers `409`. `200 { data: category + movedMembers + clearedAutoTransitions }`. Audit `member_category.archive` |
| `/club-members/transition-suggestions` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write` **and** `members:read_sensitive`, else `403` — the list is derived from birth dates; `/apply` only needs `members:write`) | Age transition proposals, derived at read time (no job; scheduler in Wave 9): members of categories with `autoTransitionToKey` + `maxAge` who are older than `maxAge`. `[{ id, name, category: {id,key,name}, suggestedCategory: {id,key,name} }]` — no birth dates |
| `/club-members/transition-suggestions/apply` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Body `{ memberIds: uuid[1..500] }`. One transaction: the suggestions are **recomputed on the locked rows**, qualifying members get the target category (history reason "Altersübergang", audit `club_member.age_transition`). `200 { data: { applied: [{id, fromCategoryId, toCategoryId}], skipped: [{id, reason: "no_suggestion"}] } }` (stale, foreign or unknown ids are skipped, never moved) |
| `/club-members/:id/transition` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Status and/or category change with history, see [Member lifecycle](#member-lifecycle). Body `{ toStatus?, categoryId? \| categoryKey?, effectiveOn? (YYYY-MM-DD), reason?, reopen? }` (at least `toStatus` or a category). `200 { data: <member shape>, transition: { kind: "status\|category\|scheduled_exit\|cancel_exit", fromStatus, toStatus, fromCategoryId, toCategoryId, effectiveOn } }`. `409` for a status outside the matrix (`details.allowed`), no-op or same category, `422` for invalid dates / missing `reopen`+`reason`, `404` for foreign ids, `403` without `members:write`. Audit `club_member.transition` (ids and dates, the free-text reason stays in the history) |
| `/club-members/:id/history` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`members:read_sensitive`) | `club_member_status_history` of one member, newest first: `[{ id, fromStatus, toStatus, fromCategoryId, toCategoryId, effectiveOn, reason, changedBy, createdAt }]` (exit reasons are personal data → sensitive) |
| `/club-stats` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`club_stats:read`: `vorsitz`, `stellv_vorsitz`, `schriftfuehrer`, `kassenwart`, `beisitzer`) | Club KPIs (B11), aggregates only — see [Club KPIs](#club-kpis). `403` for everybody else (including `owner` without a club role, trainers, plain members) |
| `/audit-log` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:read`: `vorsitz`, `stellv_vorsitz`; others `403`, foreign club `404`) | Wave 10 B2. Access and change log of the caller's club only (`audit_log.club_id`; platform events without a club never appear). Query: `subjectId` (exact), `actorId` (user id), `type` (exact event type or prefix with trailing `*`, e.g. `club_member.*`), `kind` (`read` = somebody saw data / export / download, `change` = everything else), `from` / `to` (ISO date or date-time; a plain `to` date includes that day), `page` (default 1), `pageSize` (default 50, max 100). `200 { data: [{ id, eventType, kind, subjectId, subjectMember: { id, name } \| null, actor: { userId, name } \| null, payload, createdAt }], page, pageSize, total }`, newest first. `422` for a bad filter. 60 requests/min per IP. |
| `/audit-log/members/:clubMemberId` | GET | as above | Member view "who saw / changed the data of X": same query and response plus `member: { id, name }`; entries whose `subject_member_id` is this member (`kind=read` = seen, `kind=change` = changed). `404` for a member of another club, an unknown id or a non-UUID. |
| `/audit-log/export` (alias `/audit-log/export.csv`) | GET | as above | The same filters (no paging) as Excel-DE CSV (UTF-8 BOM, `;`, CRLF, formula-neutralised; columns Zeitpunkt, Ereignis, Art `gesehen`/`geändert`, Akteur, Person, Objekt-ID, Details = whitelisted payload as JSON), at most 10 000 rows (`X-Export-Truncated: true` when cut), `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. 10/min per user. Writes `audit_log.export` (row count and filter names only). |
| `/club-members/me/data-export` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Wave 10 B7. The caller's own data (Art. 15), query `format=json` (default, `200 { data: { generatedAt, club, clubMemberId, sections, files, note } }`) or `html` (download). `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, rate limited per account (`DATA_EXPORT_PER_HOUR`, default 5/h → `429`). See [Data export and erasure](#data-export-and-erasure-wave-10-b7-b8) |
| `/club-members/:id/data-export` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:manage`) | Body `{ identityChecked: true, format?: "json" \| "html" }` (the board confirms it checked the requester's identity offline; `422` without it). Also for members without an account. Same document, audit `member_data.export`, closes an open `access` request. Foreign club / unknown id `404` |
| `/club-members/:id/erasure/plan` | POST | as above | Dry run, changes nothing: `200 { data: { clubMemberId, displayName, blockers: [{ code, message }], steps: [{ key, deleted, anonymized, kept, keptReason? }], planDigest } }`. `blockers` codes: `legal_hold`, `exit_pending`, `open_loans`, `board_role`, `sole_owner` (`steps` is empty while blocked) |
| `/club-members/:id/erasure/execute` | POST | as above | Body `{ planDigest, confirm: true }` (digest from the plan). `200 { data: { clubMemberId, erased: true, steps } }`; `409` with `details.code` = a blocker code (`details.blockers` lists all) or `plan_outdated` (data changed since the plan). Irreversible |
| `/club-members/:id/legal-hold` | PUT | as above | Body `{ active, reason? }` (`reason` 1-200 chars, mandatory when `active`) → `200 { data: { clubMemberId, legalHold, reason } }`. While set, erasure is refused (`legal_hold`) |
| `/club-members/me/erasure-request` | POST | session + `clubGuard` | The member asks for erasure: `201 { data: { id, kind: "erasure", status: "open", receivedAt, dueAt } }` (`dueAt` = one month), `200` with the existing open request. Erases nothing; the board runs plan + execute |
| `/club-members/me/data-requests` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Own requests `[{ id, kind, status, receivedAt, dueAt, completedAt }]` |
| `/guardian/wards/:wardId/data-export` | GET | session + `clubGuard` + `guardianScope` | Query `format=json\|html`. The ward's export for the guardian (same document, audit `via: guardian`); a ward that is not the caller's is `404` Needs a link with `has_custody` (`403 guardian_requires_custody` otherwise). |
| `/guardian/wards/:wardId/erasure-request` | POST | as above | As `/club-members/me/erasure-request` for the ward Needs a link with `has_custody` (`403 guardian_requires_custody` otherwise). |
| `/data-subject-requests` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:read`) | Query `status` (`open\|in_progress\|done\|rejected`), `kind` (`access\|erasure\|rectification`), `overdue=true`, `page`, `pageSize` (≤ 100). `200 { data: [{ id, clubMemberId, memberName, kind, status, receivedAt, dueAt, completedAt, result, daysLeft, overdue }], page, pageSize, total }`, soonest deadline first |
| `/data-subject-requests` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:manage`) | Body `{ clubMemberId, kind }` records a request that came in by letter/mail/phone (`201`, `200` if an open one exists) |
| `/data-subject-requests/:id` | PATCH | as above | Body `{ status: "in_progress" \| "done" \| "rejected", result? }` (`result` ≤ 500 chars, mandatory for `rejected`, no personal data); closed requests `422 already_closed`; foreign club `404` |
| `/retention-policies` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:read`) | Wave 10 B9. `200 { data: { dryRun, noticeDays, rules: [{ category, scope: "club" \| "platform", label, description, defaultDays, minDays, maxDays, effectiveDays, overridden, editable }] } }`; `dryRun: true` means nothing is deleted yet |
| `/retention-policies/preview` | GET | as above | What the next sweep would concern in this club (count only): `[{ category, label, due }]` for the club categories |
| `/retention-policies/runs` | GET | as above | The club's sweep log, newest first, `page`, `pageSize` (≤ 100): `{ data: [{ id, category, dryRun, notice, scanned, affected, errors, startedAt, finishedAt }], page, pageSize, total }` |
| `/retention-policies/:category` | PUT | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:manage`) | Body `{ retainDays }` (integer inside the rule's `minDays`..`maxDays`, else `422 out_of_bounds` with the bounds in `details`; a platform category `422 not_editable`; unknown category `404`) → `200 { data: { category, effectiveDays, overridden: true } }`. Audit `retention_policy.update` |
| `/retention-policies/:category` | DELETE | as above | Back to the default → `204` (`404` without an override). Audit `retention_policy.reset` |
| `/admin/retention-runs` | GET | platform admin (`adminGuard`) | Wave 10 A3, read only. Query `scope` (`platform` default \| `club` \| `all`), `category`, `page`, `pageSize`. `200 { data: [{ id, clubId, clubName, category, dryRun, notice, scanned, affected, errors, errorCode, startedAt, finishedAt }], page, pageSize, total, dryRun }` (counters only) |
| `/club-privacy-notices/current` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (any member) | Wave 10 B11. `200 { data: { id, version, body, sha256, publishedAt } \| null }`: the club's current privacy notice, `null` while none is published |
| `/club-privacy-notices` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:read`) | All versions without the text, newest first: `[{ id, version, sha256, publishedAt, publishedBy }]` |
| `/club-privacy-notices/draft` | GET | as above | `{ data: { body, current } }`: the generated template for the editor (filled with club data, settings and retention periods; not stored) and the current version for a diff |
| `/club-privacy-notices/:id` | GET | as above | One version with its text; foreign club / unknown / malformed id `404` |
| `/club-privacy-notices` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:manage`) | Body `{ body? }` (100-50000 characters, default: the generated draft) publishes the next version: `201 { data }`, `200` with the existing one when the text equals the current version, `422 notice_length`. Audit `privacy_notice.publish`. There is no edit route |
| `/clubs/:slug/privacy-notice` | GET | session only | The club's current notice for the join page: `200 { data: { id, version, body, sha256, publishedAt, clubName } \| null }`, unknown slug `404` |
| `/club-members/:id/privacy-notice` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:read`, `privacy:manage` or `members:write`) | Art. 14 status of a member: `{ sentAt, sentVersion, currentVersion, upToDate }` |
| `/club-members/:id/privacy-notice-sent` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:manage` or `members:write`) | Notes that the current notice was handed over (stamps date and version; the first date of a version is kept); `409 no_published_notice` without a notice; audit `club_member.privacy_notice_sent` |
| `/dpa/current` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (any member; never locked by the DPA gate) | Wave 10 B10. `200 { data: { version: { id, version, title, body, subprocessors, tomAnnex, sha256, requiredFrom, publishedAt } \| null, status: { required, version, accepted, acceptedAt, signerName, mandatoryFrom, deadline, daysLeft, blocked }, canAccept } }`. `required` = the gate is on (`DPA_REQUIRED`) and a version is published; `blocked` = writing is locked now; `daysLeft` counts to `deadline` (negative once past, `null` when accepted); `canAccept` = the caller is the owner |
| `/dpa/current/document` | GET | as above | The current agreement as a download, `?format=markdown` (default, `av-vertrag.md`) or `html` (`av-vertrag.html`, escaped text); `404` while none is published |
| `/dpa/accept` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (club **owner** only, others `403`) | Body `{ dpaVersionId, signerName (2-200), authorized: true }` (`422` otherwise). `201 { data: { acceptedAt, signerName, version, status } }`, `200` for a repeat; `409 dpa_version_outdated` (with `currentVersionId`) if a newer version is current, `404` while none is published. Audit `dpa.accept` |
| `/admin/dpa-versions` | GET | platform admin | `200 { data: [{ id, version, title, body, subprocessors, tomAnnex, sha256, requiredFrom, publishedAt, publishedBy, createdAt, status: "draft" \| "published", acceptedClubs }], totalClubs, gate: { required, graceDays } }`, newest first |
| `/admin/dpa-versions` | POST | platform admin | Body `{ title (3-200), body (20-200000), subprocessors?: [{ name, purpose, location }] (≤ 50), tomAnnex?, requiredFrom? (ISO date) }` → `201 { data }` as a draft with the next version number and its checksum |
| `/admin/dpa-versions/:id` | PATCH | platform admin | Any of the create fields (at least one), drafts only; a published version is `409 version_published`. DELETE `204` (drafts only, same `409`) |
| `/admin/dpa-versions/:id/publish` | POST | platform admin | Publishes the draft (immutable, current for every club) → `200 { data }`; `409` if already published. Enqueues the owner notification (#59). Audit `dpa_version.publish` |
| `/admin/dpa-acceptances` | GET | platform admin | Query `versionId` (default the current one), `state` (`accepted` \| `missing`), `page`, `pageSize` (≤ 100). `200 { data: [{ clubId, clubName, accepted, acceptedAt, signerName, acceptedBy, deadline, daysLeft, blocked }], page, pageSize, total, version: { id, version, publishedAt } \| null, gate: { required, graceDays } }` |
| `/consent-purposes` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Wave 10 B3. `200 { data: [{ id, key, name, description, system, requested, active, minAge, sortOrder, archivedAt, currentText: { id, version, body, sha256, publishedAt } \| null }] }`.
