With `privacy:read`/`privacy:manage`: every purpose of the club (incl. inactive and archived); everybody else: only purposes that are active with a published text (what they can be asked about). `requested` only drives the "open" list and reminders, **never** a condition (Art. 7(4) GDPR) |
| `/consent-purposes` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:manage`) | Body `{ name (1-100), description?, requested?, minAge? (0-18, null = club limit), key? }` (key derived from the name, unique per club, `409`) → `201 { data: <purpose> }`, inactive until a text is published. Audit `consent_purpose.create` |
| `/consent-purposes/:id` | PATCH | as above | Body (any): `name`, `description`, `requested`, `active` (`409 no_published_text` without a text), `minAge`, `archived` (system purposes cannot be archived: `409 system_purpose`). Foreign club id `404`. Audit `consent_purpose.update` (field names) |
| `/consent-purposes/:id/texts` | GET | `privacy:read` | Version history `[{ id, version, body, sha256, publishedAt, publishedBy }]`, newest first |
| `/consent-purposes/:id/texts` | POST | `privacy:manage` | Body `{ body (10-5000) }` publishes an immutable version (+1, SHA-256 of the body, the first one activates the purpose) → `201`; sending the current text again is a no-op `200`. Audit `consent_text.publish` |
| `/consents/overview` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`privacy:read`) | Matrix of the club's current members (not left) x askable purposes. Query `q` (name), `purposeId` + `state` (`granted` \| `revoked` \| `none`, latest event; `state` needs `purposeId`), `page`, `pageSize` (max 100). `200 { data: { purposes: [{ id, key, name, requested, counts: { granted, revoked, none } }], members: [{ clubMemberId, displayName, accountLinked, states: { <purposeId>: { state, needsGuardian } } }] }, meta: { total, page, pageSize } }` |
| `/club-members/:id/consents` | GET | session + `clubGuard`; the member themself, a guardian with a live link, `privacy:read` or `privacy:manage` (others `403`, foreign club / unknown id `404`) | `200 { data: { clubMemberId, consents: [{ purposeId, key, name, description, requested, state: granted \| revoked \| none, open, text: { id, version, body }, recordedTextVersion, outdated, givenBy, decidedAt }], open, history: [{ id, purposeId, purposeKey, action, givenBy, version, createdAt, proofDate, proofSource }] } }`. `open` = `requested` and no decision yet (a reminder list only); `outdated` = decided against an older text. Paper proof fields only for the board Each consent carries `retired`: true when the purpose was archived or switched off while the consent is still granted; it stays listed so it can be revoked (`DELETE` keeps working for archived purposes, Art. 7(3); a new grant answers `404`). Any caller who is not the member, a guardian with a live link or `privacy:read`/`privacy:manage` gets `404` (identical to an unknown id, no existence oracle). |
| `/club-members/:id/consents/:purposeId` | POST | as above | Grants. Body `{ textVersionId, proofDate?, proofSource? }`; `textVersionId` must be the currently published version (`409 consent_text_outdated`, `details.currentTextVersionId`), purpose without text/inactive `409 consent_not_available`. Who decides: an adult (at/above `minAge` of the purpose, else `club_settings.consent_age_limit`, default 18; unknown birth date counts as adult) with an account for themself (`self`); for a minor a guardian with a live link and `can_consent` (`guardian`; `403 guardian_cannot_consent`), a minor with an account cannot grant alone (`403 consent_requires_guardian`); the board (`privacy:manage`) records a paper proof for anybody with BOTH `proofDate` and `proofSource` (`board_paper`; missing → `422 proof_required`). Append-only, idempotent (`201` new event, `200` repeat). Audit `consent.grant` (ids only) |
| `/club-members/:id/consents/:purposeId` | DELETE | as above | Revokes / explicitly declines (always possible for the person themself, also a minor; guardians and the board as above; optional JSON body `{ proofDate, proofSource }` for the board's paper proof). Refers to the text version of the last event. Idempotent `200`. Revoking `profilfoto_ausweis` removes the profile photo AND its `media_files` row through the media layer, the old signed URL answers `404`. Audit `consent.revoke` |
| `/club-members/:id/guardians` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Body `{ guardianMemberId, relationship?, hasCustody?, canConsent?, canReportAbsence?, validFrom?, validUntil? }` (Wave 10 B4; the board creates a confirmed link). An external guest account gets the club role `erziehungsberechtigt` (and no `mitglied`); the last link of a guest removed by `DELETE` takes the role away again. Responses carry `relationship`, `hasCustody`, `canConsent`, `canReportAbsence`, `validFrom`, `validUntil`, `confirmedAt`. Audit `guardian_link.create` |
| `/club-members/:id/guardians/:guardianMemberId` | PATCH | as above | Any of `relationship`, `hasCustody`, `canConsent`, `canReportAbsence`, `validFrom`, `validUntil` (null = open end; `validUntil` before `validFrom` → `422`). Audit `guardian_link.update` (field names) |
| `/my-wards` | GET | session only (no club) | Wave 10 B4. The wards of the account across ALL clubs, live links only (see `guardianScope`): `200 { data: [{ clubId, clubName, wards: [{ wardId, name, firstName, relationship, canConsent, canReportAbsence, hasCustody, validUntil, openConsents }] }] }`. `wardId` is the `club_members.id` for the detail routes below |
| `/guardian/wards/:wardId/overview` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`?clubId=` / `X-Club-Id`) + `guardianScope` | `{ ward: { id, name, firstName, lastName, category, departments }, link: { relationship, hasCustody, canConsent, canReportAbsence, validUntil }, openConsents, activeAbsences }`. **`guardianScope`:** every route under `/guardian/wards/:wardId` answers `404` (same answer for another child, another club, an unknown or malformed id, an unconfirmed link, a link outside `valid_from`/`valid_until`, and a ward that is 18 or has left the club) unless the caller has a live `guardian_links` row to that ward in that club, evaluated at read time (no cron). No role string opens these routes and a guardian has no implicit `mitglied` role |
| `/guardian/wards/:wardId/events` | GET | as above | Query `from`, `to` (ISO date, default today + 90 days, max 366 days). Events of the calendars of the ward's departments (department calendar, or a visibility grant for one of the ward's departments or the ward's own account) — NOT of the club-wide calendar or other departments. `[{ id, calendarId, calendarName, title, description, startsAt, endsAt, category, capacity, locationName, cancelledAt, cancelReason, response: angemeldet \| abgesagt \| warteliste \| null, canRespond }]` (`canRespond` false for a ward with an own account: the ward answers there, `response` is read from the account RSVP) |
| `/guardian/wards/:wardId/events/:eventId/response` | PUT | as above | Body `{ status: "angemeldet" \| "abgesagt" }` for a ward WITHOUT account (`ward_event_responses`). Capacity and waitlist count together with the account RSVPs (`angemeldet` at capacity → `warteliste`, event row lock). `404` event not visible for the ward, `409 ward_has_account`, `409` cancelled. `200 { data: { eventId, status } }`. Audit `ward_event_response.set` |
| `/guardian/wards/:wardId/events/:eventId/response` | DELETE | as above | Removes the answer (`204`, `404` without one); frees a seat → the next waiting ward moves up (account waitlist entries are promoted first, `DELETE /events/:id/rsvp`) |
| `/guardian/wards/:wardId/contacts` | GET | as above | Trainers / department leads of the ward's departments: `[{ clubMemberId, name, roleType, department: { id, name }, phone, email }]`; `phone` / `email` only when that person set the field to `mitglieder` (B12), else `null` |
| `/guardian/wards/:wardId/consents` | GET | as above | `{ clubMemberId, canConsent, consents: [<see /club-members/:id/consents>], open }`. Granting and revoking: `POST` / `DELETE /club-members/:wardId/consents/:purposeId` (same service and rules) |
| `/guardian/wards/:wardId/absences` | GET, POST | as above (POST needs `can_report_absence` on the link, else `403 guardian_cannot_report_absence`) | Same bodies and responses as `/absences`; `reportedByKind: "guardian"`. `PATCH` / `DELETE /guardian/wards/:wardId/absences/:id` change / withdraw (same right). Foreign absence ids `404` |
| `/absences` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` (external guests `403`) | Wave 10 B5, reports for oneself. POST `{ kind: "krank" \| "abwesend", startsOn, endsOn, note? }` (`note` ≤ 200 characters, clean text; **no diagnosis field**, clients show a "no diagnoses" hint; `endsOn >= startsOn`, at most 14 days in the past, at most 365 days long; `422` with `details.code` `invalid_period` / `period_too_old` / `period_too_long`) → `201 { data: { id, clubMemberId, kind, startsOn, endsOn, note, reportedByKind, withdrawnAt, createdAt, updatedAt } }`. GET: own reports that have not ended (`?includeWithdrawn=true` for all). Tells the trainers, see Notification catalog #52 |
| `/absences/:id` | PATCH, DELETE | as above | Own report only (foreign ids `404`). PATCH any of `kind`, `startsOn`, `endsOn`, `note` (null clears; `409 absence_withdrawn` after withdrawal). DELETE withdraws ("zurückziehen", `204`, repeating is harmless). Audit `absence.create|update|withdraw` (ids and kind, never the note) |
| `/absences/club` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`absences:read`; others `403`) | Query `from`, `to` (default today … +14 days), `departmentId`. Reports overlapping the period, withdrawn ones excluded: `vorsitz` / `stellv_vorsitz` all members, `trainer` / `abteilungsleitung` only the members of the departments they train/lead (department-bound roles). `[{ <absence>, displayName }]` incl. `note` |
| `/absences/event/:eventId` | GET | as above | Overlay for the participant list ("laut Krankmeldung abwesend"): members in the caller's scope with a report on the day of the event (club time zone): `[{ clubMemberId, displayName, startsOn, endsOn }]` — no kind, no note. `404` for an event whose calendar the caller cannot see |
| `/club-members/:id/visibility` | GET, PUT | session + `clubGuard`; the member themself, a guardian with a live link (PUT: `has_custody`), or `members:write` (others `403`, unknown ids `404`) | Wave 10 B12. Who besides the board may see `phone`, `email`, `address`, `photo`: `nur_vorstand` (default), `trainer` (trainers / department leads), `mitglieder` (club members; for the trainer contacts of `/guardian/wards/:wardId/contacts` also the guardians of the department's children). PUT body: any of the four keys, `200 { data: { clubMemberId, levels } }`. Applied in `GET /club-members`, `GET /club-members/:id` (`phone`, `email` + `contactEmail`, `street` + `postalCode` + `city` appear only when opened; `photoUrl` and `GET /club-members/:id/photo` follow `photo`), never for external guests. Audit `member_field_visibility.update` (field names) |
| `/club-members` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Board creates a member **without account**. Body `{ displayName (required), categoryId \| categoryKey (one required), status? (interessent\|probe\|aktiv\|ruhend, default aktiv), joinedAt?, memberNumber?, firstName?, lastName?, birthDate?, street?, postalCode?, city?, phone?, contactEmail?, emergencyContactName?, emergencyContactPhone? }`. Member number is generated if absent; a manually set number already used in the club answers `409`; manual numbers are 1–50 characters without control characters (`422` otherwise). The response contains sensitive fields only for callers with `members:read_sensitive` (a Schriftführer:in with `members:write` gets the non-sensitive shape back). `201 { data: member }` (full shape). `422` for an unknown/archived/foreign category or invalid status. Writes the first `club_member_status_history` row and audit `club_member.create` |
| `/club-members/me` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Caller's own member (full shape incl. all own contact data) plus `clubMemberId` (= `id`) and `permissions` (the caller's `ClubPermission[]`, now including `club_stats:read` for the board roles like every other permission, for client-side UI gating; `[]` for a plain `mitglied`, incl. the read permissions `meetings:read_board`/`locations:read_access` where granted) |
| `/club-members/me` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` | Self-service update of `firstName`, `lastName` (≤ 100, clean text), `displayName` (1–200, clean text, cannot be cleared), `birthDate`, `emergencyContactName/Phone`, `street`, `postalCode`, `city`, `phone`, `contactEmail` (each nullable; `""` clears; the names reject control/bidi characters with `422`). **Display name rule** (same as the application mapping): after the update both `firstName` and `lastName` set → `displayName` = `"First Last"` (max 200); clearing a name, or a single name while the other is empty, keeps the previous display name; an explicit `displayName` wins. Other fields (member number, category, status) are ignored. External guests keep this route for their own row, former members get `404` from `clubGuard`. Audit `club_member.update_self` (`{ clubId, changedFields }`, field names only, none if nothing changed). Returns the full member shape |
| `/club-members/me/photo` | PUT, DELETE, GET | session + `clubGuard` | Own profile photo (B13). PUT multipart `file` (JPEG/PNG/WebP, **5 MB**, magic bytes, EXIF/GPS stripped, quota), replaces and deletes the previous photo, returns the member shape with the new `photoUrl`; **`403` for a minor** (known birth date < 18: the board sets it). DELETE `204` (idempotent). GET streams the own photo authenticated (`private, no-store`), `404` without one. Audit `club_member.photo_set` / `club_member.photo_remove` (no file names) |
| `/club-members/:id/photo` | PUT, DELETE, GET | session + `clubGuard` (write: `members:write`) | Same for another member, also members **without an account** (and minors). Plain members get `403` on PUT/DELETE; other club's id `404`. GET streams the photo with the same rules as the member shape's `photoUrl` (own / `members:write` / `members:read_sensitive` / linked guardian, else `404`) |
| `/club-members/:id` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | One member; `:id` is the canonical `club_members.id` (uuid). **Scoping:** `404` (never 403) for another member's row when the caller is an external `guest`, and for members with another effective status than `probe`/`aktiv`/`ruhend` when the caller has neither `members:write` nor `members:read_sensitive` (own row always reachable); same rule for `/:id/departments`, `/:id/roles`, `/:id/guardians`. **Transition:** an id that is not a uuid is resolved as the Better Auth `member.id` (ADR-003) — both are strictly club-scoped, another club's id is `404`. Sensitive fields gated as in the list |
| `/club-members/:id` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Board-only master data: `displayName`, `firstName`, `lastName`, `memberNumber` (string, or `null` to clear — only when the key is in the body; duplicate in the club → `409`), `categoryId` or `categoryKey`/`category` (key; unknown → `422`), `birthDate`, `street`, `postalCode`, `city`, `phone`, `contactEmail`, `emergencyContactName/Phone`. **`status` and `leftAt` are rejected (`422`)** — status changes and exit dates go through `POST /club-members/:id/transition` (or the [exit requests](#exit-workflow)); clients that used `PATCH leftAt` must switch. A category change here also writes a history entry. Same response rule as POST (sensitive fields only with `members:read_sensitive` or on the caller's own row, so an empty PATCH cannot be used to read data). A `memberNumber` equal to the current one is accepted even if legacy data holds duplicates; only a changed number is checked for `409`. Audit `club_member.update` lists only the changed field names |
| `/club-members/:id/invite` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Body `{ email }` (lower-cased). Only for members **without account** (`409` otherwise, `409` for members who left). Creates the invitation (replaces an open one), TTL `MEMBER_INVITE_TTL_DAYS` (default 14), mails the link `${WEB_ORIGIN}/einladung?token=…` (German template, HTML-escaped). `201 { data: { id, clubMemberId, email, status: "pending", expiresAt, acceptedAt: null, createdAt, emailSent } }` — **the token is never returned**; a mail failure leaves `emailSent: false` (logged without the address) and blocks nothing. Rate limit **20/hour per user** (`429`). Audit `club_member.invite` (no address) **Since B11** the mail goes through the dispatcher (`mandatory`, sent inline, `member_invite.created`); `emailSent` is `true` if the transport accepted it. The link is wiped from the stored delivery after sending. |
| `/club-members/:id/invite` | GET, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | `GET` → `{ data: invite \| null }` (the open invite of the member; `status` `pending` \| `expired`) for the "Einladungsstatus" display; `DELETE` revokes it (`204`, `404` if none) |
| `/club-members/:id/invite/resend` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | No body. Revokes the open invitation (also an expired one) and issues a new token + mail to the **same address** → `201` same shape as `POST /:id/invite` (`emailSent`; the token is never returned). `404` if there is no open invitation (or the member already has an account). Shares the **20/hour/user** limit with invitation creation (`429`). Audit `club_member.invite_resend` |
| `/club-members/:id/unlink` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Body `{ force? }`. Detaches the account: the `club_members` row stays (status unchanged — **not an exit**, `last_user_id` kept, `account_unlinked_at` set), the account's `member` row and everything bound to it (roles, loans, key holder entries, RSVPs) goes. `200 { data: <member shape> }` (`hasAccount: false`). `409` for a member without account, the caller's own account, an `owner` account, or an account with open loans/keys (`details.code = "account_has_open_items", openLoans, keys`) unless `force: true`. Audit `club_member.unlink` |
| `/club-members/:id/departments` | GET, POST, DELETE `/:departmentId` | session + `clubGuard` (POST/DELETE need `members:write`) | Department assignment with validity period. GET `[{ id, name, since, until }]`; POST `{ departmentId, since? }` → `201` (department must belong to the club, else `404`; already assigned → `409`); DELETE ends the open assignment (`until` = today or `?until=YYYY-MM-DD`) and keeps the history → `204`, `404` if none open |
| `/club-members/:id/roles` | GET, POST, DELETE `/:roleId` | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `roles:write`) | `club_roles` assignment on the **linked account** — `roleType` one of `CLUB_ROLE_TYPES` (`src/lib/club-permissions.ts`), optional `departmentId`/`termEndsAt`; assigning a role the member already holds returns `409`. **`mitglied` is implicit and cannot be assigned (`422`, not part of the enum) or revoked (`409`);** `GET` answers `{ data: [stored club_roles rows], implicit: ["mitglied"] }` — `data` is unchanged (only revocable rows), the implicit base role is listed separately. **A member without account answers `409 "Member has no account"`** on all three methods (Wave 11 B8: the role rows and the `roles` of the member list carry `termStatus` and `termDaysLeft`) |
| `/club-roles/expiring` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage` or `roles:write`) | Wave 11 B8. `200 { data: [{ id, clubMemberId, name, roleType, departmentId, termEndsAt, termStatus, termDaysLeft }], termExpiryEffect }` for roles with `termStatus` `laeuft_bald_ab` or `abgelaufen`, soonest end first. The term end is the last valid day; `laeuft_bald_ab` = within the longest `termWarningDays` (90 days for the display while none are stated). Link the UI to `POST /elections` (`roleType`, `departmentId`) for "Neuwahl planen". With `termExpiryEffect = suspend_permissions` (club setting, default `none` = display only) `clubGuard` drops the rights of a role type whose rows are all expired — except while nobody holds an unexpired `vorsitz`/`stellv_vorsitz` role, so the club keeps its capacity to act |
| `/club-members/:id/guardians` | GET, POST, DELETE `/:guardianMemberId` | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `members:write`) | `guardian_links` for the externe Rolle — board-managed only. `:id` is the **ward** (any club member, account optional); `guardianMemberId` (body / path) is a `club_members.id` or, for older clients, the guardian's `member.id`, and the guardian must have an account (`409` otherwise, `404` if not in this club, `422` if equal to the ward, `409` for a duplicate). **Reading** (`GET`) needs `members:read_sensitive` or being a guardian of this ward (`403` otherwise) because guardian names are personal data. Link shape: `{ id, guardianMemberId (member.id), guardianClubMemberId, guardianName, wardClubMemberId, wardMemberId (account id or null), createdAt }` **GET for a guardian (without `members:read_sensitive`) returns only their own link, never the co-guardians.** |
| `/club-settings` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | `200 { data: { noticePeriodDays, noticeMode, memberNumberPrefix, allowSelfLoans, timezone, reminderEventEnabled, reminderEventLeadHours, reminderLoanEnabled, reminderLoanLeadDays, reminderMaintenanceEnabled, reminderMaintenanceLeadDays, reminderRsvpEnabled, reminderRsvpLeadDays, reminderInvitationEnabled, reminderInvitationLeadDays, reminderCredentialEnabled, reminderCredentialLeadDays, ageTransitionAuto, notifyBoardRequests, notifyMeetingInvites, notifyAgeTransition, notifyHandover, mailLanguage, consentAgeLimit, consentBothGuardians, privacyContact, privacyController, senderName, signature, replyTo, textBlocks } }` for **every** member; the four mail template fields (B15) are `null`/`null`/`null`/`[]` unless the caller holds `announcements:write` (defaults `null`/`null`/`null`/`true`, `Europe/Berlin`, reminders on with 24 h / 1 / 7 / 3 and invitations 3 days / credentials 30 days, age transition off, without a row; `memberNumberPrefix` is `null` when not set, i.e. the slug abbreviation is used) |
| `/club-settings` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write` for the club parameters, `announcements:write` for the mail template fields; `privacy:manage` for the privacy fields; an empty body needs `members:write`) | Body (all optional): `noticePeriodDays` (int 0–365 \| null), `noticeMode` (`immediate` \| `month_end` \| `quarter_end` \| `year_end` \| null), `memberNumberPrefix` (1–10 letters/digits, stored upper case, null = slug abbreviation; used for NEW member numbers), `allowSelfLoans` (bool), `timezone` (valid IANA name), `reminderEventEnabled`/`reminderLoanEnabled`/`reminderMaintenanceEnabled`/`reminderRsvpEnabled` (bool), `reminderEventLeadHours` (int 1–168), `reminderLoanLeadDays` (0–30), `reminderMaintenanceLeadDays` (0–90), `reminderRsvpLeadDays` (1–30), `reminderInvitationEnabled` (bool), `reminderInvitationLeadDays` (1–30), `reminderCredentialEnabled` (bool), `reminderCredentialLeadDays` (1–365), `ageTransitionAuto` (bool), `notifyBoardRequests` (bool), `notifyMeetingInvites` (bool), `notifyAgeTransition` (bool), `notifyHandover` (bool), `mailLanguage` (`de` \| `en`; language of the system texts in club mails and pushes, `members:write`), **privacy (Wave 10, `privacy:manage`)**: `consentAgeLimit` (int 10–18, age from which a person consents alone, default 18), `consentBothGuardians` (bool), `privacyContact` (≤300 chars, one line, empty/null clears), `privacyController` (≤500 chars, empty/null clears); both texts feed the generated privacy notice; **mail template (B15)**: `senderName` (≤80 chars, one line, empty/null clears), `signature` (≤1000 chars plain text, line breaks allowed), `replyTo` (valid e-mail address, normalized to lower case, null clears; no CR/LF), `textBlocks` (≤20 × `{ key: 1–40 chars [a-z0-9_-], unique, title ≤80, text ≤2000 }`, replaces the whole list; e.g. a default cancellation text the client inserts into the editor). Control and bidi characters are rejected (`422`). Creates the row on demand; `200 { data: <same shape as GET> }`. Audit `club_settings.update` |
| `/club-settings/assembly` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (external guests `403`) | `200 { data: { rules, missing, complete } }`. `rules` = the statute parameters of the general assembly (Wave 11 B1): `invitationMinDays`, `invitationDeliveryBufferDays`, `invitationForm` (`textform`\|`schriftlich`), `agendaRequiredInInvitation`, `quorumMode` (`none`\|`fraction`\|`absolute`) + `quorumValue`, `majorityByTopic` (`allgemein`, `satzungsaenderung`, `zweckaenderung`, `wahl`, `entlastung`, `aufloesung` → `einfach`\|`absolut`\|`zwei_drittel`\|`drei_viertel`\|`einstimmig`), `abstainCountsAs` (`not_counted`\|`against`), `proxyAllowed` + `maxProxiesPerPerson`, `onlineAllowed` (`none`\|`hybrid`\|`virtual`), `circularAllowed` + `circularMinParticipation` + `circularUnanimity`, `secretBallotDigitalAllowed`, `votingMinAge`, `votingStatuses`, `termWarningDays`, `termExpiryEffect` (`none`\|`suspend_permissions`), `satzungReference`, `satzungPageSlug`. **No defaults for legal parameters** (`null` = not stated); the switches default closed (`onlineAllowed: none`, `circularAllowed: false`, `secretBallotDigitalAllowed: false`, `termExpiryEffect: none`). `missing` lists the keys the club still has to state before the first invitation (`majorityByTopic.allgemein` for topics), `complete` = `missing` empty |
| `/club-settings/assembly` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Partial body with any of the keys above (`null` clears a legal parameter, `majorityByTopic` merges topic by topic); merged result is validated (`quorumValue` 0-1 for `fraction`, whole number for `absolute`, empty for `none`; `maxProxiesPerPerson` empty while proxies are off; no duplicates) → `422`; parallel patches are serialised on the settings row. Audited as `club_settings.assembly_update` (field names only). Returns the same shape as GET |
| `/club-settings/mail-preview` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`announcements:write`) | Renders a club mail with **sample data** (sample recipient "Max", sample title/text; nothing is sent or stored; 30/min per user). Body (all optional): `template` (`announcement` default \| `urgent` \| `reminder` \| `decision`), unsaved edits `senderName`/`signature`/`replyTo` that override the stored values for this preview, `textBlockKey` (use a stored block as body; unknown key → `422`) → `200 { data: { sample: true, subject, html, from, replyTo } }`. `from` is the fixed `EMAIL_FROM` address with the club display name (a name cannot change the address); `html` is a complete mail document (without the cid logo image, which only exists as an attachment of the real mail), to be shown in a sandboxed iframe. `Cache-Control: private, no-store` |
| `/club-exit-requests/preview` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | `?requestedOn=YYYY-MM-DD` (default today Europe/Berlin; past → `422`) → `200 { data: { requestedOn, noticePeriodDays, noticeMode, earliestEffectiveOn } }` |
| `/club-exit-requests/form` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | The club's published **exit form** (`austritt`, Wave 8 B9) for the cancellation dialog: `{ data: { formId, formVersionId, version, purpose, name, privacyNotice, childApplication, fields } }` (applicant view: no admin data). Standard form v1: `reason` (textarea, optional, ≤ 1000) and `confirm` (checkbox, required). `Cache-Control: private, no-cache`. The standard forms are seeded on first access if the club lacks them |
| `/club-exit-requests/mine` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Own requests (about the caller's member row), newest first: `{ data: [request] }` |
| `/club-exit-requests` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Board queue. Query `status` (`pending`\|`approved`\|`rejected`\|`withdrawn`), `kind` (`cancellation`\|`exclusion`), `page`, `pageSize` (default 50, max 100). `{ data: [request], page, pageSize, total }`, newest first |
| `/club-exit-requests/:id` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | `{ data: request & { checklist, submission } }` for the board, the requester and the member concerned (`404` for everybody else and for foreign clubs). `submission` = `null` without a form, else `{ id, formVersionId, version, submittedAt, answers: [rendered answer] }` rendered against the version the member filled in (see [Exit form](#exit-form-wave-8-b9)); fields marked `sensitive` are `redacted` unless the caller has `members:read_sensitive` or is the submitter. `checklist` = `{ openLoans: [{ id, itemId, itemName, borrowedAt, dueAt, overdue }], keys: [{ id, locationId, locationName }], isEmpty }`, computed live from the **account's** open `inventory_loans` and `location_key_holders` (members without account: always empty) |
| `/club-exit-requests` | POST | session + `clubGuard` | **Member:** body `{ desiredEffectiveOn?, reason?, answers?, formVersionId? }` (no `clubMemberId`, or the own one) → `201 { data: request & { adjustedToEarliest } }`, status `pending`. With `answers` the exit form is validated by the form engine (`422 details.fields: { key: code }`, `409 FORM_VERSION_OUTDATED` for a stale `formVersionId`) and stored as a submission, `formSubmissionId` on the request ([Exit form](#exit-form-wave-8-b9)); without them nothing changes. **Board** (`members:write`, `403` otherwise): `{ clubMemberId, kind: "cancellation" \| "exclusion", reason (required for exclusion), effectiveOn?, answers? (cancellation only) }` → `201 { data: request, transition }`, recorded as `approved` at once. Rules: [Exit workflow](#exit-workflow). `409` (`details.code` `exit_request_pending` / `exit_already_scheduled`) for a second open request or a scheduled exit, `409` for a member who already left, `422` for dates before the earliest allowed date **Notifications (B11):** a member request is confirmed (`exit.received`, with the earliest possible date); a board-recorded cancellation sends `exit.accepted`, an exclusion `exit.exclusion` (the reason is included); see [Decision notifications](#decision-notifications-wave-9-b11). |
| `/club-exit-requests/:id/approve` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Body `{ effectiveOn?, note? }`. Claims the request (`UPDATE … WHERE status = 'pending'`) and applies the exit via `transitionMember` in one transaction → `200 { data: request, transition }`. `effectiveOn` ≥ earliest date, else `422 details.earliestEffectiveOn`. `409` if already decided **Notification (B11):** `exit.accepted` (or `exit.exclusion`) with the effective date. |
| `/club-exit-requests/:id/reject` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Body `{ note }` (required, `422` if empty) → `200 { data: request }`; `409` if already decided **Notification (B11):** `exit.rejected` with the board's note. |
| `/club-exit-requests/:id/withdraw` | POST | session + `clubGuard` | Requester or `members:write`; body `{ note? }`. `pending` → `withdrawn`; `approved` cancellation not yet effective → `withdrawn` **and** the scheduled exit is taken back (`left_at` cleared, history "Kündigung widerrufen"). `409` once effective or for an exclusion; `403` for the member concerned who is not the requester, `404` for strangers |
| `/club-applications` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Pending Aufnahmeantraege of the club, oldest first: `{ data: [ { id, userId, name, email, category, birthDate, status, onBehalfOf, createdAt, formSubmissionId, legacy, subjectName, summary: [{ key, label, display }], fileCount, consentGiven } ], page, pageSize, total }` (query `page` default 1, `pageSize` default 50, max 100; `Cache-Control: private, no-store`). `birthDate` stays in the shape but is **null without `members:read_sensitive`**. `summary` = the first 4 answered, **non-sensitive**, compact fields (no free-text areas, no consents; values cut to 80 characters) rendered with the labels/option names of the version the applicant filled in; `subjectName` = the child for a child application, else the applicant; a legacy row has `summary: []`, `consentGiven: null`. Lazy cleanup of the club's overdue data runs here ([Review API](#review-api-and-retention-wave-8-b10)). 403 without the permission |
| `/club-applications/:id` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | **Full review** of one application of any status ([Review API](#review-api-and-retention-wave-8-b10)): `{ data: { application, applicant, subject, form, answers, answersRetained, mapping: { preview, entries }, files, consent, duplicates, warnings, decision } }`. Answers are rendered against the **original** version; `sensitive` fields **and the protected master data** (birth date, address, phone, emergency contact) are `redacted` without `members:read_sensitive`; delivering them is audited (`club_application.sensitive_read`: field keys). `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. `404` for a foreign club's, unknown or malformed id; `403` without `members:write` |
| `/club-applications/:id/approve` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Approve a pending application: `auth.api.addMember({ role: 'member' })` (its `afterAddMember` hook creates the `club_members` row), then in one DB transaction the row gets the application's category/birthDate, `joinedAt` = today, status `aktiv` and an automatic member number, application set to `approved` with `decided_at`/`decided_by` (caller's `member.id`). `200 { data: { application, member, membership, clubMember } }` (`membership` is the pre-Wave-7 shape, deprecated; `clubMember` is the canonical `club_members` row). 404 for an unknown id or another club's application, 409 if not `pending` or if the applicant is already a member. **Exception — re-admission:** if the applicant is a former member (`ausgetreten`, account membership still present) no new `member` is created; the existing `club_members` row is reopened via `transitionMember` (`reopen`, reason "Wiederaufnahme per Aufnahmeantrag", application category/birth date applied, original `joinedAt` and number kept) and the same response shape is returned. If the transaction fails after Better Auth created the `member`, the `club_members` and `member` rows are removed again (best effort) and the application stays `pending`. Optional body `{ categoryId | categoryKey }` (the board changes the applicant's category; `422` if not an active category). **Form applications** (`form_submission_id` set) additionally apply the mapping allowlist (master data, photo handover, custom fields), set the submission `accepted` and answer with `mapping` (`409 application_withdrawn` if the applicant withdrew, `409 decision_busy` after a lock timeout/deadlock — retry); see [Application acceptance](#application-acceptance-and-mapping-wave-8-b6). Audit `club_application.approve`. **Child application** (`on_behalf_of = guardian`): no account is created for the child; response `200 { data: { application, member: null, clubMember (the child, account-less), guardian: { memberId, userId, createdAsExternal, linked }, mapping } }`, see [Child applications](#child-applications-wave-8-b8) **Notification (Wave 9 B11):** after the commit the applicant (for a child application: the parent) gets a `mandatory` message (`application.approved`, in-app + e-mail, with the member number); best effort, never changes the result. |
| `/club-applications/:id/reject` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write`) | Reject a pending application: status `rejected`, `decided_at`/`decided_by`, no member row. Optional body `{ note?, applicantMessage? }` (≤ 1000 characters each; `note` = internal, `applicantMessage` = shown to the applicant as `decisionNote`; both ignored for legacy applications). `200 { data: { application } }`. 404/409 as for approve. Audit `club_application.reject` **Notification (B11):** the applicant gets a `mandatory` message (`application.rejected`) containing `applicantMessage` only (never the internal `note`). |
| `/member-fields` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Custom field **definitions**: `{ data: [{ id, key, label, type, options, validation, helpText, translations, visibleTo, editableBy, sensitive, sortOrder, archived, archivedAt }] }`. Holders of `forms:write` get all (with `valueCount`; `?includeArchived=true`), everybody else only the live definitions whose values members may see of others or that they may fill in themselves (`includeArchived` is `403`) `translations` is `{ de: { label, helpText }, en: { label, helpText } }` for a definition derived from a profile module whose label and help text the board has not changed, else `null` (a board-edited text is club content and is never translated). `?lang=en` (explicit only, no Accept-Language) additionally returns `label`/`helpText` in English for those definitions; stored text stays German. |
| `/member-fields` | POST | `forms:write` (checked before the body) | Body `{ key, label, helpText?, type, options?, validation? (credential only), visibleTo? (vorstand\|funktionstraeger\|mitglied, default vorstand), editableBy? (vorstand\|self, default vorstand), sensitive?, sortOrder? }`. Shape rules are the form engine's (key `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,40}$`, reserved words, clean text, ≤ 50 options, choice types need options): `422`; duplicate key `409` (`custom_field_exists`); max 100 per club (`409 custom_field_limit`). `201 { data: definition }`. Audit `member_field.create` |
| `/member-fields/catalog` | GET | `forms:write` | Profile module catalog with the activation state per club: `{ data: { lang, groups: [{ id, label, translations: { de: { label }, en: { label } } }], modules: [{ id, group, label, description, helpText, translations: { de: { label, description, helpText }, en: { label, description, helpText } }, type, options, validation, visibleTo, editableBy, sensitive, mandatory: false, neverMandatoryGroup, state: "active" \| "archived" \| "inactive", fieldId, customized }], limit: { used, max } } }` (see [Profile modules](#profile-modules-profil-bausteine-and-the-credential-type)) **Language:** `?lang=de\|en` (wins) or the first supported `Accept-Language` entry (`en...`), default `de`, unknown values and an empty `?lang=` are `de` (an explicit `?lang`, even empty, beats the header); the catalog responses send `Vary: Accept-Language`; `lang` echoes the language used. `label`/`description`/`helpText` and the group `label` are in that language, `translations` always carries German and English (`helpText` is `null` when the module has none). Option labels are not translated. The same `?lang`/Accept-Language applies to `activate` and `deactivate` (`module`; `field` follows the `/member-fields` rule). |
| `/member-fields/catalog/:moduleId/activate` | POST | `forms:write` (checked before anything else) | One-click activation (sensitive modules also need `members:read_sensitive`, `403`): creates the definition from the template (`201 { data: { module, created: true, reactivated: false, field } }`), is a no-op for an active module (`200`, `created: false`), restores an archived one (`200`, `reactivated: true`, board customisations kept). **Never overwrites** an existing definition; serialised with `POST /member-fields` per club (max 100 definitions: `409 custom_field_limit`). Unknown module `404`. Audit `profile_module.activate` (ids, module id) |
| `/member-fields/catalog/:moduleId/deactivate` | POST | `forms:write` | Archives the module's definition, **values stay** for the record; idempotent (`200 { data: { module, changed } }`, `changed: false` when already archived/never active). Audit `profile_module.deactivate` |
| `/member-fields/:id` | PATCH | `forms:write` | `label`, `helpText`, `validation` (credential `{maxLength}`), `type`, `options`, `visibleTo`, `editableBy`, `sensitive`, `sortOrder`. **Type change only while no value exists** (`409 custom_field_has_values`), an option in use cannot be removed (`409 option_in_use`), archived definitions are read-only (`409 custom_field_archived`). Foreign / unknown ids `404`. Audit `member_field.update` (changed property names) |
| `/member-fields/:id` | DELETE | `forms:write` | Deletes a definition **without values**; with values it is **archived** instead: `200 { data: { id, outcome: "deleted" \| "archived" } }`. Audit `member_field.delete` / `member_field.archive` |
| `/member-fields/:id/unarchive` | POST | `forms:write` | Restores an archived definition (`409` if it is not archived) |
| `/club-members/credentials` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (external guests `403`) | **Credential overview** (profile modules, expiry visible to the board and Funktionsträger). Query: `fieldKey?` (a credential definition key), `status?` = `expired\|expiring\|valid\|missing`, `withinDays?` (1–730, default 90, the "expiring" window), `page?`, `pageSize?` (≤ 100, default 50), `includeFormer?=true` (only honoured for `members:write`/`members:read_sensitive`). **Callers: board-level (`members:write` / `members:read_sensitive`) and the roles `trainer` / `abteilungsleitung` only; plain members get `403`, even for a credential field visible to `mitglied`.** The scope of a trainer / Abteilungsleitung is **club-wide** (all probe/aktiv/ruhend members, not only their department). Rate limit 60/min per user, `page` ≤ 1000, `pageSize` ≤ 100. Response `{ data: [{ clubMemberId, displayName, firstName, lastName, fieldKey, fieldLabel, sensitive, value, updatedAt, enteredBy, expiry: { status, daysLeft, validUntil } }], page, pageSize, total, today (Berlin), withinDays, fields: [{ id, key, label, sensitive, options }] }`. Only credential fields the caller may read (visibility + `sensitive` rules, i.e. `members:read_sensitive` for Kinderschutz) and only members the caller may read (probe/aktiv/ruhend like the member list; former members and interessenten only with `includeFormer=true` for the board). Without `fieldKey`: held credentials of all visible fields; `status=missing` needs a `fieldKey` (`422 field_key_required`) and lists members who do not hold it. A `fieldKey` that is unknown, archived, not a credential or not visible is `404` (indistinguishable). Sorted by `validUntil` ascending (expired first; no-expiry and missing last), then name. **Every** query that includes a `sensitive` field writes ONE `member_field.sensitive_read` audit entry (`via: credentials_overview`) — also for `status=missing` or an empty page, because `total` and the missing list disclose who holds the credential; it carries the field ids and the member ids of rows with a value, never values. No notifications (Wave 9) |
| `/club-members/:id/custom-fields` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | `{ data: { clubMemberId, fields: [{ id, key, label, type, options, validation, helpText, translations, visibleTo, editableBy, sensitive, sortOrder, archived, archivedAt, editable, value, updatedAt, enteredBy, expiry }] } }` — only the definitions the caller may see (rules above), `value` is `null` when unset, `expiry` is `{ status, daysLeft, validUntil }` for `credential` fields (see [Expiry semantics](#profile-modules-profil-bausteine-and-the-credential-type), 90-day window; `missing` without a value) and `null` for every other type; `enteredBy` is `self` (the person recorded it itself: a **self-declaration, not verified** by the club), `guardian` (a guardian of the child recorded it, an **unchecked declaration** like `self`: **clients must treat every `enteredBy` other than `board` as unverified**; stored in `member_field_values.entered_via`, so it stays after the guardianship ended; a later write by the board or the person resets it), `board` (somebody else wrote it) or `null` (no value); no person ids are exposed; `?includeArchived=true` needs `members:write`/`members:read_sensitive`. Member row scoped like the member detail (`404`); an active guardian of the child (not the board) gets the guardian view (see the rights table; `editable` is true for `editable_by = self` non-sensitive fields). A read of somebody else's `sensitive` value writes `member_field.sensitive_read` |
| `/club-members/:id/custom-fields` | PUT, PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (`members:write` writes as the board; otherwise only an **active guardian of that child** (`guardian_links`) writes, everybody else `403`) | Body `{ values: { [definitionKey]: value \| null } }` (1–100 keys; `null`/empty clears). Validated atomically: `422` with `details.fields` (`unknown_field` also for archived or invisible keys, `invalid_type`, `not_an_option`, `too_long`, ...; no values are echoed), `403` for a visible field the caller may not change; a `sensitive` field also needs `members:read_sensitive`. Returns the same view as GET. Audit `member_field.write` (field ids only; `via: "board"`, or `via: "guardian"` with `changedBy` = the guardian's `member.id` and subject = the child's `club_members.id`). **Guardian mode:** only `editable_by = self`, non-archived, non-`sensitive` fields (credentials included, e.g. Juleica); a sensitive field (Kinderschutz) or any field the guardian cannot see (including board-internal ones) is `422 unknown_field`; the guardianship is re-checked under a share lock inside the write transaction, so a link removed concurrently cannot land a write; the `403` for non-guardians is answered before body validation; per-user rate limits apply before any lookup (`PUT`/`PATCH` 60/min, `GET` 120/min), a visible field only the board may edit is `403`; values are validated like for the board; removing the link (`DELETE /club-members/:id/guardians/:guardianId`) ends the access immediately. PATCH is an alias of PUT |
| `/club-members/me/custom-fields` | GET, PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (external guests `403`) | Own values (incl. sensitive and what the board recorded). PATCH `{ values }` changes only `editable_by = self` fields (`403` for a visible read-only one, `422 unknown_field` for a hidden one); same validation and audit as PUT |
| `/club-members/me/children` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (120/min per user) | The children the caller is an **active guardian** of in this club (`guardian_links`): `{ data: [{ clubMemberId, displayName, firstName, lastName, status }] }`, ordered by name, `status` = effective status. Wards who left (`ausgetreten`/`ausgeschlossen`/`verstorben`) are not listed; foreign clubs' links never show. Non-guardians get `200 { data: [] }` (no 403 oracle). `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. Use `clubMemberId` with `/club-members/:id/custom-fields` |
| `/forms` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`forms:write`) | Forms of the club (standard `aufnahme`/`austritt` forms are created lazily on first access). Query `purpose?`, `includeArchived?=true`. `{ data: [{ id, purpose, name, archivedAt, createdAt, updatedAt, publishedVersion, draftVersion, versionCount }] }`. **Every `/forms` route needs `forms:write` (vorsitz, stellv_vorsitz, schriftfuehrer), reads included (`403` otherwise); forms of another club and malformed ids are `404`** |
| `/forms` | POST | `forms:write` | Body `{ purpose, name (1-120), fields?, privacyNotice? (up to 10000 characters, or null) }` → creates the form with a first draft (version 1). At most 50 active forms per club (`409`, `details.code = form_limit`). `fields` is validated by `formFieldsSchema` (`422` with `details.issues`). `201 { data: { form, draft } }`. A second active `aufnahme`/`austritt` form is `409` (`details.code = form_purpose_exists`); those two always exist, so edit the standard form. Audit `form.create` |
| `/forms/:id` | GET | `forms:write` | `{ data: { form, published: version \| null, draft: version \| null } }`; a version is `{ id, version, status, fields, privacyNotice, publishedAt, publishedBy, createdAt, updatedAt }` |
| `/forms/:id/draft` | PUT | `forms:write` | Save the draft (created with the next version number if none): body `{ fields, name?, privacyNotice? }` (`privacyNotice` omitted = keep, or copy from the live version for a new draft). `422` for an invalid definition or when a key that was part of a published version changes its type (`details.problems: [{ code: "key_type_immutable", fieldKey }]`; dropping fields is always allowed). `409` (`details.code = form_archived`) for an archived form; at most 200 versions per form (`409`, `details.code = version_limit`). Runs under a row lock on the form. Audit `form.draft_save` (ids and field count only, never the definition). `200 { data: { form, draft } }` |
| `/forms/:id/versions/:n` | PUT | `forms:write` | Same body; only the number of the current draft is accepted. **A `published`/`archived` version is immutable: `409` (`details.code = version_immutable`)**; `404` for an unknown number |
| `/forms/:id/draft` | DELETE | `forms:write` | Discard the draft (`204`; `404` if there is none). Audit `form.draft_discard` |
| `/forms/:id/publish` | POST | `forms:write` | Publish the draft: in one transaction under the form's row lock the previous `published` version becomes `archived` and the draft becomes `published` (`published_at/by`). **Two parallel publishes create exactly one new version** (the loser finds no draft: `409`, `details.code = no_draft`). Publish checks (`422`, `details.problems: [{ code, fieldKey? }]`): `aufnahme` needs a privacy notice block (`privacy_notice_missing`) with text (`privacy_notice_text_missing`) and no required consent: **a consent is voluntary (Art. 7(4) GDPR), so any form with a `consent` field that is `required` is refused with `required_consent_not_allowed`** (the old `required_consent_missing` is gone); `austritt` must not contain `file`/`image` fields because the cancellation cannot upload (`file_not_supported`, `fieldKey`; also enforced at `PUT /forms/:id/draft`, `PUT /forms/:id/versions/:n` and `POST /forms` with the same `422 details.problems`); `mapsTo`/`customField` only on `aufnahme` (`mapping_not_allowed`); every `customField` must be a non-archived `member_field_definitions` key of the club with a matching type (`custom_field_unknown`, `custom_field_type_mismatch`); `key_type_immutable` as above; `sensitive_mapping_not_allowed` (a `sensitive` field must not map to master data), `custom_field_not_sensitive` (its custom field must be `sensitive` too), `no_selectable_category` (a category field but no applicant-selectable category). `200 { data: { form, version } }`. Audit `form.publish` (ids/version numbers only) |
| `/forms/:id/archive` | POST | `forms:write` | Archive the form: published version → `archived`, an open draft is deleted, applicants no longer get it (`409` if already archived). For `aufnahme`/`austritt` a fresh standard form (v1) is created lazily on the next access, so archiving the customised one resets the club to the standard form. Audit `form.archive` |
| `/forms/:id/versions` | GET | `forms:write` | `{ data: [{ id, version, status, fieldCount, publishedAt, createdAt, updatedAt }] }`, newest first |
| `/forms/:id/versions/:n` | GET | `forms:write` | Read-only view of any version: `{ data: { form, version } }` |
| `/club-forms/:clubSlug/aufnahme` | GET | session (no `clubGuard`; 60/min per user) | The **published** application form for applicants: `{ data: { formId, formVersionId, version, purpose, name, privacyNotice, childApplication, fields } }` (`childApplication`: the form asks for first and last name, so a parent can apply for a child with it). Select options are resolved (`member_categories` → active **applicant-selectable** categories); **no admin data** (no `mapsTo`/`customField`, no draft, no club data). `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. `404` for an unknown club or a malformed slug. **Reading does not write**: the standard form and categories are seeded (one atomic transaction under an advisory lock) only when this access finds the club without a published `aufnahme` form or without an applicant-selectable category |
| `/form-submissions` | POST | session (no `clubGuard`; `FORM_SUBMISSIONS_PER_HOUR` per user) | Create an application draft. Body `{ clubSlug or clubId, formVersionId?, answers?, purpose?: "aufnahme", onBehalfOf?: "self" \| "guardian" }` (`guardian` = child application, B8: verified e-mail required `403 email_not_verified`, the form must name the child `409 child_application_unsupported`, at most 5 open child applications per account `409 child_application_limit`, a former member `409 guardian_not_active`; ids of wards in the body are ignored). `answers` are validated in draft mode. `201 { data: { submission } }`; a submission is `{ id, formId, formVersionId, clubId, onBehalfOf, status, answers, submittedAt, decidedAt, decisionNote, createdAt, updatedAt, files: [{ id, fieldKey, filename, mimeType, sizeBytes, kind, url \| null, createdAt }] }` — a file carries a short-lived **signed** `url` only for non-sensitive images, never a storage key. `409`: `FORM_VERSION_OUTDATED` (given `formVersionId` is not the published one), `ALREADY_MEMBER` (real members only: an external guest guardian may apply for themself), `APPLICATION_PENDING` (pending application or a submitted form), `details.code = draft_limit` (max 3 open drafts per user and club) or `draft_limit_user` (max 10 over all clubs), both enforced under advisory locks. `422` for invalid answers: `details.fields: { key: code }` (codes only, never the submitted values) |
| `/form-submissions/mine` | GET | session | Own submissions (newest 100) with `purpose`, `version`, `club: { id, name, slug }`. `Cache-Control: private, no-store` |
| `/form-submissions/:id` | GET | session (submitter only) | `{ data: { submission, form, outdated } }` — `form` is the applicant definition of the pinned version, `outdated` = draft whose version is no longer the published one. `Cache-Control: private, no-store` (drafts hold child/health data) |
| `/form-submissions/:id` | DELETE | session (submitter only; `FORM_SUBMISSIONS_PER_HOUR` per user, own bucket) | Discards an own **draft**: hard-deletes the submission, its answers and files (registry entries tombstoned in the transaction, bytes purged after the commit; drafts have no `club_applications` row). `204`. `409 submission_not_deletable` (`details.status`) for submitted/accepted/rejected/withdrawn submissions, `404` for foreign or malformed ids. Audit `form_submission.delete` (ids and file count only, no answers) |
| `/form-submissions/:id` | PATCH | session (submitter only) | Draft only (`409 submission_not_editable` otherwise). Body `{ answers?, formVersionId? }`: `answers` are **merged** into the stored ones (`null` removes a key), validated in draft mode — **only the keys the request changes must be valid**: an unchanged stored answer that became invalid (the club archived the category/option) neither blocks the edit nor is lost, `submit` validates everything and names the key (unknown keys incl. `__proto__`, info/file keys → `422`). A draft on a stale version answers **`409 FORM_VERSION_OUTDATED`** with `details: { submissionId, currentVersion }` (`currentVersion` = the applicant definition, `null` if the club has no such form right now); sending `formVersionId` of the current version **re-pins** it: answers are carried over by key, `200 { data: { submission, repinned: true, droppedAnswerKeys } }`, files of fields that vanished or changed kind are deleted |
| `/form-submissions/:id/files` | POST | session (submitter only; `FORM_UPLOADS_PER_HOUR` per user) | Draft only. Multipart `file` + `fieldKey` (must be a `file`/`image` field of the pinned version). **Always through the media layer**: `image` field → JPEG/PNG/WebP, 5 MB if `mapsTo: photo` else 10 MB, EXIF/GPS stripped, one file per field (a new one replaces the old), non-sensitive; `file` field → PDF/JPEG/PNG up to 10 MB, registered **sensitive** (no signed URL, audited stream only). Magic bytes must match the declared type, max 5 files per submission, **max 10 files and `FORM_DRAFT_MAX_BYTES_PER_USER` (25 MiB) over all open drafts of a user in one club** (`422 QUOTA_EXCEEDED`, `details.code = draft_upload_cap`), club quota applies; the status/version are checked before any bytes are processed, the image processing and file write run outside the transaction that locks the submission (a failed follow-up transaction tombstones the stored file again); stored under `forms/<clubId>/<submissionId>/…` with `owner_type = form_submission`. Alternatively a JSON body `{ fieldKey, mediaKey }` **claims** an unattached image of the same user and club (uploaded via `POST /media`; one claim per upload; only `image` fields). `201 { data: { file } }`; `409 FORM_VERSION_OUTDATED` on a stale draft |
| `/form-submissions/:id/files/:fileId` | DELETE | session (submitter only) | Draft only; removes the row, tombstones the registry entry, deletes the bytes. `204` |
| `/form-submissions/:id/files/:fileId` | GET | session (submitter, or `members:write` of the submission's club for **submitted** applications) | Authenticated stream (`Content-Disposition` inline for images, attachment for documents, `nosniff`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`). Every retrieval is audited: documents `media.sensitive_access`, images `form_submission.file_access` (`accessedBy`, `reason` `form_submission:board` \| `form_submission:submitter`). Everyone else, drafts for the board and other clubs' boards: `404`. A file of a field marked `sensitive` additionally needs `members:read_sensitive` for the board (`403`) |
| `/form-submissions/:id/submit` | POST | session (submitter only; `FORM_SUBMISSIONS_PER_HOUR` per user) | Body `{ answers? }` (merged like PATCH). Validates in submit mode against the pinned version (required fields, hidden fields are dropped and never required, required `file`/`image` fields need an uploaded file, required consent must be `true`); files of fields the answers made invisible are deleted. `draft` → `submitted` and, for an application form, the `club_applications` row is created in the same transaction: `200 { data: { submission, application: { id, status, createdAt } | null, warnings } }` (a child application is additionally checked: first and last name answered, birth date not in the future → `422` with field codes; `warnings` are advice for the board, never blocking). `409 FORM_VERSION_OUTDATED` if the version is no longer published (the client re-pins via PATCH and keeps the answers by key), `ALREADY_MEMBER`/`APPLICATION_PENDING` as above, `submission_not_editable` for a double submit (parallel submits: one wins). Audit `form_submission.submit` (ids, no answers) |
| `/form-submissions/:id/withdraw` | POST | session (submitter only) | `draft`/`submitted` **without `decided_at`** → `withdrawn` (nothing to withdraw once the board decided: `409 submission_not_withdrawable`); the uploaded files are deleted, the answers stay as a record, a linked pending `club_applications` row is closed (`withdrawn`). `409` otherwise, and `409 submission_not_withdrawable` for a submission that belongs to an exit request (use `POST /club-exit-requests/:id/withdraw`) |
| `/departments` | GET, POST, PATCH `/:id`, DELETE `/:id` | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `departments:write`) | Club sub-units (Fußball, Tennis, ...) |
| `/club-info` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Aggregate Vereinsinfo: current board (members holding a board `role_type`; each entry has `photoUrl` = signed URL of the board member's profile photo, but **only** for callers with `members:write` / `members:read_sensitive` and for the person themselves — `null` for everyone else, adult or minor, until the Wave 10 consent management), departments, info pages — one request instead of three. Each board entry carries the derived `termStatus` (`aktiv` / `laeuft_bald_ab` / `abgelaufen`) and `termDaysLeft` (Wave 11 B8) |
| `/club-info/:slug` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | One info page (Satzung/Leitbild/...) |
| `/club-info/:slug` | PUT | session + `clubGuard` (`club_info:write`) | Create-or-update by slug. Body `{ title, contentMarkdown?, externalUrl? }` |
| `/club-info/:slug` | DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (`club_info:write`) | |
| `/calendars` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Every club calendar for a `calendars:write` caller, else only what `src/lib/calendar-visibility.ts` grants. A club with no calendar gets its standard "Vereinskalender" (`isDefault: true`) created on the spot |
| `/calendars` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`calendars:write`) | Body `{ name, departmentId?, isDefault?, icalImportUrl? }`. Setting `isDefault` unsets it on every other club calendar in the same transaction (advisory lock per club) |
| `/calendars/:id` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | 404 unless visible to the caller (write permission or a matching grant) |
| `/calendars/:id` | PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (`calendars:write`) | `409 { details.code: "default_calendar_required" }` when DELETE / `isDefault: false` / `departmentId` hits the default calendar (always) |
| `/calendars/:id/visibility` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`calendars:write`) | Raw `calendar_visibility` rows for the calendar (board-facing config, not a public list) |
| `/calendars/:id/visibility` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`calendars:write`) | Body: exactly one of `{ memberId }` \| `{ roleType }` \| `{ departmentId }` |
| `/calendars/:id/visibility/:visibilityId` | DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (`calendars:write`) | |
| `/events` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Query `from?`/`to?` (ISO dates). Only events on calendars visible to the caller. **Overlap semantics (Wave 12 B3):** an event belongs to the range if `coalesce(ends_at, starts_at) >= from AND starts_at <= to`, so a multi-day event that began before `from` is returned. Every row carries `myRsvp` (`angemeldet`/`warteliste`/`null`), `attendeeCount` and `waitlistCount` (account RSVPs + guardian responses for wards without account), loaded with two grouped queries (no N+1) |
| `/events` | POST, `/events/:id` PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (`calendars:write`) | Body includes `calendarId`/`title`/`startsAt`/`endsAt?`/`category?`/`capacity?`/`allDay?`/`locationId?` (a location of the caller's club, else `404`); moving `calendarId` re-checks it belongs to the caller's club. PATCH additionally accepts `notify?: boolean`; a change of start, end or location of a not cancelled event announces it (`notify: false` suppresses it and writes the audit entry `event.notify_suppressed`). DELETE of an event with RSVPs is announced like a cancellation (`?notify=false` suppresses). Responses carry `cancelled`, `cancelledAt`, `cancelReason` ([details](#event-and-meeting-change-notifications-wave-9-b10)) | `endsAt` before `startsAt` is rejected with `422` (create and update, checked against the stored value on PATCH).
| `/events/:id/cancel` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`calendars:write`) | Cancels instead of deleting. Body `{ reason? (≤ 500), notify? }` → `200 { data: event }` with `cancelled: true`; `409` if already cancelled, `404` for foreign/unknown ids, `403` without `calendars:write`. Tells attendees, waitlist, the calendar's department members and their guardians (class `optional`, category `events`). A cancelled event refuses new RSVPs (`409`) and shows `STATUS:CANCELLED` in `/events.ics`. Audit `event.cancel` |
| `/events/:id/cancel-preview` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`calendars:write`, like the cancellation) | Audience preview before cancelling, **numbers only** (no names, no addresses): `200 { data: { alreadyCancelled, confirmed, waitlisted, departmentCalendar, audience: { total, reachable, inApp, email, emailOnly, unreachable, sharedAddress, tooLarge } } }`. `audience` is the audience `POST /:id/cancel` would address (RSVP `angemeldet` + `warteliste`, the department members of a department calendar, guardians), resolved with the dispatcher's resolver for in-app + mail; `email` is an upper bound (individual mail opt-outs are only known at send time); `tooLarge` is `true` (with `total` = size) when the audience exceeds `AUDIENCE_MAX_RECIPIENTS` and a cancellation would not be sent. Read-only: no job, no audit entry. `403` without `calendars:write`, `404` for unknown or foreign events |
| `/events/:id` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | 404 unless the event's calendar is visible to the caller. Same read shape as the list incl. `myRsvp`/`attendeeCount`/`waitlistCount`/`allDay` |
| `/events/:id/rsvp` | POST | session + `clubGuard` | Self-service RSVP. Row-locks the event (`SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`) before counting confirmed attendees; returns `"angemeldet"` or `"warteliste"` once over capacity |
| `/events/:id/rsvp` | DELETE | session + `clubGuard` | Cancels the caller's own RSVP; same row lock, promotes the earliest-waitlisted member (FIFO) if the caller was confirmed; the promoted member (and a child's guardians) is told after the commit (`event.promoted`, optional, events) |
| `/events.ics` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | iCal feed (`text/calendar`) of events on calendars visible to the caller. Session-gated only — no public/token link yet, see [Key decisions](#key-decisions) |
| `/availability/slots` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` | Caller's own recurring weekly `availability_slots`. Body `{ weekday, startTime, endTime, note? }` |
| `/availability/slots/:id` | PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` | Own row only |
| `/availability/exceptions` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` | Caller's own one-off `availability_exceptions`. Body `{ date, isAvailable, note? }` |
| `/availability/exceptions/:id` | PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` | Own row only |
| `/availability/overlap` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Query `members`/`candidates` (comma-separated ids/ISO timestamps). Raw per-candidate, per-member availability — no auto-ranked suggestion, see [Key decisions](#key-decisions) |
| `/meetings` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `meetings:write`) | Body `{ type: 'vorstandssitzung' \| 'mitgliederversammlung' \| 'sonstige', title, scheduledAt?, agenda? }` (other `type` values → 422). Omitting `scheduledAt` creates it in `"terminfindung"` status. **GET is read-scoped:** `vorstandssitzung` rows only for invitees and callers with `meetings:write`/`meetings:read_board`; **external `guest` accounts read no meetings at all (`404`)** (see [Rights and read scoping](#rights-and-read-scoping-wave-7-b7b8)). Wave 11 B10: POST/PATCH also take `participationMode` (`praesenz` default \| `hybrid` \| `virtual`) and `onlineUrl` (https, max 500, `null` clears); `422 online_not_allowed` (`details.allowed`) unless `onlineAllowed` of the statute parameters permits the mode (`none` = room only, `hybrid` = room + hybrid, `virtual` = all three; an unchanged mode is not re-checked), `422 online_url_requires_mode` for a link on a room meeting. `onlineUrl` is returned only to invitees and to `meetings:write` / `meetings:read_board` / `assembly:manage`, `null` for everybody else (list and detail) |
| `/meetings/:id` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | `404` (never 403) for a `vorstandssitzung` the caller may not read — same for every `/meetings/:id/*` read below. Wave 11 B9: `minutes` is the text of the version the caller may see (managers the latest, everybody else the latest **approved** one, else `null`), plus `minutesStatus`; the legacy column is no longer read or written |
| `/meetings/:id` | PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (`meetings:write`) | DELETE of an open meeting (`terminfindung`/`geplant`, not past) with invitees tells them like a cancellation; `?notify=false` suppresses it (audit `meeting.notify_suppressed`). PATCH also accepts `type`, `title`, `scheduledAt` (nullable), `agenda`, `status`, `minutes`, `notify?: boolean`. A changed `scheduledAt` (or a date set for the first time) and `status: "abgesagt"` (cancellation) are announced to the invitees; `notify: false` suppresses it (audit `meeting.notify_suppressed`). Wave 11 B9: `minutes` in the PATCH body is redirected to the working draft (`PUT /meetings/:id/minutes`; `null` empties it), `409 minutes_locked` once the version is submitted or approved; DELETE of a meeting with approved minutes is `409 minutes_approved`. Review M1: moving `scheduledAt` of an assembly that already has an invitation re-checks the statute period from the day the FIRST invitation went out (rules frozen with it): `422 invitation_period_violated` (with the check, like the invitation), or with body `exceptionReason` and `assembly:manage` the change is saved and appended to `meetings.scheduleExceptions` (`[{ at, from, to, reason, requiredDays, daysAvailable, by }]`, shown to managers on the meeting); clearing the date of an invited assembly is `422 meeting_without_date` |
| `/meetings/:id/invitees` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | `200 { data: [{ id, meetingId, clubMemberId, memberId (account id or null), displayName?, response }] }`. `displayName` only for `meetings:write` / `meetings:read_board`, everybody else who may read the meeting gets ids only |
| `/meetings/:id/invitees` | POST, DELETE `/:clubMemberId` | session + `clubGuard` (`meetings:write`) | POST body `{ clubMemberId, notify?: boolean }` (Wave 11: invitees are `club_members`, so members **without an account** can be invited; the account membership id `memberId` is still accepted as an alias, also in the DELETE path): the invited person gets `meeting.invited` (optional, events) unless `notify:false` or `club_settings.notify_meeting_invites = false`. `404` for an unknown, foreign-club or left member, `409` when already invited. Same table serves the Terminfindung candidate list and post-scheduling zu-/absage tracking |
| `/meetings/:id/invitees/me` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` | Self-service RSVP for the caller's own invitee row. Body `{ response: "zugesagt" \| "abgesagt" }` |
| `/meetings/:id/overlap` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Query `candidates` only — auto-loads the meeting's own invitees instead of a caller-supplied `members` list |
| `/meetings/:id/attendance` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | `200 { data: [{ id, meetingId, clubMemberId (null after erasure), memberId (account id or null), displayName? (managers only), present, hasVotingRight, checkedInAt, leftAt, checkInMethod, proxyFor: clubMemberId[], proxyForMemberId }] }` |
| `/meetings/:id/attendance` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (`meetings:write` or `assembly:manage`) | Body: array (1-500) of `{ clubMemberId, present?, method? (schriftfuehrer\|vollmacht), hasVotingRight?, proxyForClubMemberId? (nullable) }` (`memberId` / `proxyForMemberId` account ids are accepted as aliases). **Partial update:** only the listed members and only the fields sent are written — omitted members/fields keep their stored value. `present: true` is the **check-in** (also for members without account; `checkedInAt`, `checkInMethod` default `schriftfuehrer`, `leftAt` cleared); `present: false` after a check-in is **leaving** (`leftAt`). Whoever gave a proxy cannot be checked in (`409 proxy_granted`, withdraw it first). A new row takes `hasVotingRight` from the frozen voting roll, else from the live derivation (not a blanket `true`); `hasVotingRight` in the body is accepted only while the roll is not frozen (`422 use_voting_roll_override` afterwards). `proxyForClubMemberId` records a proxy (holder = the entry's member) by the same statute rules as `POST /proxies`, `null` clears the proxies the person holds. The proxy-giver must belong to the same club and differ from the attendee (`422`/`404`); the whole batch is validated before anything is written and runs in one transaction. One audit entry for the whole batch, not per row |
| `/meetings/:id/agenda-items` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Structured agenda (Wave 11 B3), ordered: `{ id, meetingId, position, title, description, voteKind (none\|resolution\|election), topic (key of `majorityByTopic`), announced, announcedInInvitationId }`. Callers with `assembly:manage` / `meetings:write` see the draft, everybody else only the items an invitation announced |
| `/meetings/:id/agenda-items` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage` or `meetings:write`) | Body `{ title (≤300), description?, voteKind?, topic?, position? }`; `voteKind: election` defaults `topic` to `wahl`; `position` inserts and renumbers (1..n). `201` |
| `/meetings/:id/agenda-items/:itemId` | PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage` or `meetings:write`) | PATCH any of the POST fields (`position` moves the item). **An item announced in an invitation is fixed** (`409 agenda_item_announced`): changes go through a Nachtrag (add new items, send again). DELETE `204` renumbers |
| `/meetings/:id/invitations/preview` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | `200 { data: { kind (einladung\|nachtrag), period, missingRules, agendaCount, newAgendaCount, agendaRequiredButEmpty, recipients: { total, email, inapp, post, sharedAddress } } }`. `period` = `checkInvitationPeriod` for sending **now**: `{ ok, reason (ok\|too_late\|no_date\|rules_missing), minDays, bufferDays, requiredDays, sendDay, meetingDay, daysAvailable, earliestMeetingDay, latestSendDay, timezone }` (calendar days in the club time zone, default Europe/Berlin) — the live "earliest valid date" of the planning wizard |
| `/meetings/:id/invitations` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`; 20/min per user) | Sends the invitation, or a **Nachtrag** when one was already sent (version +1; needs at least one new agenda item, `422 nothing_to_add`). Body `{ message?, exceptionReason? (5-1000 chars), audience? (`all_members` default \| `voting_members`: only members with a voting right on the meeting day) }` (the preview takes `?audience=` too). `201 { data: { id, version, kind, sentAt, content, contentHash, sentBy, recipientCount, periodCheck, rulesSnapshot, exceptionReason, deliveries, summary } }`. Refusals are `422` with `error.details.code`: `assembly_rules_incomplete` (+ `missing`), `invitation_period_violated` (+ the whole period check incl. `earliestMeetingDay` / `latestSendDay`; sendable only with `exceptionReason`, which is stored and flagged for the minutes), `agenda_required`, `meeting_without_date`, `meeting_not_planned`, `invitation_not_supported` (only type `mitgliederversammlung`). Recipients (`all_members`): every member who is not left and not `interessent` (also members without account); channels per member `inapp` (account), `email` (account e-mail, else contact e-mail; a shared address is mailed once), `post` (neither account nor address; or everybody when the statute form is `schriftlich`). All recipients become invitees (`ausstehend`) so RSVP and the Wave 9 RSVP reminder ("Erinnerung an Nicht-Antwortende") work. The first invitation stores the rules on `meetings.rules_snapshot` |
| `/meetings/:id/invitations` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | All versions with `summary` (`[{ channel, status, count }]`) |
| `/meetings/:id/invitations/latest` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | The newest invitation as the members received it: `{ id, version, kind, sentAt, content, contentHash }` (no recipients, no exception reason); `404` before the first one |
| `/meetings/:id/invitations/:invId` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | **Proof of delivery:** the invitation, `summary` and `deliveries: [{ id, clubMemberId, displayName, channel, status, errorCode, statusAt, postedAt }]`; query `channel`, `status` (`queued\|sent\|failed\|bounced\|manual_posted`), `limit` (≤1000, default 200), `offset`. Status is derived from the dispatcher when read (`queued` → `sent`/`failed`/`bounced`); a mail that failed puts the member (and the holders of a shared address) on the post list. One failing recipient never affects the others |
| `/meetings/:id/invitations/:invId/post-list.csv` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`members:read_sensitive`) | Excel-DE CSV (BOM, `;`) of the members on the post list: name, street, postal code, city, reason, status; `?status=all` includes posted ones. Cells starting with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@` are neutralised; `Cache-Control: private, no-store`; audited as `meeting_invitation.export` (row count only) |
| `/meetings/:id/invitations/:invId/deliveries/mark-posted` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Body `{ clubMemberIds? }` (default all queued post rows) → `manual_posted` with `postedAt`. `200 { data: { marked, summary } }`; audited |
| `/meetings/:id/voting-roll` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage` only — review M7: the roll shows the reason why somebody may not vote) | `200 { data: { frozen, frozenAt, asOf, eligibleTotal, entries: [{ clubMemberId, displayName, eligible, basis, overrideReason }] } }`; query `eligible=true\|false`. Frozen: the stored roll; otherwise the live derivation for the reference day (`asOf` = the meeting day in club time, else today). `basis` = `ok`, `rules_missing`, `not_yet_member`, `left`, `status`, `no_category`, `category`, `birth_date_unknown`, `min_age`, `override` |
| `/meetings/:id/voting-roll/me` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | The caller's own entry: `{ frozen, asOf, eligible, basis }` ("am I allowed to vote, and why not") |
| `/meetings/:id/voting-roll/freeze` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Freezes the **voting roll** once (`201 { data: { frozen, total, eligible, asOf } }`, `409 voting_roll_frozen` afterwards, `422 assembly_rules_incomplete` while `votingStatuses` / `votingMinAge` are not stated). Everybody who has not left before the reference day is stored with the basis; existing attendance rows take the roll's decision |
| `/meetings/:id/voting-roll/:clubMemberId` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Manual override of a frozen entry. Body `{ eligible, reason (5-500) }`; `409 voting_roll_not_frozen` before the freeze. The reason is stored on the roll row and never in the audit trail (`meeting_voting_roll.override` carries ids and the new value) |
| `/meetings/:id/proxies` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Proxies `[{ id, grantorClubMemberId, grantorName, holderClubMemberId, holderName, createdAt }]`: the board sees all, a member only the proxies they gave or hold. Review M7: the full list with names and `proofSource` needs `assembly:manage` (`meetings:write` alone sees only its own proxies). Minutes drafts, versions and names in the export stay readable with `meetings:write` on purpose: Wave 2 let those roles write the minutes, and they cannot work without reading them; today only roles that also hold `assembly:manage` have `meetings:write` |
| `/meetings/:id/proxies` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (self-service; `assembly:manage` for somebody else) | Body `{ holderClubMemberId, grantorClubMemberId? }`; without a giver the caller gives their own proxy. Only for type `mitgliederversammlung` (`422 proxy_not_supported`). Statute rules (`422`/`409` with `error.details.code`): `assembly_rules_incomplete` (`proxyAllowed` / `maxProxiesPerPerson` not stated), `proxy_not_allowed`, `proxy_self`, `grantor_not_eligible`, `holder_not_eligible` (voting right per roll / derivation), `grantor_present` (409), `holder_is_grantor` / `grantor_is_holder` (409, no chains), `max_proxies_reached` (409, `max`), `meeting_closed` (409, members only: the board can still record proxies afterwards). A new holder of the same giver replaces the old one. `201 { data: { grantorClubMemberId, holderClubMemberId } }`. Review M9: when the board gives a proxy for somebody else, `proofSource` (1-500, e.g. "Vollmacht per E-Mail vom 3.3.") is mandatory (`422 proof_required`), stored on the proxy, returned to `assembly:manage` and audited; `409 secret_ballot_open` while a secret ballot of the meeting is open. The same applies to `proxyForClubMemberId` / `proxyForMemberId` in `PATCH /meetings/:id/attendance`, which then needs `proxyProofSource` |
| `/meetings/:id/proxies/:grantorClubMemberId` | DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (self-service for the own proxy; `assembly:manage` for others) | Withdraws a proxy (`204`, `404` if there is none, `403` for somebody else's without `assembly:manage`) |
