| `/meetings/:id/quorum` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Live quorum, aggregated (no names): `{ rollFrozen, asOf, eligibleTotal, presentEligible, proxiesRepresented, represented, quorumMode, quorumValue, required, result (beschlussfaehig\|nicht_beschlussfaehig\|regeln_fehlen) }`. `represented` = present voting members + proxies whose holder is present and whose giver is absent (voting right of both required). Quorum `none` is always `beschlussfaehig`; a fraction is rounded up; nobody entitled or present is never a quorum. The rules of the first invitation (`meetings.rules_snapshot`) apply once there is one. B5 recomputes it whenever a vote is opened (`computeQuorum`) |
| `/meetings/:id/online/join` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (invited accounts only, `404` otherwise; 30/min) | Wave 11 B10, self check-in of an account into the online part: `200 { data: { present, checkedInAt, leftAt } }`, attendance row with `checkInMethod: self_online` and the voting right of the roll. Idempotent; a rejoin after leaving keeps the first `checkedInAt`. `409` `not_online` (room meeting), `online_not_allowed`, `outside_check_in_window` (from 1 h before the start to 12 h after it), `proxy_granted` (withdraw the proxy first). Members without an account are checked in by the secretary (`PATCH /meetings/:id/attendance`) |
| `/meetings/:id/online/leave` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (invited accounts only) | Ends the own online check-in (`present: false`, `leftAt`); `409 not_self_online` for a check-in the secretary made |
| `/meetings/:id/minutes` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (read scoping as the meeting: `404` for a board meeting the caller may not read) | Wave 11 B9. `200 { data: { current, versions, amendments, approvalHint } }`. `current` = `{ id, meetingId, version, status (entwurf\|zur_genehmigung\|genehmigt\|archiviert), approved, content, contentHash, approvedInMeetingId, approvedResolutionId, approvedAt, createdAt, updatedAt, confirmations? }` — managers (`meetings:write` / `assembly:manage`) get the latest version incl. `confirmations [{ role, confirmedAt, matchesText }]`, everybody else only the latest **approved** one (`null` before). `versions` (managers only) lists every version; `approved: false` + `archiviert` = superseded, never approved. `amendments [{ id, version, note, createdAt }]`. `approvalHint` (managers, unapproved) = the next meeting of the same type `{ id, title, scheduledAt }` where the item "Genehmigung des Protokolls" belongs |
| `/meetings/:id/minutes` | PUT | session + `clubGuard` (`meetings:write` or `assembly:manage`) | Body `{ content }` (max 200000 chars, no control characters). Creates version 1 or edits the draft in place and drops the confirmations; `409 minutes_locked` (`details.status`) when the latest version is not an `entwurf` (also a decision record of a circular resolution) |
| `/meetings/:id/minutes/confirm` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`, and the role of the confirmation: `leitung` = `vorsitz` or `stellv_vorsitz`, `schriftfuehrung` = `schriftfuehrer`, or the chair/deputy if the club has no `schriftfuehrer` at all; else `403 role_required`) | Body `{ role: leitung\|schriftfuehrung }`: text form confirmation of the current draft (no qualified signature). `409 minutes_not_draft`, `minutes_already_confirmed`, `minutes_two_persons` (the two roles need two accounts); `422 minutes_empty`. Returns the full view as GET |
| `/meetings/:id/minutes/submit` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`meetings:write` or `assembly:manage`) | `entwurf` → `zur_genehmigung`; `409 minutes_not_confirmed` (`details.missing`) until both roles confirmed this text. Review M4: submitting an assembly's minutes freezes the numbers in `meeting_minutes.attendance_snapshot` (present, present with voting right, proxies, eligible, quorum rule and result — counts, no names); the export and the approved record report these, so a later erasure of a member, a changed roll or statute never alters them (protected by the immutability trigger once approved). Drafts report live numbers |
| `/meetings/:id/minutes/reopen` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`meetings:write` or `assembly:manage`) | The approval did not happen: the submitted version becomes `archiviert` (not approved) and version n+1 starts as a draft with the same text; `409 minutes_not_submitted` |
| `/meetings/:id/minutes/approve` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Body `{ resolutionId }`: `zur_genehmigung` → `genehmigt`, **only** through an accepted resolution (`result` starts with `angenommen`) of a LATER meeting (`scheduledAt`) of the same type: the approving meeting must be held (`abgehalten` / `protokolliert`, else `422 approval_meeting_not_held`) and a digital resolution (`source: digital`) needs its finalized ballot (`422 resolution_ballot_missing`); `422 approval_meeting_not_later`, `approval_meeting_type_mismatch`, `resolution_not_accepted`; `404` unknown resolution; `409 minutes_not_submitted`. From here on the version is immutable (app check and DB trigger) |
| `/meetings/:id/minutes/archive` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | `genehmigt` → `archiviert` (`409 minutes_not_approved`) |
| `/meetings/:id/minutes/amendments` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Body `{ note }` (1-2000): Berichtigungsvermerk on the latest approved version (`201 { id, version, note, createdAt }`, `409 minutes_not_approved`); the text is never changed, notes are append-only (DB trigger) |
| `/meetings/:id/minutes/export` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (20/min) | Query `format=html\|md` (default `html`, else `422`; PDF follows with Wave 13). A document of the version the caller sees (`404` without one): invitation proof (date, recipients, period check and exception reason, delivery summary, date changes after the invitation with their reasons), attendance and quorum (the frozen snapshot of a submitted version; names of the present only for managers; erased persons as "(pseudonymisiert)"), resolutions with results, the text, Berichtigungsvermerke and the SHA-256. HTML is escaped, no scripts (`Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'`), headers `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`; audited (`meeting_minutes.export`) |
| `/ballots` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (external guests `404`) | `200 { data: Ballot[] }` (Wave 11 B5), newest first; query `status`, `mode` (`in_session`\|`circular`), `meetingId`, `electionId`, `limit` (≤200), `offset`. `assembly:manage` sees all, a member everything but drafts of the general assembly plus the circular resolutions they are a voter of. A ballot whose window ended is closed on read. `Ballot` = `{ id, mode, kind (resolution\|election), meetingId, agendaItemId, electionId, round, title, description, secret, topic, majority, maxChoices, audience, status, phase (draft\|scheduled\|voting\|ended\|cancelled), opensAt, closesAt, closedAt, options: [{ id, position, label, kind (yes\|no\|abstain\|choice\|candidate), candidateClubMemberId }], result, resultHash, finalizedAt, createdAt }`; `result` only when `finalized` (managers: from `counted`) |
| `/ballots` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Creates a draft. Body `{ mode? (`in_session` default\|`circular`), meetingId (required in session: a `mitgliederversammlung`; not allowed for circular), agendaItemId?, title, description?, secret? (default false; `422 secret_ballot_not_allowed` unless `secretBallotDigitalAllowed`), topic? (key of `majorityByTopic`, default `allgemein`), maxChoices? (1-20), choices? (2-20 free options instead of Ja/Nein), allowAbstain? (default true), audience? (circular: `all_voting_members` default\|`board`), closesAt? }`. Default options Ja / Nein / Enthaltung. `422 circular_not_allowed` while `circularAllowed` is off. `201` with the detail shape. Review M2: `topic` is optional and, for a ballot in a session, derived from the agenda item (`agendaItemId`): a different value is `422 topic_mismatch` (`details.agendaItemTopic`), a topic other than `allgemein` without an agenda item is `422 topic_needs_agenda_item` (PATCH of `topic`/`agendaItemId` follows the same rule). Opening needs an ANNOUNCED agenda item for every topic other than `allgemein` (`422 agenda_item_not_announced`, unless the statute says `agendaRequiredInInvitation = false`) and re-checks that the item still has the ballot's topic (`409 topic_mismatch`). A circular resolution has no agenda and keeps the requested topic |
| `/ballots/:id` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | Detail: the `Ballot` plus `meeting: { id, title, scheduledAt }`, `me: { eligible, hasVoted, canVote, checkedIn (session; null for circular), ownVoteOptionIds (open ballot only, null when secret), proxies: [{ grantorClubMemberId, displayName, hasVoted }] }` and, for `assembly:manage`, `participation: { eligible, voted }` (aggregated, also for a secret ballot) and `opening` (quorum and eligible count at opening, warnings). `404` for drafts (members), foreign clubs and circular resolutions the caller does not vote on |
| `/ballots/:id` | PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Draft only (`409 ballot_not_draft`): PATCH any create field except `mode`/`meetingId`; DELETE `204` |
| `/ballots/:id/open` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Body `{ opensAt?, closesAt? }` (default: now; a future `opensAt` makes the phase `scheduled`; a circular resolution needs `closesAt`, `422 closes_at_required`). Freezes the statute rules and the majority on the ballot, **checks the quorum again** (session; `409 not_quorate` with the quorum numbers, `422 assembly_rules_incomplete` + `missing` for incomplete parameters), freezes the meeting's voting roll if it is not frozen yet, fixes the voter roll (session: the roll's eligible members; circular: members with a voting right today or the board accounts; `409 no_eligible_voters`). A resolution on an agenda item that no invitation announced is only warned about (`warnings: ["agenda_item_not_announced"]`, shown in the result and the resolution text); satzungsaenderung / zweckaenderung / aufloesung and elections are blocked (`422 agenda_item_not_announced`) unless `agendaRequiredInInvitation` is false. `200` with the detail shape + `warnings`. Review M1: for a ballot in a session the opening records the invitation the assembly was called with in `warnings` (`no_invitation`, `invitation_period_violated`, `invitation_period_exception`, `date_changed_after_invitation`); they are warnings, not a block, and stay in `opening` |
| `/ballots/:id/vote` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (any member with an account; 100/min per user) | Body `{ optionIds, forClubMemberId? }` (yes/no ballot: exactly one option; free options: 1..`maxChoices`; an abstention is exclusive). `forClubMemberId` = the proxy holder votes for a grantor (session only, holder and proxy as in `/meetings/:id/proxies`). One transaction: conditional `UPDATE ballot_voters SET has_voted` (window checked with the database clock) and the insert into `ballot_votes_open` or `ballot_votes_secret`. `201 { data: { ballotId, voted: true, forClubMemberId } }` (the choice is never echoed). Errors with `error.details.code`: `403 not_eligible`, `403 not_checked_in`, `403 no_proxy`, `403 proxy_not_allowed` (circular), `409 already_voted`, `409 ballot_closed`, `409 ballot_not_started`, `409 grantor_present`, `422 invalid_selection` / `unknown_option` / `duplicate_option` (nothing is consumed). No audit entry per vote |
| `/ballots/:id/close` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Ends the voting now and counts: `200` detail with `status: counted` and the preliminary `result` (`409 ballot_not_open`) |
| `/ballots/:id/count` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Counts a closed ballot (also one whose window just ended; `409 ballot_not_closed`). `result` for a yes/no ballot: `{ form: "yes_no", outcome (angenommen\|abgelehnt\|nicht_beschlussfaehig), reason (ok\|quorum\|participation\|unanimity\|rules_missing), yes, no, abstain, participants, eligible, majority, abstainCountsAs, tallies: [{ optionId, label, kind, votes }], quorum, flags: { unannouncedAgendaItem }, final: false }`; for free options / election rounds `{ form: "choice", outcome (entschieden\|gleichstand\|kein_ergebnis\|nicht_beschlussfaehig), seats, electedOptionIds, tiedOptionIds, openSeats, qualifiedOptionIds, … }` |
| `/ballots/:id/finalize` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Freezes the result (`final: true`, `resultHash` = SHA-256 of the canonical result) and writes the resolution into the meeting (`meeting_resolutions` with `ballot_id`, `source: digital`); a circular resolution additionally gets a meeting of its own (type `vorstandssitzung` for the board, else `sonstige`; status `protokolliert`) and its decision record as `meeting_minutes` version 1 (status `archiviert`). `409 ballot_not_counted` |
| `/ballots/:id/cancel` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Body `{ reason? }` (1-1000). Cancels a **draft**, or an **open** ballot — then `reason` is mandatory (`422 reason_required`), kept in `ballots.cancel_reason` and in the audit entry `ballot.cancel`. A ballot whose voting is over (`closed` / `counted`) cannot be cancelled: `409 ballot_not_cancellable` (`details.status`), the result is finalized instead; `409 ballot_final` once finalized or cancelled. An election round that was cancelled while open or draft is replaced by a new round (`POST /elections/:id/rounds`); a finalized round is decided, never discarded |
| `/ballots/:id/votes` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | After the voting ended (`409 ballot_not_ended` before): `{ secret, votes }`. Open ballot: `[{ optionId, clubMemberId, displayName }]`; secret ballot: `[{ optionId }]` only, in random order on every call |
| `/elections` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` (GET: members, guests `404`; POST: `assembly:manage`) | Wave 11 B7. POST body `{ meetingId? (a general assembly), roleType (assignable role except `erziehungsberechtigt`), departmentId? (required for `abteilungsleitung`/`trainer`, forbidden otherwise), seats (1-20), termMonths? (1-120), electionLeadClubMemberId? }` → `201` detail. GET list `[{ id, meetingId, roleType, departmentId, seats, termMonths, electionLeadClubMemberId, status (planned\|running\|decided\|closed), decidedAt, createdAt }]` |
| `/elections/:id` | GET, PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (PATCH: `assembly:manage`) | Detail = list item + `electionLeadName`, `openSeats`, `candidates: [{ id, clubMemberId, displayName, status (proposed\|willing\|declined\|withdrawn\|elected\|accepted\|pending_account), (board: proposedBy, hasAccount) }]` (members see only who stands — willing or elected — and their own candidacy; the board sees all), `rounds: [{ ballotId, round, status, secret, outcome }]`, `me: { candidateId, status } \| null`, board: `decision` (per round: elected candidates, lot with note). PATCH `{ meetingId?, seats?, termMonths?, electionLeadClubMemberId? }` (`409 lead_is_candidate`, `409 seats_below_elected`) |
| `/elections/:id/candidates` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Body `{ clubMemberId, willing? }` records a proposal (or a candidacy declared in the assembly with `willing: true`). `409 lead_is_candidate` (the election lead cannot stand), `409 already_candidate`, `409 election_filled`; `404` for a member of another club. `201` with the election detail |
| `/elections/:id/candidates/:candidateId` | PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (PATCH: the candidate themself or `assembly:manage`; DELETE: `assembly:manage`) | PATCH `{ status: willing\|declined\|withdrawn }` (`409 invalid_candidate_transition`, `409 round_running` while the candidate is on an unfinished round; another member `403`); only the board re-activates a declined or withdrawn candidate. DELETE `204` removes a candidacy that is not elected and never stood in a round |
| `/elections/:id/rounds` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Sets up the next Wahlgang as a **draft ballot** (`kind: election`, options = the willing candidates + Enthaltung, `maxChoices` = the open seats): body `{ candidateIds? (narrow a Stichwahl), secret? (needs `secretBallotDigitalAllowed`), majority? (this round's majority kind, default the statute's `wahl` majority), agendaItemId? }` → `201 { data: { ballotId, round } }`. Then open / vote / close / finalize through `/ballots/:id/...`. `409 round_pending`, `409 round_not_decided`, `409 election_filled`, `422 meeting_required` / `no_candidates` / `candidate_not_willing` |
| `/elections/:id/decide` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`assembly:manage`) | Body `{ lot?: { candidateIds, note (5-1000) } }`. Turns the latest **finalized** round into elected candidates and writes the resolution into the meeting (`gewählt: …` or `kein Ergebnis`; the next round may follow when nobody won). A tie at the last seat is never decided silently: `409 tie_needs_lot` (+ `tiedCandidateIds`, `openSeats`) until the board names exactly the open seats among the tied candidates with a note on how the lot was drawn (`422 invalid_lot`); the note is stored in `elections.decision`. `200 { data: { round, electedCandidateIds, filled, election } }` |
| `/elections/:id/candidates/:candidateId/accept` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (the elected person, or `assembly:manage` on their behalf) | Body `{ accept? (default true) }` for an `elected` candidate (`409 not_elected` otherwise). Accepting with an account writes `club_roles` (upsert, `term_ends_at` = today + `termMonths`) and the history `club_role_terms` (source = the election) at once → `accepted`; without an account → `pending_account` and the role follows when the account is linked (invitation accepted). Declining sets `declined` and reopens the seat. When all seats are elected and everybody has answered (nobody `pending_account`), the previous holders of the office lose the role and their open term ends today, and the election becomes `closed`. `200 { data: { candidateId, status, election } }` |
| `/meetings/:id/resolutions` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `meetings:write`) | Body `{ description, votesFor, votesAgainst, votesAbstain, result }` (Beschluss); vote counts are integers `0..100000` (`422` beyond) |
| `/meetings/:id/resolutions/:rid` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (`meetings:write`) | **Correction instead of silent overwrite:** body = any of the resolution fields **plus a mandatory `correctionNote`** (Berichtigungsvermerk, `422` without it or without a changed field; vote counts `0..100000`). Sets `correction_note`, `corrected_at`, `corrected_by`; audit `meeting_resolution.correct` stores the changed field names only (no previous values and no note: club-scoped audit viewers must not read resolution content; the note stays on the resolution row). Resolutions cannot be deleted |
| `/internal/stats` | GET | `INTERNAL_STATS_TOKEN` bearer token | Aggregate counts only (total users, new users last 7 days, active sessions, `audit_log` event-type breakdown) — never raw rows. Not gated by a Better Auth session; see [Security](#security) for why. Returns 503 if `INTERNAL_STATS_TOKEN` is unset (fails closed) |
| `/admin/activity-stats` | GET | session + `adminGuard` | Query: `interval` (`day`\|`week`), `period` (`7d`\|`30d`\|`90d`). Per-bucket New/Active/Retained/Reactivated user counts plus period-over-period `changePercent`, for `_template_better-auth-admin`'s dashboard. "Active" = had a session created in the window (a login proxy, not request-level activity — this backend has none). Classification logic is a pure function in `src/lib/activity-stats.ts`, unit-tested separately from the DB query in `src/routes/admin-stats.ts` |
| `/admin/club-stats` | GET | session + `adminGuard` | `200 { clubs: { total }, registrations: { pending } }` — number of all clubs (rows in `organization`) and of club registrations with status `pending` (one COUNT query, so the admin navbar badge needn't load the review queue). Dashboard KPI of the admin panel (`src/routes/admin-clubs.ts`, tested in `admin-clubs.test.ts`) |
| `/admin/user-clubs` | GET | session + `adminGuard` | Query `userIds=<id1,id2,…>` (comma-separated, trimmed, de-duplicated, empty entries dropped; none left or more than 100 → 422). `200 { data: { "<userId>": [{ clubId, name, slug }] } }` via `member`→`organization`; every requested id is a key (empty array without membership), clubs per user sorted by name |
| `/admin/send-verification-email` | POST | session + `adminGuard` | Body: `{ userId, callbackURL? }`. Re-enters Better Auth server-side (no session) to send a verification e-mail for a **different** user — the client-side `sendVerificationEmail` endpoint requires the signed-in session's email to match the target (`EMAIL_MISMATCH`), so an admin can never use it for anyone else; the server-side anonymous path has no such check. 404 if the user doesn't exist, 409 if already verified. Backs the admin dashboard's "Resend verification email" button (`src/routes/admin-emails.ts`, integration-tested in `src/routes/admin-emails.test.ts`) |
| `/admin/users/:id` | DELETE | session + `adminGuard` | Wave 10 H2. Deletes an account through the erasure process: the person is first erased/anonymised from every club (same blockers as the self-service deletion: `legal_hold`, `exit_pending`, `open_loans`, `board_role`, `sole_owner` → `409` with `details.code`, nothing changes), then the user row goes. `422` for the caller's own id, `404` for an unknown id. Better Auth's `/api/auth/admin/remove-user` is disabled (`disabledPaths`) because it bypasses `deleteUser.beforeDelete`; audit `admin.user_remove` |
| `/notifications` | GET | session | Own visible notifications (broadcast + targeted, minus own soft-deletes). Query: `filter` (`unread`\|`read`, omit for all) |
| `/notifications/unread-count` | GET | session | `{ count }` for the bell badge |
| `/notifications/:id/read` \| `/unread` | POST | session | Upserts the caller's `notification_state` row. 404 if the notification isn't visible to the caller |
| `/notifications/:id` | DELETE | session | Soft-delete (own `notification_state` row only). 403 if `deletable` is false, 404 if not visible to the caller |
| `/admin/jobs/stats` | GET | session + `adminGuard` | Queue overview (Wave 9 A1): `200 { data: { generatedAt, totals: { queued, running, done, failed, dead }, types: [{ type, queued, running, done, failed, dead, total }] (by type), oldestDue: [{ id, type, status: queued\|failed, runAt, overdueSeconds, attempts, maxAttempts, lastError, ... }] (jobs whose `run_at` has passed, oldest first, max 20), dead: [...] (latest 20 dead jobs, same shape) } }`. No payloads; `lastError` is single line, max 300 chars, with e-mail addresses and push tokens replaced by `[address]` / `[push-token]`. `Cache-Control: private, no-store`; 60/min. A signed-in non-admin gets `401 Admin privileges required` like all admin routes |
| `/admin/jobs/:id/retry` | POST | session + `adminGuard` | Re-schedules a **dead** job (`status = queued`, `attempts = 0`, due now, error and lock cleared) → `200 { data: { id, type, status: "queued" } }`; audit `admin.job_retry` (job type and actor). `409` if the job is not dead (`details.status`), `404` for an unknown or malformed id |
| `/admin/notifications` | GET | session + `adminGuard` | All admin-authored notifications (not system ones), newest first. Each row includes `targetUserEmail` (joined for display, `null` for a broadcast) |
| `/admin/notifications` | POST | session + `adminGuard` | Body: `{ targetUserId?, translations, deletable }` where `translations` is `Record<langCode, {title, body}>` with `de`/`en` required. Omit `targetUserId` for a broadcast |
| `/admin/notifications/:id` | DELETE | session + `adminGuard` | Hard delete — cascades `notification_state` rows via FK |
| `/admin/notification-templates` | GET | session + `adminGuard` | One row per known `translation_key` (`NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATE_KEYS`), `translations` `null` when there's no override yet |
| `/admin/notification-templates/:key` | PATCH | session + `adminGuard` | Body: `{ translations }` (`Record<langCode, {title, body}>`, `de`/`en` required, other supported languages optional). Upserts the override. 404 if `key` isn't in `NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATE_KEYS` |
| `/admin/notification-templates/:key` | DELETE | session + `adminGuard` | Reverts to no-override (deletes the row, idempotent). 404 if `key` isn't in `NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATE_KEYS` |
| `/locations` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `locations:write`) | Any member reads; body `{ name, address?, latitude?, longitude?, openingHours?, photoKey?, contactPerson?, accessNote? }`. `photoKey` is a key from `POST /media` (claimed exactly once; foreign/invented keys `422`); responses carry `photoUrl` (short-lived signed URL or `null`), never the key; the removed free-text `photoUrl` input is a `422`. **`accessNote` (Zutrittsinfo) is `null` and `accessRestricted: true` without `locations:write`/`locations:read_access`** (list and detail) |
| `/locations/:id` | GET, PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `locations:write`) | PATCH `photoKey` replaces (old file deleted) or `null` removes the photo; DELETE removes the photo file too. Detail includes `keyHolders` (entries `{ id, locationId, memberId, keyLabel, memberName }`; `memberName` = linked club member's display name, also for departed members, else the newest key-history snapshot name, else `null`; key holders = access info: `[]` + `accessRestricted: true` without `locations:write`/`locations:read_access`) |
| `/locations/:id/key-holders` | POST, DELETE `/:memberId` | session + `clubGuard` (`locations:write`) | POST body `{ memberId, keyLabel? (≤120, "Schlüssel Nr. 3"), note? (≤500) }`; 409 on a duplicate `(locationId, memberId)`. DELETE takes an optional JSON body `{ note? }` (return note, body capped at 4 KiB; no body is fine, malformed or oversized → 422). Both write a `location_key_history` entry in the same transaction as the change (B14); the `audit_log` entry carries ids only, never names or notes. POST and DELETE tell the key holder (`key.issued` / `key.returned`, optional) unless the holder did it themself or `club_settings.notify_handover` is off |
| `/locations/:id/key-history` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`locations:write` or `locations:read_access`; external guests and plain members `403`) | Key handover log, newest first: `?memberId=` `&page=1&pageSize=50` (max 200) → `{ data: [{ id, action: 'issued'\|'returned', memberId (null once the account is gone), memberName (snapshot), keyLabel, note, recordedBy, recordedByName, createdAt }], page, pageSize, total }`. Same access rule as the key-holder list (it is access info); the return checklist of the exit workflow still reads the live holder list. The snapshots (`member_name`, `recorded_by_name`) are personal data and belong to the Wave 10 deletion concept. Removing an account (`POST /club-members/:id/unlink`, Better Auth `removeMember` via a `beforeRemoveMember` hook) writes a `returned` entry with the note "Konto entfernt" for every key it held; `/leave` and deleting the user fire no hook, there the cascade removes the holder row **without** a `returned` entry (open point). Existing holders were backfilled with one `issued` entry (note "Bestand bei Einführung der Schlüsselhistorie", date = migration time) |
| `/locations/:id/wifi` | GET, POST, PATCH `/:wifiId`, DELETE `/:wifiId` | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `locations:write`) | GET filters by `visibility` (`guest` → `gast`; member → `gast`+`mitglied`; `locations:write`/`locations:read_access` → all). Body `{ label, ssid, password?, security? ('open'\|'wpa'\|'wep'\|'wpa3'), visibility? ('gast'\|'mitglied'\|'vorstand', default mitglied), visibleToGuests? (legacy) }`; responses carry `visibility` + derived `visibleToGuests`, `security` and `qrPayload` (the `WIFI:T:WPA\|WEP\|SAE\|nopass;S:…;P:…;;` string for the QR code, special characters escaped, all-hex SSIDs/passwords quoted, WPA3 as `SAE`; built in `src/lib/wifi-qr.ts`). **Security rules (B14):** without `security` an empty/missing password means `open` and anything else `wpa` (legacy clients keep working); `open` never stores a password; every other type requires one (`422`); PATCH keeps type and password consistent (emptying the password of a network without explicit type makes it `open`, setting one on an open network makes it `wpa`). Migration `0021` set existing password-less networks to `open`, all others to `wpa`. Passwords are never written to `audit_log` |
| `/locations/:id/links` | GET, POST, PATCH `/:linkId`, DELETE `/:linkId` | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `locations:write`) | Same visibility filter as `/wifi`. Body `{ title, url, icon?, visibility?, visibleToGuests? }` |
| `/inventory-items` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `inventory:write`) | Any member reads; query `locationId?`/`category?` filters. Body `{ name, category?, condition, locationId?, acquisitionValueCents?, acquiredAt?, maintenanceIntervalDays?, lastMaintenanceAt? }`. Responses include derived `maintenanceDue`/`maintenanceDueAt` (see [Data model](#data-model)) |
| `/inventory-items/:id` | GET, PATCH, DELETE | session + `clubGuard` (write needs `inventory:write`) | |
| `/inventory-items/:id/loans` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` | Self-service: POST always borrows for the caller (`memberId` = caller's own membership). Body `{ dueAt? }`. **`403` when the club switched self-service borrowing off (`club_settings.allow_self_loans = false`, default `true`); `inventory:write` holders are exempt.** Responses' `status` is the live-derived value (`"ueberfaellig"` overrides the stored value once past due) |
| `/inventory-items/:id/loans/:loanId` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` | Marks returned (no body or `{}`). Allowed for the borrower themself or a caller with `inventory:write`. **Extension:** body `{ dueAt }` (ISO date-time with offset, strictly later than the current return date and in the future; other keys → `422`) needs `inventory:write` (`403` for the borrower), audit `inventory_loan.extend`; body over 1 KiB (checked by `bodyLimit` on `Content-Length` and while streaming, before the body is read) or malformed → `422`. `409` if already returned. Both actions tell the borrower unless they did it themself, see [Notification catalog](#notification-catalog) H |
| `/inventory-items/:id/damage-reports` | GET, POST | session + `clubGuard` | Self-service: POST always reports as the caller. Body `{ description, photoKey? }`: `photoKey` must be an unattached image the caller uploaded to this club via `POST /media` (claimed once in the same transaction, else `422` and no report). Responses carry `photoUrl` = short-lived signed URL (or `null`), never the key |
| `/inventory-items/:id/damage-reports/:reportId` | PATCH | session + `clubGuard` (`inventory:write`) | Body `{ status }`. `resolvedAt` auto-set when `status` becomes `"behoben"`, cleared otherwise |
| `/media` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (per-user limit 30/min) | Multipart upload, field `file`. JPEG/PNG/WebP, 10 MB, magic bytes + declared type, metadata stripped, club quota. `201 { data: { key, url, mimeType, sizeBytes } }` (`url` = signed preview URL); the file is registered `unattached` and claimed once by a feature route (`photoKey`). `422` for wrong/corrupt/oversized/empty files and `QUOTA_EXCEEDED` (`error.details.code` = `club_quota` for the club storage quota, `unattached_cap` for the per-user cap on unclaimed uploads) |
| `/media/<key>?exp=&sig=` | GET | **none** (signed URL) | Serves a registered, live, non-sensitive file after verifying the HMAC signature (`403` missing/wrong/expired/tampered — also for unknown keys, so keys cannot be probed; `404` valid signature but deleted/unknown/sensitive/file gone). `public_opt_in` files need no signature. Headers: registry `Content-Type`, `Cache-Control: private, max-age=<remaining>`, `nosniff`, `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: cross-origin` |
| `/club-announcements` | POST | session + `clubGuard` (`announcements:write` or `announcements:write_department`) | Creates a **draft**. Body `{ title (≤200), body (≤10000, plain text, no control/bidi characters), audience: { all? \| departmentIds? roleTypes? categoryKeys? memberIds? includeGuardians? }, channels? (default `["in_app"]`, `in_app` is always added), urgent?, pinned?, publishAt?, expiresAt? }` → `201 { data: announcement }`. The whole router answers `403` to everybody without one of the two permissions **before** validation. `announcements:write_department` may only address `departmentIds` that are subsets of the caller's own departments (no `all`/roles/categories/members, never `urgent`): `403` otherwise. `urgent` and club-wide sends need `announcements:write` |
| `/club-announcements` | GET | same | Query `status`, `page`, `pageSize` (default 25, max 100) → `{ data: [announcement], pagination }`, newest first. `announcements:write` sees the club, the department variant only the **own** announcements |
| `/club-announcements/preview` | POST | same | Body `{ audience, channels? }` → `{ data: { total, reachable, email, push, inApp, emailOnly, unreachable, sharedAddress } }` from the [audience resolver](#audience-resolver-wave-9-b5): **counts only, no person list** (`Cache-Control: private, no-store`, 30/min per user). Same scope rule as create; `422` above `AUDIENCE_MAX_RECIPIENTS` |
| `/club-announcements/:id` | GET | same | One announcement (`announcement = { id, title, body, audience, channels, urgent, pinned, publishAt, expiresAt, status (draft\|scheduled\|sending\|sent\|cancelled), sentAt, authorMemberId, authorName, withdrawn, createdAt, updatedAt }`). `404` for another club's, not-own (department variant) and unknown/malformed ids |
| `/club-announcements/:id` | PATCH | same | `draft`/`scheduled`: any of `title, body, audience, channels, urgent, pinned, publishAt (future; `null` only while draft), expiresAt` (scope re-checked against the merged result; a new `publishAt` of a scheduled one enqueues a new publish job, the old one becomes a no-op). Changing `audience`, `channels` or `urgent` of a `scheduled` announcement resets it to `draft` (it was confirmed for the old values; a new `/send` with limits and `confirmRecipients` is needed). `sent`: only `{ pinned }` and `{ withdraw: true }` (sets `expires_at = now`, unpins; it leaves every inbox, still `queued` email/push deliveries become `skipped` / `withdrawn`, the deliveries stay as proof; audit `club_announcement.withdraw`); anything else / `sending`/`cancelled` → `409` |
| `/club-announcements/:id` | DELETE | same | `204` for draft/scheduled/cancelled; `409` for `sending`/`sent` (withdraw instead; deliveries are the proof of delivery). Audit `club_announcement.delete` |
| `/club-announcements/:id/send` | POST | same | Body `{ publishAt?, confirmRecipients? }` (`confirmRecipients` = the recipient count shown in the preview, required above `ANNOUNCEMENT_CONFIRM_THRESHOLD`, else `422 RECIPIENT_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED`; anti-spam limits answer `429` with `details.rule`, see [Rate limits](#rate-limits-and-anti-spam-wave-9-b14); omitted: the stored `publishAt` if it lies in the future, else now; `null`: now; a past value → `422`). **Now:** `sendAnnouncement` claims the row (`FOR UPDATE`, `draft\|scheduled` → `sending`), fans out through `notify()` (one delivery per recipient and channel, `class = optional`, category `announcements`; opt-outs respected) and marks it `sent` → `200 { data: { status, sentAt, delivery: { recipients, inApp, queuedEmail, skippedOptout, skippedNoAddress, alreadyDelivered } } }`; audit `club_announcement.send` (counts and ids only, no text, no addresses). **Later:** status `scheduled` + job `announcement.publish` (dedupe `announcement.publish:<id>:<ms>`) → `200 { data: { status: "scheduled", publishAt, delivery: null } }`; the job re-checks that the announcement is still scheduled for exactly that time **and** that the author still holds the scope (otherwise it is `cancelled`, audit `club_announcement.send_denied`). `409` if not draft/scheduled, `422` for an audience above the limit (status unchanged). A crashed `sending` is retryable after 5 minutes; deliveries are idempotent. 20/min per user. `push` in `channels` is delivered by [`notify.push`](#push-notifications-wave-9-b8) |
| `/club-announcements/:id/cancel` | POST | same | `scheduled` → `cancelled` (`200 { data: announcement }`), else `409`. Audit `club_announcement.cancel` |
| `/club-announcements/:id/deliveries` | GET | same | `{ data: { summary: { recipients, read, byChannel: { <channel>: { total, read, statuses: { <status>: n } } } } } }` for every author. With `announcements:write` additionally `recipients: [{ id, clubMemberId, name, channel, status, errorCode, attempts, sentAt, readAt }]` (never addresses) + `pagination` (query `page`, `pageSize` ≤ 200, `status`); reading the list is audited (`club_announcement.deliveries_read`) because read state is behavioural data |
| `/club-announcements/:id/resend-failed` | POST | same | Sent, not withdrawn/expired announcements only (`409` otherwise): `failed` e-mail deliveries with a retained address go back to `queued` (recipients who opted out since become `skipped_optout`) and get a new `notify.email` job → `{ data: { requeued } }`. Audit `club_announcement.resend_failed`. 20/min per user |
| `/club-inbox` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | The caller's own in-app messages of this club (announcements **and** system messages: `kind`), pinned first then newest. Query `unread=true`, `page`, `pageSize` (default 20, max 100) → `{ data: [{ id (= delivery id), kind: announcement\|system, announcementId, title, body, urgent, category, ctaLabel, ctaUrl, pinned, authorName, sentAt, readAt }], pagination }`. Withdrawn/expired/unsent announcements are hidden. `Cache-Control: private, no-store` |
| `/club-inbox/unread-count` | GET | session + `clubGuard` | `{ data: { count } }` |
| `/club-inbox/:id/read` | POST | session + `clubGuard` | Marks the own delivery read (idempotent) → `{ data: { id, readAt } }`. Another user's, another club's, unknown and malformed ids are the same `404` |
| `/notification-preferences` | GET | session | Own preferences. Without query: the **global defaults**; with `?clubId=<club>`: the **effective switches for that club** (club-specific overrides win over the global default) plus `overrides: [{ category, channel, enabled }]`, the switches that have a club-specific row of the account **or of its linked account-less member record(s) in that club** (several rows for one switch: `enabled` is their AND, like the effective value); `404` if the account does not belong to the club. Shape: `{ data: { clubId (null for the global view), categories: [{ category: announcements\|events\|reminders (+ board for accounts with a members:write role), channels: [{ channel: in_app\|email\|push, enabled, switchable }] }], mandatory: [{ key: account_security\|own_decisions\|invitation_deadlines, switchable: false }] } }`. `in_app` is always on (`switchable: false`); no stored row = enabled. `Cache-Control: private, no-store` |
| `/notification-preferences` | PUT | session | Body `{ clubId?, preferences: [{ category, channel: email\|push, enabled }] }` (1–20 entries, atomic) → same shape as GET. **`clubId`** (body, deliberately not the query: generic clients add `?clubId=` to club calls) writes the club-specific override for that club (`404` if the account is not a member); omitted/`null` writes the global default. `enabled: null` (club-specific only, `422 reset_needs_club` otherwise) removes the override again (the account's row and the linked member records' club-specific rows). A global write never deletes club overrides (they stay visible in `overrides`). `422` for a mandatory class (`details.code: mandatory_not_switchable`), the `in_app` channel (`channel_not_switchable`) or unknown values; nothing is applied then. Also overrides rows stored for the caller's account-less period (`club_member_id`): a global write rewrites the global rows of all own member records; a club write rewrites the club-specific AND the global row of the member record **in that club** (that row exists for this record only and would otherwise veto the explicit choice through the AND rule), never the account's own global row and never member records of other clubs |
| `/notification-preferences/members` | GET | session + `clubGuard` (`announcements:write`) | Leadership view, **yes/no only**, resolved for **this club** (club-specific > global): `{ data: [{ clubMemberId, displayName, unsubscribed }], pagination }` where `unsubscribed` = announcement e-mails switched off (no categories, no channels, no other category). Query `unsubscribed=true\|false`, `page`, `pageSize` (default 50, max 200); left/dormant members are not listed |
| `/push-tokens` | POST | session | Body `{ token (Expo push token), platform: ios\|android, appVersion? }` → `200 { data: { registered: true } }`. Binds the token to the caller's account (idempotent, refreshes `last_seen_at`, re-enables a disabled token); a token of another account is re-assigned silently. `429` above `PUSH_TOKENS_MAX_PER_USER` devices. `Cache-Control: private, no-store` |
| `/push-tokens/:token` | DELETE | session | Removes the caller's own token (device logout) → `204`; unknown or foreign tokens are an identical `204` |
| `/unsubscribe/:token` | GET | public (signed token) | Confirmation payload `{ data: { category, channel: "email", clubName, scope: "club"\|"all", unsubscribed } }` (`unsubscribed` = the effective state for the token's scope) (changes nothing). With `Accept: text/html` a small German confirmation page with a button instead. Invalid, tampered, expired and malformed tokens are one identical `404` (no oracle which case it was); a validly signed token of a deleted person answers like a real one |
| `/unsubscribe/:token` | POST | public (signed token) | RFC 8058 one-click target of `List-Unsubscribe-Post: List-Unsubscribe=One-Click` (body ignored) and the page's button: switches the **e-mail** channel of the token's category off for the account (`u`) or, for a member without account, the `club_members` row (`m`), **for the token's club only** (old tokens: globally, see above) → `200 { data: { category, channel, scope, unsubscribed: true } }`. Idempotent and reusable until expiry (a reuse re-applies the opt-out). 30 requests/min per client IP (`429`), `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` |

<a id="club-registration"></a>
### Club registration (Wave 6)

End users create clubs themselves, but a club only exists after a platform admin (Better Auth role `admin`) has reviewed it. Flow: applicant creates a draft, uploads proof documents, submits (`pending`); an admin requests more info (`needs_info`, applicant edits and resubmits), rejects (`rejected`, applicant may file a new registration) or approves (`approved`). The routes are session-only (no `clubGuard` — the applicant has no club yet) and need a verified e-mail address (403 otherwise). A registration is private: someone else's id is always 404. Everything below returns `{ data: ... }`.

`registration` shape: `{ id, clubName, legalForm ('e_v'|'nicht_eingetragen'|'sonstige'), registerCourt|null, registerNumber|null, street, postalCode, city, websiteUrl|null, claimedRole ('vorsitz'|'stellv_vorsitz'), status ('draft'|'pending'|'needs_info'|'approved'|'rejected'), reviewNote|null, slugSuggestion|null, clubId|null, clubSlug|null (approved only), submittedAt|null, createdAt, documents: [{ id, kind ('registerauszug'|'satzung'|'freistellungsbescheid'|'gruendungsprotokoll'|'sonstiges'), filename, mimeType, sizeBytes }] }`.

| Route | Method | Auth | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/club-registrations` | POST | session, verified e-mail | Create a draft. Body `{ clubName, legalForm, registerCourt?, registerNumber?, street, postalCode, city, websiteUrl?, claimedRole? = 'vorsitz' }` (`e_v` requires court + number; `websiteUrl` must be http(s)). `201 { data: { registration } }`. 422 on invalid input, 409 if the user already has an open (`draft`/`pending`/`needs_info`) registration. Rate limit 10 per hour per client IP. Audit `club_registration.create` |
| `/club-registrations/mine` | GET | session | Own registrations, newest first: `{ data: [registration] }` |
| `/club-registrations/:id` | GET | session | `{ data: { registration } }`; 404 for someone else's/unknown/malformed id |
| `/club-registrations/:id` | PATCH | session | Partial update of the draft fields (merged result is re-validated), only in `draft`/`needs_info` (409 otherwise). `200 { data: { registration } }` |
| `/club-registrations/:id/documents` | POST | session | `multipart/form-data` with `file` + `kind`. Only in `draft`/`needs_info` (409). Allowlist PDF/JPEG/PNG (declared type must match the file's magic bytes), max 10 MB, max 5 files per registration, else 422. Upload and delete share a per-user limit of 30 per hour. Filename is sanitized (path separators stripped), stored under the private `registrations/<id>/` path. `201 { data: { document } }`. Audit `club_registration.document_add` |
| `/club-registrations/:id/documents/:docId` | GET | session (applicant or admin) | Streams the file as an attachment (`Content-Type` from the allowlist, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, RFC 5987 `filename*`). Admins may only read submitted (non-draft) registrations; every admin access is audit-logged (`club_registration.document_access`, `accessedBy`). 404 for everyone else |
| `/club-registrations/:id/documents/:docId` | DELETE | session | Remove a document (only `draft`/`needs_info`, checked under the same row lock as submit/upload). `204`. Audit `club_registration.document_remove` |
| `/club-registrations/:id/submit` | POST | session | `draft`/`needs_info` → `pending`. Needs at least one qualifying proof (`e_v`/`sonstige`: Registerauszug, Satzung, Freistellungsbescheid or Gründungsprotokoll; `nicht_eingetragen`: Satzung or Gründungsprotokoll; `sonstiges` alone never counts) else 422. 409 if an already approved club has the same register court + number (compared case/whitespace-insensitively; the message leaks no club data). All checks run in one transaction after locking the registration row (so a parallel document delete cannot produce a `pending` registration without proof). Limited to 5 submits per user per day (429); the reviewer mail is sent at most once per hour per registration. Writes `slug_suggestion`, clears `review_note`, sends the receipt mail to the applicant and the review notification (see `CLUB_REVIEW_NOTIFY_EMAIL`). `200 { data: { registration } }`. Audit `club_registration.submit` |
| `/admin/club-registrations` | GET | session + `adminGuard` | Review queue, oldest submission first, max 200. `?status=pending\|needs_info\|approved\|rejected` (omitted: all except drafts; `draft`/unknown → 422). `{ data: [{ ...registration, applicant: { id, name, email }, duplicateHints: [{ clubId, name, city, postalCode }] }] }`. `duplicateHints` (max 10) lists existing clubs approved through a registration with the same Registergericht + number (listed first), with the same name/generated slug, or with the same postal code + city; `city`/`postalCode` are `null` for clubs not created via a registration |
| `/admin/club-registrations/:id` | GET | session + `adminGuard` | Same shape, one object in `data`. 404 for drafts/unknown ids |
| `/admin/club-registrations/:id/documents/:docId` | GET | session + `adminGuard` | Authenticated file download (404 for drafts; every access is audit-logged as `club_registration.document_access`) |
| `/admin/club-registrations/:id/request-info` | POST | session + `adminGuard` | Body `{ note }` (required) → `needs_info`, mail to the applicant. `200 { data: { registration } }`. Audit `club_registration.request_info` |
| `/admin/club-registrations/:id/reject` | POST | session + `adminGuard` | Body `{ note }` (required reason) → `rejected`, mail to the applicant. `200 { data: { registration } }`. Audit `club_registration.reject` |
| `/admin/club-registrations/:id/approve` | POST | session + `adminGuard` | Body `{ slug? }`. Creates the club, see [Approval](#club-registration-approval). `200 { data: { registration, club: { id, name, slug } } }` |

All three decisions only work from `pending`; any other status (or a lost race between two admins) is `409`. A non-admin session on `/admin/club-registrations/*` is rejected by `adminGuard` with `401`, same as every other `/admin` route.

<a id="club-registration-approval"></a>**Approval.** `approve` takes a row lock on the registration (`SELECT … FOR UPDATE`), so parallel approvals serialize and the loser answers 409 without creating a second club. Inside that lock it first re-checks the register key (court + number, same normalization as the submit guard) against already approved registrations (409 — two pending registrations can share a key), then calls `auth.api.createOrganization({ name, slug, userId })` (server-side, no session — makes the applicant `owner`; the organization's `metadata` carries `{ registrationId }`; if the call fails after inserting the organization but before its owner member, that owner-less organization is recognised by this tag and removed before the next slug is tried), then in the same transaction marks the registration `approved` (`club_id`, `reviewed_by`, `reviewed_at`), makes sure the founder has a `club_members` row (category `aktiv`, automatic member number `<ABBR>-0001`, see [Member numbers](#member-numbers); the five default categories are seeded) and inserts a `club_roles` row for `claimed_role`, creates the club's standard calendar (`ensureDefaultCalendar`), and writes audit `club_registration.approve`. `claimed_role` must grant `members:write` and `roles:write` (`vorsitz`, `stellv_vorsitz` do; checked against `club-permissions.ts`; `schriftfuehrer` lacks `roles:write` and was therefore removed from `CLAIMABLE_ROLES` in Wave 7 B16 — decision: do not widen its rights, and `POST /club-registrations` answers `422` for it), so the founder can run the club right away (e.g. `GET /club-applications`). If anything fails after the organization was created, the organization is deleted again and a failed cleanup is logged (`logger.error`), never swallowed; the registration stays `pending` and can be retried.

<a id="club-slug-rules"></a>**Slug rules** (`src/lib/club-slug.ts`). The slug is the public join key of a club (`clubSlug` in `POST /club-members/apply`) and is generated, not chosen by the applicant: lowercase, umlauts/ß transliterated (`ä→ae`, `ö→oe`, `ü→ue`, `ß→ss`, other accents dropped), legal-form additions (`e.V.`, `eV`, `eingetragener Verein`) removed, everything outside `a-z0-9` collapsed to single `-`, 3–50 characters (too short names get `-verein` appended, names without usable characters become `verein`). (`æ→ae`, `œ→oe`, `ø→o`, `ł→l`, `đ→d`, `þ→th` as well). Reserved slugs (`admin`, `api`, `app`, `www`, `demo`, `vereine`, `join`, `dashboard`, `docs`, `impressum`, `datenschutz`, `me`, …, see `RESERVED_SLUGS`; `verein` is deliberately allowed, it is the fallback) get `-verein` appended. The suggestion is stored at submit but only *assigned* on approval (open registrations reserve nothing, so no squatting); on collision `-2`, `-3`, … is appended, and a collision that happens between lookup and insert is retried (`organization_slug_uidx` is the final arbiter). An admin may override the slug in `approve` (`slug`, validated: `^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$`, 3–50, not reserved, 422; already taken → 409, no automatic suffix). **The slug is immutable once assigned**: `POST /api/auth/organization/update` with a different `slug` is rejected (403, `beforeUpdateOrganization` hook in `src/auth/auth.ts`).

The scaffold's `/accounts` and `/examples` routes (ownership-scoped example resource / connectivity-check) were removed in Wave 1 (see [Key decisions](#key-decisions)) — `src/routes/club-members.ts` is now the reference implementation for "a real, club-scoped resource" that a new route should copy the pattern from, in place of the old `accounts.ts`.

`/internal/stats` exists for a different kind of caller than everything else in this table: not an end user's browser/app, but another backend — a central fleet/portal tool aggregating KPIs across many deployed instances of this template (see `project-portal-backend` in the company's `project-portal` repo for a real consumer). That's why it's token-gated instead of session-gated: such a caller has no user account on this instance at all.

## Rights and read scoping (Wave 7 B7/B8)

**Deny-by-default.** Every account member (`member.role` `owner`/`member`; **not** external `guest`s such as guardians) implicitly holds the base role **`mitglied`**: `clubGuard` (`src/middleware/club-guard.ts`) puts it first in `clubRoleTypes`; it is never stored in `club_roles`, cannot be assigned/revoked (see `/club-members/:id/roles`) and `ROLE_PERMISSIONS.mitglied` is explicitly `[]`. Writes need a role that grants them explicitly; the rights matrix test below enforces it.

| Permission | vorsitz | stellv_vorsitz | schriftfuehrer | kassenwart | beisitzer | abteilungsleitung | trainer / erziehungsberechtigt / mitglied |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `members:write` | x | x | x | | | | |
| `members:read_sensitive` | x | x | | x | | | |
| `roles:write` | x | x | | | | | |
| `departments:write` | x | x | | | | x | |
| `club_info:write` | x | x | x | | | | |
| `calendars:write` | x | x | x | | | x | |
| `meetings:write` | x | x | x | | | | |
| `meetings:read_board` (new) | x | x | x | | x | | |
| `locations:write` | x | x | x | | | x | |
| `locations:read_access` (new) | x | x | x | | | x | |
| `inventory:write` | x | x | x | | | x | |
| `club_stats:read` (now a regular permission) | x | x | x | x | x | | |
| `forms:write` (Wave 8 B4: form builder `/forms`, reads included; B7: custom field definitions `/member-fields`, writes) | x | x | x | | | | |
| `announcements:write` (Wave 9 B7: club-wide announcements, urgent flag, per-recipient delivery list) | x | x | x | | | | |
| `announcements:write_department` (Wave 9 B7: only the **own** departments, scope = `club_roles.department_id` of the caller's `abteilungsleitung`/`trainer` roles) | | | | | | x | trainer: x |
| `privacy:read` (Wave 10 B2: audit log incl. member view and CSV, later consent overview, data subject requests, retention runs) | x | x | | | | | |
| `privacy:manage` (Wave 10: consent purposes/texts, paper proofs, privacy notice, retention overrides, export/erasure of a member) | x | x | | | | | |
| `absences:read` (Wave 10: absence reports; trainer/abteilungsleitung only for members of their **own** departments, scope applied in the route) | x | x | | | | x | trainer: x |
| `assembly:manage` (Wave 11: statute parameters, invitations, voting roll, attendance, ballots, minutes of the general assembly; also in `permissions` of `GET /club-members/me`) | x | x | x | | | | |

`erziehungsberechtigt` holds **no** permission at all (deny-by-default): a guardian only reaches ward-bound data through the guardian scope (Wave 10 B4), never through a `ClubPermission`.

**Read scoping** (404, never 403, so existence does not leak):

| Resource | Rule |
|---|---|
| Meetings `type = vorstandssitzung` (list, detail, invitees, overlap, attendance, resolutions) | only invitees (`meeting_invitees`) and callers with `meetings:write` or `meetings:read_board`; the list filters them out |
| Meetings `mitgliederversammlung` / `sonstige` | every member of the club |
| Locations: `accessNote`, key holders, wifi/links with `visibility = vorstand` | only `locations:write` / `locations:read_access` (shape stays stable: `accessNote: null`, `keyHolders: []`, `accessRestricted: true`) |
| Key history (`GET /locations/:id/key-history`, B14) | like key holders: only `locations:write` / `locations:read_access`; plain members and guests `403` (dedicated endpoint, so no `accessRestricted` stub) |
| Member CSV export / import (B10) | export: `members:write` or `members:read_sensitive`, sensitive columns only with `members:read_sensitive` (`403` otherwise); import and template: `members:write`. Plain members and external guests `403` |
| Wifi/links `mitglied` / `gast` | members see `gast` + `mitglied`; `guest`-role callers only `gast`. The legacy flag `visibleToGuests: false` lowers a `gast` row to `mitglied` but never raises a `vorstand` row |
| **External guests** (`member.role = guest`, e.g. guardians) | **no meetings** (`404` on the list and every `/meetings/:id/*` read), **no member list** (`GET /club-members` → `403`), **no inventory and no departments** (`GET /inventory-items[/:id[/loans|/damage-reports]]`, `GET /departments[/:id]` → `403`), **calendars and events only with an explicit visibility grant** (a calendar without any grant is club-wide for members but hidden from guests: `GET /calendars`, `/events`, `/events.ics` leave it out, by id `404`), other members' detail/`departments`/`roles`/`guardians` → `404`; own row, `/club-members/me`, club info, calendars and `gast` wifi/links stay. the guardian routes (`/my-wards`, `/guardian/wards/:wardId/*`) are the intended way for their ward data |
| **Former members and interessenten** in `/club-members` | callers with neither `members:write` nor `members:read_sensitive` only see members whose **effective** status is `probe`/`aktiv`/`ruhend` (list and detail; detail of others → `404`; the own row is always reachable); `status` filters only narrow that set. `/club-stats` is unchanged (counts only) |
| Age transition suggestions (`GET /club-members/transition-suggestions`) | `members:write` **and** `members:read_sensitive` (derived from birth dates); `apply` only `members:write`, it recomputes on locked rows |
| Access after leaving | `clubGuard` answers `404 "Club not found"` once the effective status is `ausgetreten`/`ausgeschlossen`/`verstorben` ([Exit workflow](#exit-workflow)) |
| Custom field values (`/club-members/:id/custom-fields`, B7) | (credential overview `GET /club-members/credentials`: same read rules, see API reference) per definition: `visible_to` + `sensitive` + self (table in [Application acceptance and mapping](#application-acceptance-and-mapping-wave-8-b6)); writes `members:write` (sensitive: + `members:read_sensitive`), self only `editable_by = self`, an active guardian of the child only `editable_by = self` and not `sensitive`; the member row is scoped like the member detail (guests and hidden former members `404`) |
| Application decisions (`/club-applications`, B6) | `members:write`; the submission and its files are reachable for the board only through the application (files: `GET /form-submissions/:id/files/:fileId`, audited) |
| Form submissions (`/form-submissions`, B5) | only the submitter (foreign / malformed ids `404`; deleting is limited to drafts); the file download additionally for `members:write` of the submission's club once it is submitted (audited); the form builder `/forms` needs `forms:write` for every method |
| Inventory | everyone reads; management `inventory:write`; borrowing and damage reports stay self-service, borrowing switchable per club via `club_settings.allow_self_loans` (`403` when off) |

**Migration `0016_roles-scoping`:** **not lossless** — `meetings.type` free text → `vorstandssitzung` (contains `vorstand` or `sitzung`) | `mitgliederversammlung` (contains `versammlung`) | `sonstige`; ambiguous legacy values therefore land in the more restricted type, and the original free-text value is **lost** (the column is overwritten; no backup column). Adds `location_wifi_networks.visibility`/`location_links.visibility` (backfilled from `visible_to_guests`, which is dropped) and `meeting_resolutions.correction_note/corrected_at/corrected_by`.

**Rights matrix test (`src/routes/rights-matrix.test.ts`, B12 part).** It lists every router mounted in `src/index.ts` (a sync check fails if `index.ts` gets a router the test does not know), enumerates all registered `POST/PATCH/PUT/DELETE` routes from the Hono app and calls each with an account holding **only** `mitglied`; the expected answer is **`403` only** (`404` only for routes listed in `MAY_BE_404`; the `/admin/*` routes may answer `401` because `adminGuard` does so for authenticated non-admins). A `401` would mean a dead session and make the matrix vacuous, so the test first verifies that every test account's session is valid (`GET /club-members/me` → 200), and then repeats the whole run with an **external guest** account; guests must also be refused the sensitive reads (meetings, member list). A router registered with `.all()` would escape the per-method enumeration, so the test forbids `.all()` handlers. Self-service routes (own profile/availability/RSVP/notifications, join request, founding application, loans, damage reports, media upload) are in the explicit `SELF_SERVICE` allowlist with a reason (new: accepting an own invitation, the own cancellation `POST /club-exit-requests`, withdrawing an own request, `PATCH /club-members/me/custom-fields`). A dedicated Wave 8 test keeps application decisions, custom field definitions and foreign custom field values behind their rights. The profile module activation/deactivation routes (`POST /member-fields/catalog/:moduleId/activate|deactivate`) are covered by the generic loop (`forms:write` is checked before anything else). Body validation runs before most permission checks, so routes with required bodies need an entry in `SAMPLE_BODIES` (the test says so on a `422`). **A new writing route without a permission check, allowlist entry or sample body fails the test.**

## Adding a new API route

A checklist for a new dev adding an endpoint. `src/routes/club-members.ts` is the reference implementation for a club-scoped resource — it uses every piece described below in one file. Keep it open while you read this.

**1. Pick or create a route file.** One file per resource under `src/routes/` — a new resource gets a new file (`events.ts`, `inventory-items.ts`, ...), a new action on an existing resource is a new handler in that file. A club-scoped resource exports its own `Hono<ClubEnv>()` and needs `sessionGuard` **and** `clubGuard`, in that order (`ClubEnv` extends `SessionEnv` — see `src/middleware/club-guard.ts`); a resource with no club concept (rare) uses plain `Hono<SessionEnv>()` with just `sessionGuard`, like `src/routes/notifications.ts`.

```ts
export const widgetRoutes = new Hono<ClubEnv>();
widgetRoutes.use("*", rateLimit({ windowMs: 60_000, max: 60 }));
widgetRoutes.use("*", sessionGuard);
widgetRoutes.use("*", clubGuard);
```

**2. Validate the input.** Define a Zod schema and wire it with `@hono/zod-validator`'s `zValidator`, throwing `ValidationError` — not a bare `c.json(..., 400)` — on failure, so bad input goes through the same error path as everything else:

```ts
const createWidgetSchema = z.object({ name: z.string().max(100) }).strict();

widgetRoutes.post(
  "/",
  zValidator("json", createWidgetSchema, (result) => {
    if (!result.success) throw new ValidationError("Invalid request body", { details: result.error.issues });
  }),
  async (c) => {
    const body = c.req.valid("json");
    // ...
  },
);
```

`.strict()` isn't decoration — it's what stops an extra body field from silently reaching a query.

**3. Guard it.** `sessionGuard` (`src/middleware/session-guard.ts`) for anything that needs a logged-in user, `clubGuard` (`src/middleware/club-guard.ts`) additionally for anything club-scoped, `adminGuard` for admin-only, nothing at all for a genuinely public read. If a handler touches one specific row, filter by *both* the row's id and `clubId`/the caller's id in the same query, and respond with `NotFoundError` — never `ForbiddenError` — when it belongs to a different club or a different person. A 403 confirms the row exists; a 404 doesn't. `club-members.ts`'s `GET /:id` is the example. For a permission check *within* a club a caller does belong to (board-only write, etc.), use `hasClubPermission(c.get("clubRoleTypes"), "...")` from `src/lib/club-permissions.ts` and throw `ForbiddenError` on failure — that one legitimately is a 403, since the caller already knows the club/row exists. **Every writing route must also be covered by the rights matrix test** (`src/routes/rights-matrix.test.ts`, see [Rights and read scoping](#rights-and-read-scoping-wave-7-b7b8)): either check a permission that a plain `mitglied` lacks, or add the route to its `SELF_SERVICE` allowlist with a reason (and a `SAMPLE_BODIES` entry if the body is validated before the permission check).

**4. Throw errors, don't format them.** Every class in `src/lib/errors.ts` (`NotFoundError`, `ConflictError`, `TooManyRequestsError`, ...) maps to its HTTP status in exactly one place: `app.onError` in `src/index.ts`. Throw the typed error from inside the route and stop thinking about status codes there.

**5. Talk to the DB through Drizzle.** Import `db` from `src/db/client.ts` and the table from `src/db/schema/*.ts`. A new column or table means editing/adding a schema file there, then:

```
npm run db:generate   # writes a migration from the schema diff
npm run db:migrate    # applies it locally
```

Commit the generated migration file — it's what actually runs on deploy, the schema file alone changes nothing in a running database.

**6. Mount the router.** One line in `src/index.ts`, alongside the existing `app.route("/club-members", clubMemberRoutes)`:

```ts
app.route("/widgets", widgetRoutes);
```

Ordering only matters *within* a single file: a static path (`/search`) has to be registered before a dynamic one (`/:id`) that would otherwise swallow it as a parameter value.

**7. Test against a real session and a real second club, not a mock.** Add `widgets.test.ts` next to the route file. Mount just that router plus `app.onError` in a throwaway `Hono` instance, create real (verified) users and at least two clubs through `auth.api.createUser`/`signInEmail`/`createOrganization`, and reuse the session cookies — `club-members.test.ts`'s `signUpAndVerify` helper is the one to copy. Always include a cross-club case (a member of club A requesting club B's data gets 404) — that's the one class of bug a mocked session/club would never catch. This needs a real `DATABASE_URL` (`docker compose up -d db` gives you one).

That's the whole loop — schema → route file → validate → guard → mount → test. None of it is a framework decision; it's copying `club-members.ts` and changing the domain-specific middle.

<a id="club-members-api"></a>
### Club members API contract (Wave 7 B3)

`GET /club-members`, `GET|PATCH /club-members/:id`, `POST /club-members` and `/club-members/me` all return this **member shape** (`:id` / `id` = `club_members.id`):

```jsonc
{
  "id": "uuid",                // club_members.id -- the canonical member id
  "memberId": "string|null",   // Better Auth member.id (null = no account)
  "userId": "string|null",
  "hasAccount": true,
  "name": "Display Name",      // club_members.display_name
  "status": "aktiv",           // stored: interessent|probe|aktiv|ruhend|ausgetreten|ausgeschlossen|verstorben
  "effectiveStatus": "aktiv",  // derived at read time: left_at <= today turns a live status into "ausgetreten" -- show/filter THIS
  "category": "jugend",        // category KEY (string, unchanged from before Wave 7) or null
  "categoryId": "uuid|null",
  "categoryName": "Jugend",
  "categoryObject": { "id": "uuid", "key": "jugend", "name": "Jugend", "votingRight": false },
  "joinedAt": "2024-01-31", "leftAt": null,
  "orgRole": "owner|member|null",      // Better Auth org role, null without account
  "roles": [{ "id", "roleType", "departmentId", "termEndsAt" }],   // [] without account
  "departments": [{ "id", "name", "since", "until" }],             // until != null = ended
  "photoUrl": "https://.../media/<key>?exp=&sig=|null",   // signed, short-lived; only for the own row, members:write, members:read_sensitive and linked guardians -- null for other plain members
  // only for the own row or with members:read_sensitive:
  "email": "account e-mail|null", "contactEmail": "...", "memberNumber": "DEMO-0001",
  "firstName": "Ada|null", "lastName": "Lovelace|null",   // `name` above is the display name
  "birthDate": "2012-04-05", "street": "", "postalCode": "", "city": "", "phone": "",
  "emergencyContactName": "", "emergencyContactPhone": "",
  "suggestedCategory": { "id": "uuid", "key": "aktiv", "name": "Aktiv" }   // or null: age transition proposal (B5)
}
```

**Names and privacy.** Reserved or misleading display names ("Vorstand", "Admin", ...) are deliberately NOT checked: the board can rename a member (`PATCH /club-members/:id`), and `displayName` is not synced with Better Auth's `user.name`. A person edits their own `firstName`/`lastName`/`displayName` via `PATCH /club-members/me`; `name` (the display name) is what other members see, the split names are part of the sensitive block (own row or `members:read_sensitive`). Records that must show the name *at the time* keep their own snapshot and are never rewritten by a rename: `location_key_history.member_name` (issue/return history of keys) stays as it was, while the live key-holder list, loans and RSVP lists show the current display name. The audit entry names changed fields only, never the names themselves.

E-mail semantics: `email` is the **account** e-mail (null without account), `contactEmail` is the **club contact** e-mail. After the backfill both start equal; they diverge as soon as either is edited (the contact e-mail is not synced with later account changes). Both are sensitive fields.

Backward-compatibility decisions: `category` was a string key before Wave 7 and **stays one** (the object form is the additive `categoryObject`/`categoryId`/`categoryName`); `orgRole`, `roles`, `userId`, `joinedAt`, `leftAt` keep their meaning; `PATCH` responses switch from the old `club_memberships` row to the full member shape (it contains the former fields `memberNumber`, `birthDate`, `emergencyContact*`, `joinedAt`, `leftAt`, `category`; `memberId` stays the account id). `id` of a member changed from `member.id` to `club_members.id` — old calls with a `member.id` in the URL keep working during the transition (non-uuid ids resolve as `member.id`); clients should switch to `id`. `name` is the club-managed display name (set from the account name at join time; it is not kept in sync with later account renames).

`GET /club-members` wraps the shape: `{ "data": [member shape], "page": 1, "pageSize": 50, "total": 123 }` (see the route table for the query parameters). **Breaking for old clients:** the list used to contain every member; it is now paged (default 50), so a client that ignores `total`/`page` silently shows only the first 50. `POST /club-members/:id/transition` returns `{ data: <member shape>, transition: {...} }`. `GET /club-members/categories` returns `{ data: [category] }`, `GET /club-stats` `{ data: stats }`; both shapes are listed below.

<a id="member-lifecycle"></a>**Member lifecycle (B5).** Statuses: `interessent`, `probe`, `aktiv`, `ruhend` (live) and `ausgetreten`, `ausgeschlossen`, `verstorben` (left). Allowed changes (source = the *effective* status, `STATUS_TRANSITIONS` in `src/lib/member-lifecycle.ts`):

| from | to |
|---|---|
| `interessent` | `probe`, `aktiv`, `ausgetreten` (withdrawn), `verstorben` |
| `probe` | `aktiv`, `ausgetreten`, `ausgeschlossen`, `verstorben` |
| `aktiv` | `ruhend`, `ausgetreten`, `ausgeschlossen`, `verstorben` |
| `ruhend` | `aktiv`, `ausgetreten`, `ausgeschlossen`, `verstorben` |
| `ausgetreten`, `ausgeschlossen`, `verstorben` | `aktiv` only, with `reopen: true` **and** a `reason` (else `422`) |

- Every change runs in one transaction under a row lock and writes `club_member_status_history` (from/to status, from/to category, `effectiveOn`, `reason`, `changedBy` = caller's `member.id`). A category-only change writes `fromStatus = toStatus`. `PATCH /club-members/:id` with a category change and the age-transition apply also write history.
- **Exit and key date (Stichtag):** a leaving status sets `left_at = effectiveOn` (default today; not before `joined_at`) and `exit_reason`. `effectiveOn` in the **future** is only allowed for `ausgetreten` (a cancellation to a key date): the stored status stays, `left_at` is set, the history entry carries the future date, and `effectiveStatus` becomes `ausgetreten` by derivation on that day — no cron job. Sending the stored status again while such an exit is pending takes it back (`kind: "cancel_exit"`). `ausgeschlossen`/`verstorben` and all non-leaving statuses cannot be dated in the future (`422`). `PATCH /club-members/:id` no longer accepts `leftAt` (`422`, "use POST /:id/transition"). **"Today" is the calendar day in Europe/Berlin** (`todayBerlin()` in `src/lib/member-lifecycle.ts`): an exit dated 1 July is effective from 00:00 CEST, not from 02:00; the status derivation, the age calculation, the key-date checks and `clubGuard` all use it.
- **No automatic revival:** `ausgeschlossen` and `verstorben` are not reopened by a re-join. A person with such a (detached) `club_members` row cannot be added again by application approval (`409`, `details.code = "member_rejoin_blocked"`), `addMember` or an accepted invitation (rejected by the Better Auth hooks `beforeAddMember`/`beforeAcceptInvitation`) until the board reopens the member via `transition` (`reopen: true`, reason). `ausgetreten` re-joins keep reusing the old row as before.
- **Normalisation and locking.** `autoTransitionToKey` is trimmed and lower-cased everywhere (create, PATCH; `" Aktiv "` names `aktiv`), so suggestions, archive clean-up and cycle detection agree. Multi-row writes lock in one fixed order — all categories of the club (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, id order; plain reads of a referenced category use `FOR SHARE`) → member rows (id order) → organization row (member numbers) — and the category lookup, the cycle check and the `from` side of the history all happen **inside** the transaction after the lock, so parallel archive / move / PATCH requests serialize instead of deadlocking or writing a stale `from` category.
- **Age transition:** a category with `autoTransitionToKey` + `maxAge` marks members older than `maxAge` (whole years from `birth_date`) as `suggestedCategory`; nothing changes until `POST /club-members/transition-suggestions/apply`.

<a id="club-kpis"></a>**Club KPIs (`GET /club-stats`, B11)** — permission `club_stats:read` (a real `ClubPermission` in `src/lib/club-permissions.ts`: `vorsitz`, `stellv_vorsitz`, `schriftfuehrer`, `kassenwart`, `beisitzer`; listed in `/club-members/me` → `permissions` for UI gating). Counts only, no personal data:

```jsonc
{ "data": {
  "members": { "total": 9, "active": 7, "withAccount": 5, "withoutAccount": 4 },
  "byStatus": { "interessent": 1, "probe": 1, "aktiv": 7, "ruhend": 1, "ausgetreten": 1, "ausgeschlossen": 0, "verstorben": 0 },
  "byCategory": [{ "key": "aktiv", "name": "Aktiv", "count": 7 }],
  "byDepartment": [{ "id": "uuid", "name": "Fußball", "count": 2 }],
  "ageBuckets": [{ "label": "0–5", "count": 0 }, { "label": "6–12", "count": 1 } /* 13–17, 18–29, 30–49, 50–64, 65+ */],
  "ageUnknown": 6,
  "movement": [{ "month": "2026-10", "joined": 6, "left": 1 }]   // last 12 months incl. the current one, oldest first
} }
```

Rules: a member counts independent of an account (ADR-003). `members.total` = effective status `probe`, `aktiv` or `ruhend` (`interessent` and everyone who left are not counted); `active` = `aktiv`; `withAccount` + `withoutAccount` = `total`. `byStatus` covers the whole club history with the effective status (a stored `aktiv` with `left_at <= today` counts as `ausgetreten`). `byCategory`/`byDepartment` (current assignments)/`ageBuckets` describe the counted population; archived categories only appear while they still have members; `ageUnknown` = counted members without `birth_date`. `movement.joined` = `joined_at` in the month (not `interessent`), `movement.left` = `left_at` of members who have left plus exits from the status history of members who were reopened later. **Accepted limitation (no k-anonymity):** the KPIs are exact counts with no minimum group size, so in a tiny category or age bucket a count of 1 identifies a person. This is deliberate — the readers are the board, who know their members anyway, and plain members have no access (`403`); it must be revisited before the numbers are shown to anyone else (e.g. a member-facing dashboard).

Member category shape: `{ id, key, name, votingRight, minAge, maxAge, autoTransitionToKey, sortOrder, archived, archivedAt, memberCount? }`.

<a id="exit-workflow"></a>**Exit workflow (B6).** Routes: `/club-settings`, `/club-exit-requests` (see the API table). Everything that changes the member goes through `transitionMember` — the same history, the same Stichtag derivation, the same matrix as `POST /club-members/:id/transition`.

- **Earliest exit date** (`earliestExitDate` in `src/lib/exit-notice.ts`, pure, Europe/Berlin): `requestedOn + noticePeriodDays`, then rounded **up** to the end of the month / quarter / year for `noticeMode` `month_end` / `quarter_end` / `year_end` (a date that already is such an end stays); `immediate` or no mode: no rounding; no settings: today. Examples with 30 days / `month_end`: request on 11 Oct → 30 Nov; request on 1 Nov → 31 Dec. `GET /club-exit-requests/preview?requestedOn=` returns it so clients can show the notice hint before submitting.
- **Cancellation by the member** (`POST /club-exit-requests`, own account): request `pending`, nothing changes yet. **Decision: a `desiredEffectiveOn` before the earliest date is raised to the earliest date, not refused** (`422` would make the member guess dates); the response says so (`adjustedToEarliest`) and shows `earliestEffectiveOn` / `proposedEffectiveOn = max(desired, earliest)`. A later wish is kept. One open request per member (`409`, enforced by a partial unique index), no second one while an exit is scheduled (`409`).
- **Board decisions.** `approve` takes `effectiveOn` (default = proposal, may be moved to a **later** date only; earlier than the earliest date → `422` with `details.earliestEffectiveOn`, computed from the request date and the *current* settings). The request is claimed with `UPDATE … WHERE status = 'pending'`; claim, `transitionMember` (→ `ausgetreten`) and audit run in one transaction, so a parallel approve/reject has exactly one winner and a failing transition leaves the request `pending`. A future date becomes a **scheduled exit** (`left_at` = date, stored status unchanged, `effectiveStatus` flips on the day — no job); today or earlier takes effect at once (a late approval of an in-time notice may carry a past date, never one before the earliest date). `reject` needs a note.
- **Recorded by the board** (`members:write`, `clubMemberId` in the body — the way to cancel for members without account): the decision is the caller's, so the request is stored `approved` immediately (no second click). Cancellation: `effectiveOn` default = earliest date, earlier → `422`. **Exclusion** (`kind: "exclusion"`): `reason` is mandatory, takes effect immediately (`ausgeschlossen`, `effectiveOn` today or earlier, never in the future), is audited, supersedes a pending cancellation of the same member (that request becomes `rejected`, note "Ersetzt durch Ausschluss"), and cannot be withdrawn — only `transition` with `reopen` brings the person back. The board cannot exclude itself (`422`).
